Every other complex software process (code, infrastructure, data, experiments) is version-controlled; reasoning is not. We introduce GitOfThoughts, which stores an agent's reasoning tree as a git repository: every scored thought is a commit, scores are notes, outcomes are tags, and retrieval is "git log" over the agent's own history. This makes reasoning replayable, auditable, and mergeable across agents at near-zero engineering cost.
  We then ask the harder question: does memory, in any substrate, actually improve accuracy? Across five substrates (none, markdown, vector, graph, git), two benchmarks, two model scales, and pre-registered replications, the answer for novel problems is no. No memory format reliably helps, and a promising early result collapsed under its own pre-registered replication. Memory pays only above what we call the copyability threshold: when the retrieved case is a near-duplicate of the current problem (similarity >~ 0.8), accuracy jumps sharply; below it, nothing. The gain is answer retrieval, not method transfer: a 4.5x larger model doubles the near-duplicate payoff yet still cannot extract a transferable method from a worked example. The only general lever we find is test-time sampling. The case for git-as-substrate is therefore auditability, provenance, and mergeability at accuracy parity. We document a retracted result and a refuted hypothesis to model the evaluation standard we hold ourselves to.

</details>


### [186] [When the Tool Decides: LLM Agents Defer Blindly to Graph Neural Network Tools, and Stronger Backbones Defer More](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14476)
*Zhongyuan Wang,Pratyusha Vemuri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了将GNN作为工具的大模型智能体普遍存在“盲目依从”问题：智能体不仅不会理性判断工具的使用时机，反而随着能力提升更加被动地完全照搬工具输出，且单纯依靠模型规模扩张无法解决该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的研究假设大语言模型智能体具备判断何时及多大程度依赖外部图形神经网络（GNN）工具的能力。作者旨在实验验证这一假设是否成立，即智能体是真的在利用工具，还是仅仅在模仿工具输出。

Method: 在ogbn-arxiv和WikiCS文本属性图数据集上，针对节点分类任务，评估采用ReAct框架的大语言模型智能体（从Qwen2.5 0.5B到7B）调用冻结GNN工具的行为模式，并引入Oracle对比策略与简单选择性调用门控机制进行分析。

Result: 1. 智能体表现为“GNN鹦鹉”，预测结果与原始GNN高度重合（97.6%-99.2%）；2. 这种盲目依从性随着模型参数（能力）的增加而变强；3. 智能体未能根据任务情况合理选择工具（即便存在性能更好的策略，智能体仍倾向于使用性能较差的GNN），导致性能损失；4. 现有的测试时特征不足以实现高效的选择性调用。

Conclusion: 大语言模型智能体并不会自动产生对外部工具（GNN）的判断力。简单地将GNN作为工具提供给智能体往往会导致智能体盲目模仿工具输出（“鹦鹉效应”），且随着模型能力提升，这种盲目依从反而增强。研究表明，需要显式设计选择性调用机制，而非依赖大模型的涌现能力。

Abstract: A growing line of work equips large language model (LLM) agents with graph neural networks (GNNs) as callable tools, assuming the agent exercises judgment over when and how much to rely on such a tool. We test this directly. We expose a frozen GNN to a ReAct-style LLM agent as an explicit tool and measure, on node classification over a text-attributed graph (ogbn-arxiv, replicated on WikiCS), whether the agent uses the tool or merely obeys it. We find the agent does not exercise judgment: its predictions agree with the raw GNN's 97.6-99.2% of the time (5 seeds), collapsing into a GNN parrot that adopts the tool's output wholesale and bypasses its own reasoning. Sweeping backbone capability (Qwen2.5 0.5B-7B), the deference is not a weak-model artifact: among models able to invoke the tool, agreement rises with capability (0.60 to 0.98 from 1.5B to 7B). Crucially, the cost of deference does not shrink as capability grows and grows where alternatives emerge: a per-node oracle over the available actions beats the parrot by 0.09-0.18 at 3B and 0.12-0.22 at 7B, roughly doubling at high homophily, because the parrot is pinned to the frozen GNN while the agent's alternatives improve; at 7B a simple neighbour-label tool overtakes the GNN at high homophily (0.81 vs 0.71) yet the agent still defers. A simple selective-invocation gate recovers about half of that high-homophily gap (0.71 to 0.83) but yields no net global gain, and held-out estimates bound the best achievable gate over standard test-time features to at most a third of the oracle headroom: reliable selective invocation looks limited by available information, not merely router design. Our results are a cautionary measurement: evaluations of agent+tool systems cannot assume the agent adds judgment on top of the tool, and selective invocation must be designed in rather than expected to emerge from scale.

</details>


### [187] [From Chatbot to Digital Colleague: The Paradigm Shift Toward Persistent Autonomous AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14502)
*Yongheng Zhang,Ziang Liu,Jiaxuan Zhu,Shuai Wang,Xiangqi Chen,Haojing Huang,Jiayi Kuang,Siyu Chen,Ao Shen,Hao Wu,Qiufeng Wang,Qian-Wen Zhang,Junnan Dong,Wenhao Jiang,Ying Shen,Hai-Tao Zheng,Yinghui Li,Di Yin,Xing Sun,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 摘要探讨了LLM从对话工具向具备深度推理能力与持久工作区管理的“数字同事”系统的范式转移，阐述了认知架构、任务执行范式及评估模式的全面升级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大语言模型仅作为对话生成器在复杂推理、任务持久性及自主工作能力上的局限性，明确由Chatbot向Digital Colleague进化的技术路径。

Method: 提出并构建了从“聊天机器人”向“数字同事”转型的分析框架，沿着“认知核心（从快思考转向慢思考）”与“工具增强（从临时调用转向持久工作区）”两个维度进行系统性论述。

Result: 定义了“Thinking LLMs”与“OpenClaw工作站系统”模型；指出了从“指令-响应”数据向“状态-动作-观察（SAO）”轨迹数据的范式转变；强调了评估体系从静态向动态沙箱环境的迁移。

Conclusion: LLMs正在演进为具备认知深度（思考型LLM）与持久任务执行能力（工作站式系统）的“数字同事”，未来AI评估与开发将向沙箱化、审计化与自我演进的生态系统转型。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are undergoing a fundamental transformation from conversational generators into integrated AI systems capable of reasoning, action, memory, and self-improvement. We conceptualize this transition as a shift from Chatbot to Digital Colleague: from conversational answers to persistent work. We organize this transition along two tightly coupled dimensions. First, at the cognitive core level, LLMs are advancing from Chatbot-era "fast thinking" systems driven by next-token prediction toward Thinking LLMs that leverage inference-time computation, Chain-of-Thought reasoning, reflection, process supervision, and reinforcement learning to support more deliberate and reliable cognition. Second, at the tool-augmented task execution level, LLMs are progressing from tool-calling Agents that invoke external resources in an ad hoc manner toward OpenClaw-style workstation systems (OpenClaw) equipped with persistent Workspaces, skills, verification loops, and governance. The "Workspace + Skill" paradigm makes episodic tool use colleague-like via state persistence, reusable procedures, task closure, and experience reuse. We examine data construction shifts from instruction-response pairs to State-Action-Observation trajectories and evaluation from static benchmarks to sandboxed, auditable, self-evolving AI ecosystems.

</details>


### [188] [Dense Coordinate-List Fine-Tuning Induces a Controllable Interference Surface in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14507)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Boguang Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了视觉语言模型微调输出密集坐标时引发的“重复输出”现象，证明该现象与结构化输出紧密相关，并可通过简单的后处理机制在保持高性能的同时实现有效控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究视觉语言模型（VLM）在微调以生成密集坐标列表时，模型输出行为（序列化、重复、终止）的变化及其作为控制面的表现。

Method: 使用 LoRA 对 Gemma 2 12B 和 Qwen3-VL-8B 进行密集坐标列表生成任务的微调，通过改变 LoRA 参数（秩、注意力模块位置）进行消融实验，并引入对象级重复监测与阻断机制。

Result: 实验表明，高容量 LoRA 能显著提升定位性能（F1@0.3 从 0.007 提升至 0.448），但也诱发了明显的输出重复问题；通过实施“对象级重复阻断”，可消除重复问题且几乎不影响 F1 分数，且该效应存在于特定的边界框坐标结构中。

Conclusion: 密集坐标列表的微调会产生结构特定的跨模型干扰（如重复输出），但这可以通过特定于任务的解码后处理（如对象级重复阻断）进行有效控制，且不损失模型性能。

Abstract: Fine-tuning vision-language models to emit dense coordinate lists improves visual grounding but also changes how models serialize, repeat, and terminate structured outputs. We study this behavior as a generation and control surface. In Gemma 4 12B, high-capacity q/k/v/o LoRA raises class-aware F1@0.3 from 0.007 to 0.448 while inducing repeated-tail pressure (duplicate rate 0.080, max repeat 23). A q/v rank sweep keeps max repeat at 21-22 across ranks 4-64, showing capacity persistence. The target signal is separable: object-level repeat-stop removes exact repeated records (duplicate rate 0.000, max repeat 1) while preserving F1 (0.494 to 0.490) and stricter F1@0.5 (0.381 to 0.385). Structure-axis probes localize the effect to bbox-coordinate object lists; dense non-bbox and spatial/count JSON remain repeat-clean, including under high-capacity adapters. Qwen3-VL-8B reproduces a clean controlled endpoint (F1@0.3 0.318, duplicate rate 0.000), and COCO 2017 reproduces acquisition plus duplicate pressure. Dense coordinate-list adaptation therefore creates a structure-bound, cross-family interference surface that can be measured and controlled.

</details>


### [189] [Every Eval Ever: A Unifying Schema and Community Repository for AI Evaluation Results](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14516)
*Jan Batzner,Sree Harsha Nelaturu,Anastassia Kornilova,Jon Crall,Tommaso Cerruti,Yanan Long,Yifan Mai,Sanchit Ahuja,Asaf Yehudai,Marek Šuppa,John P. Lalor,Oluwagbemike Olowe,Jatin Ganhotra,Brian H. Hu,Eliya Habba,Andrew M. Bean,Chang Liu,Sander Land,Steven Dillmann,Aniketh Garikaparthi,Elron Bandel,Saki Imai,James Edgell,Wm. Matthew Kennedy,Jenny Chim,Patrick Meusling,Asteria Kaeberlein,Venkata Ramachandra Karthik Chundi,Manasi Patwardhan,Martin Ku,Austin Meek,Leon Knauer,Brian Wingenroth,Srishti Yadav,Usman Gohar,Felix Friedrich,Michelle Lin,Jennifer Mickel,Arman Cohan,Stella Biderman,Irene Solaiman,Zeerak Talat,Anka Reuel,Mubashara Akhtar,Gjergji Kasneci,Avijit Ghosh,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对 AI 评估领域数据格式散乱、框架不一致的问题，本文推出了 Every Eval Ever 统一模式及众包数据库，实现了跨平台的评估结果标准化，提升了科研的可比性与复用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 AI 评估结果存在严重的碎片化问题，主要体现在格式不兼容、分布零散，以及不同评估框架导致的结果差异和元数据记录不一致，极大阻碍了模型间的横向对比、评估科学的发展及成本优化。

Method: 1. 设计了一个跨框架的统一 JSON 模式（包含结果元数据及实例级数据）；2. 开发了自动转换工具，将现有的各种评估格式、测试平台和榜单数据转化为统一模式；3. 基于 Hugging Face 构建了一个社区驱动的众包数据库。

Result: 建立了包含 22,235 个模型、2,273 个独特基准测试和 31 种评估格式的统一数据库，有效实现了评估数据的标准化和可复用性。

Conclusion: 本文提出了 Every Eval Ever，这是第一个用于 AI 评估结果的统一模式和社区众包存储库，旨在通过标准化解决 AI 评估领域的数据碎片化和不一致问题。

Abstract: AI evaluations are widely used for testing and understanding progress. However, the diverse evaluators bring with them inconsistencies that challenge analysis and comparison. First, results are saved in incompatible formats, scattered across leaderboards, papers, blog posts, evaluation harness logs, and custom repositories. Second, results are created by different evaluation frameworks, which produce divergent scores for nominally identical evaluations and record metadata inconsistently, hindering comparison, cross-community evaluation science, cost reduction, and reuse. We introduce Every Eval Ever, the first shared schema and community-crowdsourced repository for AI evaluation results. The schema standardizes how evaluations are represented in a unified, single JSON document. It is source-agnostic by design, ingesting results from evaluation harnesses and papers alike, and optionally stores per-instance outputs for fine-grained analysis. We contribute: (i) a community-governed metadata schema with a companion instance-level schema, the first standardization effort of its kind; (ii) automatic converters from popular formats, evaluation harnesses, and leaderboards to the unified schema; and (iii) a crowdsourced community database hosted on Hugging Face, currently spanning to date 22,235 models, 2,273 unique benchmarks, and 31 evaluation formats.

</details>


### [190] [StreamMemBench: Streaming Evaluation of Agent Memory for Future-Oriented Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14571)
*Guanming Liu,Yuqi Ren,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Weihang Wang,Jiahao Liu,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现有智能体记忆基准在流式交互评估上的缺失，提出了 StreamMemBench，通过两阶段任务序列量化考察智能体的证据检索、即时使用以及基于反馈的经验迁移能力，实验揭示了当前系统在处理长期记忆应用时的显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化智能体记忆评估基准大多将对话回溯或任务改进割裂开来，缺乏对从“流式观察”到“未来辅助”这一完整轨迹的测试，导致智能体难以将历史信息转化为有效的长期辅助能力。

Method: 提出了 StreamMemBench 流式基准测试，该基准以 EgoLife 自中心视角流为基础，构建了基于“证据锚点”的两阶段任务序列：第一阶段测试证据的即时使用，第二阶段测试反馈和交互经验的再次利用，并设定了四个针对性的量化评估指标。

Result: 通过对八种不同记忆系统及两种主干模型的实验验证，发现现有系统在证据利用率和反馈迁移至后续行为方面表现普遍较差，揭示了记忆系统在长期交互中的局限性。

Conclusion: 当前的记忆系统在处理流式观察和将反馈转化为可靠的后续行为方面存在显著不足，即便数据已被存储，系统也难以有效地利用这些证据来优化后续任务。

Abstract: A central role of personal-agent memory is to turn stored information and prior interactions into future-oriented assistance. In daily use, useful cues come from what the agent observes and how the user interacts with the agent, and the agent must carry them forward from the current request to similar future tasks. Existing memory benchmarks usually test dialogue recall or task improvement in isolation, leaving the trajectory from streaming observations to later assistance largely untested. We introduce StreamMemBench, a streaming benchmark that constructs a two-step task sequence around each evidence anchor from EgoLife egocentric streams. The initial task tests evidence use, while the follow-up task tests whether feedback and interaction experience are reused. Four metrics diagnose evidence recall, initial evidence use, feedback incorporation, and follow-up reuse. Experiments with eight memory systems across two backbones show that current systems often fail to use observed evidence or turn feedback into reliable follow-up behavior, even when evidence is stored or feedback is incorporated locally. StreamMemBench is publicly available at https://github.com/landian60/StreamMemBench.

</details>


### [191] [VISTA: View-Consistent Self-Verified Training for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14579)
*Xinyu Qiu,Yunzhu Zhang,Heng Jia,Shuheng Shen,Changhua Meng,Linchao Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VISTA 通过多视图对比与自验证机制改进了 GUI Grounding 任务中的 GRPO 算法，有效解决了样本同质化问题，实现了模型性能与鲁棒性的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对 GUI Grounding 应用 GRPO 时，单一视图采样常导致组内结果高度同质化（全成功或全失败），产生无效的相对优势估计，限制了模型的学习效果。

Method: 提出了 VISTA (View-Consistent Self-Verified Training) 框架：1) 构建包含同一 GUI 实例多个“目标保持”视图的比较组，跨越视觉变化进行策略优化；2) 引入自验证跨视图锚点（Self-verified cross-view anchor），通过优势加权损失与高奖励滚动引导，稳定坐标生成。

Result: 在五个 GUI Grounding 基准测试中全面提升，以 ScreenSpot-Pro 为例，Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B 模型性能显著提升（如 30B 模型从 53.7% 提升至 67.0%），且表现出更高的最差视图准确率与更低的预测波动率。

Conclusion: VISTA 通过引入多视图一致性与自验证机制，有效解决了 GUI Grounding 任务中 GRPO 方法因样本同质化导致的训练不稳定问题，显著提升了模型在多项基准测试中的定位精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: When applying Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) for GUI Grounding, rollouts are sampled from a single screenshot view; groups often become either all failures on difficult instances or all successes on easy ones, yielding no useful relative advantage. We propose VISTA (View-Consistent Self-Verified Training), a GRPO-based training framework that constructs each comparison group from multiple target-preserving views of the same GUI instance.Each view is generated by a crop that keeps the target element visible and remaps its box exactly, so model rollouts are compared across semantically equivalent but geometrically different inputs. To stabilize short coordinate generation without turning reinforcement learning into unconditional imitation, VISTA further adds a self-verified cross-view anchor: an oracle answer optimized with an advantage-weighted loss, excluded from the group baseline and activated only when the model has produced a maximum-reward rollout. Across five GUI-grounding benchmarks and multiple Qwen backbones, VISTA consistently improves grounding accuracy.On ScreenSpot-Pro, it raises Qwen3-VL 4B/8B/30B-A3B from 55.5/52.7/53.7 to 63.4/65.8/67.0. Robustness analyses further show higher worst-view accuracy and lower prediction flip rates.

</details>


### [192] [A Temporal Planning Framework for Disruption Aware Dynamic Route Optimization in Heterogeneous Railway Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14582)
*Pollob Chandra Ray,Sabah Binte Noor,Fazlul Hasan Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于 PDDL 2.1 的时序规划框架，旨在解决异构铁路网络下路径优化与中断管理的复杂调度问题，能够自动生成安全、无冲突的运行轨道与时间计划，降低对人工调度的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究大多集中在宏观时刻表编制，忽略了底层运营细节（如道岔切换协调），在异构轨道系统和存在随机干扰的情况下，导致调度复杂度高且过度依赖人工决策，增加了铁路运营的安全风险。

Method: 提出了一种基于时序规划（Temporal Planning）的框架，使用 PDDL 2.1 语言对铁路运行进行形式化建模，明确考虑了轨距兼容性约束和多种干扰场景，并利用现有的时序规划器生成冲突检测的含时间戳操作计划。

Result: 通过包含 200 个实例（最多 1000 个轨道点和 120 列火车）的基准测试集验证，证明该框架能高效生成符合约束的运营计划，并具备处理多轨距兼容性和随机干扰的能力。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将铁路运行建模为时序规划问题，有效地解决了异构铁路网络中复杂的动态路径优化与运行中断管理问题，减少了对人工决策的依赖，提升了运行的安全性和协同效率。

Abstract: Efficient route optimization play a vital role in ensuring both safety and punctuality in railway operations. It is very crucial particularly in heterogeneous multi-gauge railway networks with varying train speed, stopping pattern, infrastructure compatibility constraints increase coordination complexity. In single-track systems these challenges are further intensify due to all trains to share the same track and requires frequent track switching.Stochastic disruptions events including blocked tracks, blocked trains, engine failure and speed slowdowns introduces additional unpredictability in operations and deviate the timetable. However, existing studies predominantly focuses on high-level timetabling, omitting operational details such as track switching coordination. As a result leaving decision to human operators, increasing safety risks into railway operations. This study proposes a framework based on temporal planning for dynamic route optimization and disruption management in heterogeneous railway systems. The framework formulates railway operations as a temporal planning problem using PDDL 2.1 with explicitly modeling gauge compatibility constraints and diverse disruption scenarios. It generates conflict-free timestamped operational plans specifying both optimized schedules and executable action sequences. To evaluate the proposed framework, we developed a benchmark problem set with 200 instances using up to 1,000 track points and 120 trains. Two state-of-the-art temporal planners and a plan validator were employed to assessed the framework. The experimental results demonstrate that the framework effectively generates temporal operational plans for heterogeneous railway systems and handles multi-gauge constraints, disruptions, and reduces dependence on manual decision making.

</details>


### [193] [Abstracting Cross-Domain Action Sequences into Interpretable Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14654)
*Gaurav Verma,Scott Counts*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkflowView利用大语言模型（LLM）将繁杂、低层级的交互日志抽象为高层级的工作流活动，显著提升了行为分析的有效性与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时序交互日志粒度过细且噪声较大，难以提取用户工作流的深层洞察；传统的深度学习聚类方法对噪声敏感且跨应用泛化能力较差。

Method: 提出了WorkflowView框架，利用大语言模型（LLMs）将低层级的、带有噪声的动作序列抽象为高层级的用户活动，并通过三个不同的任务验证了其有效性与通用性。

Result: 在三个跨领域任务中表现优异：(1) 浏览器日志的零样本任务描述重构（语义相似度 $\mu_{sim} = 0.91$）；(2) MOOC交互日志的少样本学生辍学预测（仅需5个样本即可达到加权 $F_1 = 0.90$）；(3) 实现Microsoft Word中AI工具整合的匿名化隐私保护分析。

Conclusion: 基于大语言模型（LLM）的抽象化方法为将低层级的行为日志转化为高层级、可解释且具可操作性的洞察提供了一种稳健且高效的途径。此外，研究还强调了在部署此类系统时需考虑计算效率与用户隐私保护。

Abstract: Sequential or time-stamped interaction logs provide objective records of digital application usage, yet their granularity and noise often obscure meaningful insights into people's work. Such insights are essential for improving digital products in ways grounded in real-world user interactions. Prior research has applied deep learning models to cluster user actions into high-level activities, but these approaches are highly sensitive to noise and struggle to generalize across applications. To address this limitation, we introduce WorkflowView, a framework that uses large language models (LLMs) to abstract low-level action sequences into high-level activities. We establish the effectiveness and generality of our approach across three distinct, challenging sequential tasks and diverse domains: (a) zero-shot task description reconstruction from browser logs (achieving high semantic similarity, $μ_{sim} = 0.91$), (b) few-shot student dropout prediction using MOOC interaction logs (reaching weighted $F_1 = 0.90$ with only five few-shot examples), and (c) anonymized, privacy-preserving analysis of AI tool integration within document workflows in Microsoft Word. Our work demonstrates that LLM-based abstraction is a robust and efficient path forward for transforming low-level behavioral data into high-level, interpretable, and actionable insights. We also discuss practical considerations for deploying LLM-based inferences within logging infrastructures, including computational efficiency and user privacy.

</details>


### [194] [Towards Direct Latent-Space Synthesis for Parallel Branches in LLM-Agent Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14672)
*Shikun Liu,Mufei Li,Dongqi Fu,Haoyu Wang,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Pan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Parallel-Synthesis 是一种允许合成器直接消费并行智能体 KV Cache 的框架，消除了传统文本拼接带来的计算冗余，显著提升了多任务合成的效率与表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出大模型作为智能体执行引擎时，传统的序列化文本接口与现代智能体并行任务处理（如分支探索、子任务并行）之间存在不匹配，导致了结构信息丢失及长文本拼接带来的预填充（Prefill）计算冗余。

Method: 提出了一个即插即用的框架 Parallel-Synthesis，包含两个核心组件：1) 缓存映射器（Cache Mapper），用于校准和合并来自不同分支的独立 KV Cache；2) 微调后的合成器适配器（Synthesizer Adapter），支持从非序列化的缓存接口进行生成。通过特定数据集训练模型以适配缓存内容并蒸馏出推理能力。

Result: 在九个数据集（覆盖数学、科学 QA、代码生成、GAIA 等）上，有七个数据集的表现优于或等同于传统方法。在性能方面，将首字生成时间（Time-to-first-token）缩短了 2.5 倍至 11 倍。

Conclusion: Parallel-Synthesis 为处理多智能体并行任务提供了一种比文本拼接更高效、更结构化的接口，通过直接操作 KV Cache 提升了生成速度并保持了与文本拼接相当甚至更优的性能。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly serve as execution engines for agentic systems, yet they still consume context through a sequential text interface. This creates a mismatch with modern structured agent workflows, in which independent branches explore subtasks, retrieve evidence, or generate candidate solutions before a final synthesis step. Existing systems typically merge these branches by concatenating their textual outputs, which discards the parallel structure and incurs redundant prefill computation. In this work, we introduce Parallel-Synthesis, a plug-and-play framework that enables a synthesizer to directly consume the KV caches produced by parallel worker agents. Parallel-Synthesis combines a cache mapper that calibrates independently generated branch caches with a fine-tuned synthesizer adapter that enables generation from this non-sequential cache interface. We train Parallel-Synthesis using data that exposes the synthesizer to parallel cache contexts, teaches aggregation across cached branches, and distills reasoning behavior from standard text-concatenation-based synthesis. Across nine downstream datasets spanning math, science QA, code generation, GAIA, and multi-agent database diagnosis, Parallel-Synthesis matches or outperforms text-based synthesis on seven datasets and remains close on the other two. It also reduces time-to-first-token by 2.5x-11x, suggesting that direct cache-based synthesis is a promising interface for more native and efficient synthesis over parallel agent branches.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Designing Efficient and Reachable Routes: The $k$-Step-Central Shortest Path Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14128)
*Johnson Phosavanh,Dmytro Matsypura*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了“k-Step-Central Shortest Path”问题，旨在平衡交通路线的效率与覆盖范围，并提供了一种在多项式时间内求解的高效算法，证明了通过优化可达性而非延长路径长度能更有效地提升网络覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有路线设计方案在最短路径（效率高但覆盖不足）与基于中心性的方法（可达性高但忽略操作约束）之间存在权衡。研究旨在解决如何选择一条既是最短路径又能最大化可达性（节点覆盖数）的问题。

Method: 通过分析“k-Step-Central Shortest Path”问题的结构性质，提出了一种针对无权图的高效多项式时间算法，并包含一种新的剪枝规则；同时证明了该问题在赋权图中为 NP-hard 问题，并指出该算法可用于解决寻找“紧密中心性最短路径”（Closeness-central shortest path）这一 NP-hard 问题。

Result: 在多达 2,000 个节点的合成及真实网络实验中证明了所提算法的高效性和可扩展性。研究表明，路径的有效覆盖提升可以替代路线的物理扩张。

Conclusion: 通过提高交通路线的可达性（而非单纯延长路线）可以大幅提升覆盖范围。该研究为网络设计中的资源分配提供了数据驱动的决策依据，表明投资于增强可达性的基础设施比扩建路线更有效。

Abstract: Designing rapid transportation routes requires balancing efficiency and reachability. Shortest-path models ensure direct, cost-efficient routes but ignore coverage, while centrality-based approaches maximize accessibility but do not enforce operational constraints. We study the problem of selecting a shortest path that maximizes reachability, measured as the number of nodes within a fixed distance of the path. To do this, we introduce the $k$-Step-Central Shortest Path problem and analyse its structural properties. We show that optimal solutions on unweighted graphs can be found in polynomial time and propose an algorithm with a novel pruning rule. We also prove that the problem becomes NP-hard when edge weights are introduced. Additionally, we show that our algorithm can be used to solve the NP-hard problem of finding the closeness-central shortest path in a graph. We demonstrate the efficiency and scalability of our algorithm on synthetic and real-world networks with up to 2,000 nodes. Our results show that improving reachability can substitute for route expansion: increasing the reach of transit lines drastically increases their coverage with shorter routes. This suggests that investments in active transport infrastructure that improve reachability can be more effective than extending primary routes, providing a data-driven basis for allocating resources in network design.

</details>


### [196] [On a Conjecture for Parameterized st-Orientations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14539)
*Charalampos Papamanthou*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过构建一个9顶点的反例，推翻了关于MaxSTN算法产生的最长路径总是长于或等于MinSTN算法这一学术界长期存在的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并最终推翻了关于MaxSTN和MinSTN算法在产生双连通图st-定向时最长路径长度关系的长期猜想（即 $\ell_{\max} \geq \ell_{\min}$）。

Method: 通过构造一个具有9个顶点的特定双连通图进行反例验证，对比MaxSTN和MinSTN算法在该图上的表现。

Result: 在所构造的9顶点图中，MaxSTN算法产生的最长路径长度为6，而MinSTN算法产生的最长路径长度为7，即 $\ell_{\max} < \ell_{\min}$。

Conclusion: 该猜想（对于任意双连通图，MaxSTN产生的最长路径长度不小于MinSTN产生的最长路径长度）是错误的。

Abstract: MaxSTN and MinSTN -- proposed by Papamanthou and Tollis (TCS 2008, JGAA 2010) -- are two algorithms for producing $st$-orientations of biconnected graphs with long and short longest paths respectively. Based on extensive experiments on planar and non-planar graphs of up to 5,000 nodes, it was conjectured that $\ell_{\max} \geq \ell_{\min}$ for every biconnected graph $G$, where $\ell_{\max}$ and $\ell_{\min}$ denote the longest-path lengths of the two orientations. This paper disproves this conjecture by exhibiting a biconnected graph on 9 vertices for which MaxSTN yields $\ell_{\max}=6$ while MinSTN yields $\ell_{\min}=7$, regardless of how ties are broken in either algorithm.

</details>


### [197] [An Efficient Private Algorithm for Community Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14540)
*Vincent Cohen-Addad,Alessandro Epasto,Haim Kaplan,Hanna Komlós,Silvio Lattanzi*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类基于“自适应不相交星形查询”的高效差分隐私社区发现算法，首次实现了SBM模型下近线性时间复杂度的隐私精确恢复，并将适用规模提升了两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私社区发现算法存在计算开销大（如伪多项式时间）或资源占用高（平方级复杂性）的问题，难以处理大规模图数据且难以在保证隐私的同时实现精确恢复。

Method: 提出了“自适应不相交星形算法”（adaptive disjoint-star algorithms），通过在边不相交的子图上查询节点度数，在结构上天然地实现了隐私保护，并规避了对伪多项式时间或平方级资源的依赖。

Result: 实现了差分隐私下的精确社区发现，且时间与空间复杂度达到近线性；相比于先前算法，可处理的节点规模提升了两个数量级，并在广泛的模型参数范围内均有效。

Conclusion: 该论文提出了一类新的“自适应不相交星形算法”，在保证差分隐私的前提下，实现了随机块模型（SBM）下社区发现算法在近线性时间和空间复杂度下的精确恢复，显著提升了算法的扩展性。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the community detection problem in the stochastic block model (SBM) under privacy constraints. We introduce private and highly efficient algorithms for exact community detection within the SBM framework. Our algorithms represent the first differentially private methods capable of achieving exact recovery in a wide range of model parameters with near-linear time and space complexity. This is a significant improvement over previous SBM recovery algorithms, which either required pseudo-polynomial time or a quadratic scaling of resources for a constant privacy budget.
  Central to our approach is the introduction of a new concept, adaptive disjoint-star algorithms. These algorithms efficiently explore the graph's structure by querying node degrees on edge-disjoint subgraphs. We demonstrate that this general class of algorithms inherently offers strong privacy guarantees, a result that potentially holds value beyond the scope of SBM community detection. Finally, in we perform an empirical analysis of our algorithms showing that they can scale exact recovery on graphs with two orders of magnitude more nodes than prior work.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Personalization and Evaluation of Conversational Information Access](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13717)
*Hideaki Joko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对对话式信息访问（CIA）系统，提出了三种技术创新：用于上下文理解的CREL实体链接方法、用于个性化数据集构建的LAPS框架，以及评估对话整体质量的FACE指标，实现了从上下文提取到响应生成及评估的全流程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式互动改变搜索范式，现有系统在处理个人上下文、生成个性化响应以及进行可解释评估方面仍存在挑战，需构建更可靠的CIA系统。

Method: 1. 实体链接：引入了ConEL数据集并提出CREL方法；2. 个性化生成：提出了LAPS方法用于构建大规模个性化对话数据集，并研究了用户偏好利用机制；3. 系统评估：提出了FACE，一种无需参考的自动评估方法。

Result: 通过实验验证，CREL有效提升了对话场景下的实体链接准确性，LAPS能够高效构建个性化数据集，FACE评估指标与人类判断高度一致。

Conclusion: 该研究系统地改进了对话式信息访问（CIA）系统的三个核心环节（实体链接、个性化生成与评估），为构建更具上下文感知能力且评估可解释的对话系统奠定了关键基础。

Abstract: Conversational interactions have reshaped information retrieval systems, as users increasingly favour direct answers over traditional hyperlinks. To build reliable Conversational Information Access (CIA) systems that account for personal context, this thesis addresses challenges: (1) personal context extraction, (2) personalized response generation, and (3) effective and interpretable system evaluation. First, we tackle personal context extraction by studying what Entity Linking (EL) in conversations entails, introducing a dataset for conversational entity linking (ConEL), and proposing CREL, a novel EL method tailored for conversational settings. Second, we focus on personalized response generation by proposing LAPS, a method for efficiently constructing large-scale, human-written, personalized conversational datasets, and using them to study how users' preferences can be utilized to generate personalized responses. Finally, we address the need for effective and interpretable system evaluation by introducing FACE, an automatic, reference-free method that assesses entire conversations and aligns closely with human judgments.

</details>


### [199] [Nomenclature Ontology for Medical And Disease names (NOMAD): taxonomy of types and origins of disease names](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13719)
*Spiros Denaxas,Cai Ytsma,Giannos Louloudis,Jackie MacArthur,Harry Hemingway*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究提出了 NOMAD 分类系统，对 ICD-10-CM 中的疾病命名法进行了大规模自动化分类，揭示了医学术语的构成特征、专科偏好及隐含的性别差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学疾病命名法缺乏系统性的分类框架，且因历史原因表现出高度的异质性和随意性。

Method: 构建了包含 9 个顶层及 20 个子类别的两级元分类学（NOMAD），利用三阶段机器学习驱动的流水线对 ICD-10-CM 2026 索引中的 22,548 个条目进行多标签分类。

Result: 成功分类了 99.1% 的条目；解剖学（63.8%）、描述性（48.4%）及病理生理学（40.2%）标签最常见；冠名疾病中仅 2.6% 以女性命名；不同临床专科展现出独特的命名偏好。

Conclusion: NOMAD 为分析疾病命名法提供了系统化架构，揭示了医学专业间的认识论传统差异，并指出医学命名中存在显著的性别偏见。

Abstract: The nomenclature of human disease has developed organically over the past centuries using Greek, Latin, and Arabic terminology and reflects the idiosyncrasies of different eras of medical discovery. Despite evident heterogeneity in naming practices, no systematic framework exists for characterising these conventions across all diseases. In this paper, we describe the Nomenclature Ontology for Medical And Disease names (NOMAD), a meta-taxonomy that classifies disease names according to their naming conventions. We developed a two-level taxonomy comprising 9 top-level categories and 20 subcategories and applied it to 22,548 index entries from the ICD-10-CM 2026 Alphabetical Index in a scalable three-stage machine learning-driven classification pipeline. Classification was multi-label, reflecting the compositional nature of medical nomenclature. We classified 99.1% of terms with a mean of 2.12 labels per entry. Anatomical categories were the most prevalent (63.8% of entries), followed by Descriptive (48.4%) and Pathophysiological (40.2%), while Eponymous and Geographical labels were less common than their cultural prominence might suggest (9.7% and 1.9% respectively). Among all Eponymous diseases, we identified only 57 (2.6%) of diseases named after a female person. We manually reviewed a random sample of n=2,255 entries (10%) for accuracy and calculated a full agreement rate of 70% and partial agreement rate of 26% (macro-averaged Cohen's Kappa score 0.832). Naming convention profiles varied substantially across ICD-10-CM chapters, reflecting specialty-specific epistemological traditions: infectious disease chapters were dominated by etiological labels and showed the highest proportion of geographical region related labels, the circulatory chapter by anatomical and pathophysiological labels, and mental and behavioural disorders showed the highest prevalence of socio-behavioral labels.

</details>


### [200] [TASR: Training-Free Adaptive Stopping for Iterative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13814)
*Adrian Kieback,Uyiosa Philip Amadasun,Aman Chadha,Aaron Elkins*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种即插即用的无训练停止规则 TASR，通过监控答案重复性和 Logit 边际，在维持 RAG 代理性能的前提下显著降低不必要的检索开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代检索增强生成（RAG）代理常存在过度检索问题，在模型已确定答案后继续检索会浪费计算资源。现有的停止机制通常依赖于有监督训练，存在通用性差、需针对不同任务或模型重训练的缺陷。

Method: 作者提出一种基于逻辑判断的规则 TASR：当模型重复前一轮的归一化回答，且其等张校准（isotonically calibrated）后的 Logit 边际（logit margin）超过 0.25 时自动停止检索。该方法不依赖训练，无需额外的分类器或价值头。

Result: 在 24 种不同的配置测试中，TASR 表现优异：在保持 94.8% 的固定 k=5 性能的同时，平均调用量仅为 62.6%；在多个数据集测试中超越了固定 k=3 的性能，且在不同的检索器和模型家族中具备良好的泛化性。通过对比发现，基于 Logit 的度量比基于 RLHF 模型自身的置信度打分具有更好的分类分离能力。

Conclusion: TASR 是一个无需训练的、具备审计功能的通用停止规则，在高效率地保持模型性能的同时，克服了传统训练型停止策略的局限性，并为未来的停止控制器研究提供了强有力的 Pareto 基准。

Abstract: Iterative retrieval-augmented generation agents commonly overspend by continuing to retrieve after the model has converged on an answer, incurring calls that change neither the prediction nor the supporting evidence. Existing remedies learn a stopping policy from labeled trajectories, tying the decision to a trained component that requires retraining for each new model or task. We propose TASR (Training-Free Adaptive Stopping Rule), a one-line predicate that fires when the model repeats its previous-round normalized answer and the isotonically calibrated logit margin exceeds 0.25. No classifier or value head is learned; the threshold is fixed across all twenty-four (model, retriever, corpus) configurations we evaluate. On a 3-model x 2-dataset distractor grid, TASR retains 94.8% of fixed-k=5's macro F1 at 62.6% of its calls and exceeds fixed-k=3 by +3.42 F1. The pattern holds on nine open-domain BM25 cells (55.01 F1 at 2.98 calls vs. 54.33 at 3.00 for fixed-k=3) and, with calibration locked from the distractor split, on nine dense-retrieval cells across two retriever families, with zero significant regressions in either extension. The rule was selected from an exhaustive enumeration of 381 candidate stopping rules; no alternative Pareto-dominates it on any evaluated configuration. A signal-quality analysis shows that verbalized 1-5 confidence collapses on RLHF-tuned models (96.5% of values equal 5, entropy 0.182 nats), while the logit margin achieves 44x better class-conditional separation, grounding the design in a measurable model pathology. TASR is an auditable, training-free Pareto baseline against which learned stopping controllers can be compared. Code is publicly available.

</details>


### [201] [Hybrid Neural Retrieval with Generative Query Refinement for Quranic Passage Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13837)
*Mohamed G. Salman,Mohammad E. Moftah,Ali Hamdi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对古兰经检索中的跨语言语义鸿沟难题，本文提出一种集成混合检索、语义重排序、过滤机制及AraT5聚合的四阶段神经网络系统，显著增强了多经文检索的准确性与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于现代标准阿拉伯语（MSA）与古兰经古典阿拉伯语（CA）之间存在语言复杂性和巨大的语义鸿沟，导致传统检索方法在古兰经段落检索（PR）任务中效果欠佳，亟需解决多经文检索与零命中查询挑战。

Method: 构建四阶段神经架构：1. 混合检索（AraColBERT密集索引+BM25稀疏检索）；2. 语义重排序（CAMeLBERTmix交叉编码器）；3. 置信度门控机制（过滤零答案查询）；4. 多经文聚合优化（基于AraT5的重构模块）。

Result: 在扩展版Quran QA 2022数据集上，Recall@10达到0.7024，MAP@10达到0.4947。尽管在绝对排名精度（MRR=0.5807）上较单一模型有微小权衡，但整体表现更稳健且具备更好的上下文感知能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种四阶段神经架构，有效解决了古兰经段落检索中多经文聚合、零答案过滤及现代标准阿拉伯语与古典阿拉伯语之间语义鸿沟的问题，提升了系统的综合检索性能。

Abstract: Quranic Passage Retrieval (PR) could be a challenging task due to the linguistic complexity and the semantic gap between the Modern Standard Arabic (MSA) used in daily queries and the Classical Arabic (CA) of the Holy Quran. These factors hinder conventional retrieval methods. To handle these limitations and improve multi-verse retrieval and filter the zero-answer queries, this paper proposes a four-phase neural architecture designed to enhance retrieval accuracy and contextual understanding. The methodology combines hybrid candidate retrieval using AraColBERT dense indexing and BM25 sparse retrieval, followed by semantic reranking with a CAMeLBERTmix cross-encoder. A confidence gating mechanism is then applied to filter zero-answer queries, and an AraT5-based refinement module for multi-verse aggregation. The system is evaluated on an expanded version of the Quran QA 2022 dataset. Results show improved performance compared to the baseline models, achieving a Recall@10 of 0.7024 and a Mean Average Precision (MAP@10) of 0.4947. While the system exhibits a marginal tradeoff in absolute top-rank precision (MRR = 0.5807) compared to heavily optimised single models, the proposed architecture provides a substantially more comprehensive, reliable, and context aware solution for multi-verse Quranic passage retrieval.

</details>


### [202] [Mood-Aware Music Recommendation: Integrating User Affective Signals into Ranking Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13858)
*Terence Zeng,Abhishek K. Umrawal*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于情绪感知（能量-效价空间）的音乐推荐框架，通过整合用户情感信号，有效提升了音乐推荐的用户满意度和质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作过滤在音乐领域因交互稀疏而表现不佳，且现有基于内容的推荐方法较少关注音乐的情感属性，而用户的情感状态对音乐选择具有显著影响。

Method: 提出了一种情绪条件排序框架（Mood-conditioned ranking framework），通过在能量-效价空间使用基于Softmax的采样技术，将用户的情感信号整合进推荐流程。

Result: 通过单盲实验对比发现，该方法显著提升了用户对推荐质量的主观感知，优于基准系统。

Conclusion: 将情绪信号融入音乐推荐系统是提升个性化体验的有效途径，初步验证了基于能量-效价（Energy-Valence）空间的情绪条件排序框架的优越性。

Abstract: Recommendation systems are essential in modern music streaming platforms due to the vast amount of available content. While collaborative filtering is widely used to suggest items based on the preferences of others with similar patterns, it performs poorly in domains where user-item interactions are sparse, such as music. Content-based filtering is an alternative approach that examines the qualities of the items themselves. Genre, instrumentation, and lyrics have been explored; however, relatively little attention has been given to emotion recognition. Since a user's emotional state strongly influences their music choice, incorporating mood signals offers a promising direction for personalization. In this work, we propose a mood-conditioned ranking framework that integrates user affective signals into the recommendation process via softmax-based sampling in the energy-valence space. We evaluate the approach via single-blind experiments in which participants compare recommendations from the proposed system against a baseline. The results indicate improved perceived recommendation quality, providing preliminary evidence for the effectiveness of incorporating mood-based inputs into music recommendations.

</details>


### [203] [ADORE: Iterative Query Expansion with Retrieval-Grounded Relevance Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13905)
*Amin Bigdeli,Negar Arabzadeh,Radin Hamidi Rad,Sajad Ebrahimi,Charles L. A. Clarke,Ebrahim Bagheri*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出ADORE框架，通过引入检索结果反馈和相关性评估的迭代循环，解决了传统查询扩展中缺乏语料库实时验证导致的检索漂移问题，在多项主流基准测试中显著提升了检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于LLM的查询扩展大多是单向生成的，缺乏对目标语料库实际响应的核实，容易导致检索漂移（retrieval drift）、引入误导性词汇或遗漏区分性术语。因此，需要一种能够结合检索反馈、具备闭环优化能力的扩展方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为ADORE（ADapt, Observe, Relevance Evaluate）的迭代式查询扩展框架。该方法包含三个环节：由LLM生成伪段落，通过检索器获取反馈，以及利用相关性评估器判断检索结果的质量，并将这些评价指标反馈给LLM以调整（加强或抑制）后续的词汇选择。

Result: 在TREC Deep Learning、BEIR和BRIGHT等数据集上表现领先。相比于BM25，在BEIR上nDCG@10提升24.5%，相比最强基线提升3.6%；在BRIGHT上相比BM25提升122.9%，相比最强基线提升9.2%。

Conclusion: ADORE框架证明了检索反馈循环在查询扩展中的重要性。通过利用检索和评估结果作为下一轮生成过程的指导，ADORE能够显著缓解检索漂移问题，提升查询扩展的精确度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: LLM-based query expansion improves retrieval by enriching the original query with additional context. Yet most methods remain generation-driven, producing plausible pseudo-documents or expansions without checking how the target corpus responds. This can introduce retrieval drift, amplify misleading vocabulary, or miss terms that distinguish relevant from non-relevant documents. We argue that effective expansion requires retrieval-grounded feedback, not just single-pass generation or unverified iteration. We introduce ADORE (ADapt, Observe, Relevance Evaluate), an iterative framework that turns retrieval outcomes into feedback for the next expansion. At each round, an LLM generates pseudo-passages, a retriever exposes the corpus response, and a relevance assessor evaluates retrieved documents against the original query. These judgments identify what to reinforce, what remains undercovered, and what to suppress. Across TREC Deep Learning, BEIR, and BRIGHT, ADORE consistently outperforms strong query expansion baselines with notable improvements across nearly all evaluation settings, improving average nDCG@10 by 24.5% over BM25 and 3.6% over the strongest prior query expansion method on BEIR, and by 122.9% over BM25 and 9.2% over the best query expansion baseline on BRIGHT. Our code and data are publicly available.

</details>


### [204] [When Recommendation Denoising Meets Popularity Bias: Understanding and Mitigating Their Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14046)
*Guohang Zeng,Jie Lu,Guangquan Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 现有的去噪方法常误删长尾真实信号，本文提出 PAD 插件，通过依据流行度差异化去噪强度，在消除噪声的同时保留长尾数据，提升了推荐系统的性能与多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于“小损失（small-loss）”启发式的推荐系统去噪方法存在缺陷：它们未能区分“噪声”与“难以拟合的真实长尾数据”，导致在去噪过程中过度过滤长尾信号，加剧了头部偏见（popularity bias）。

Method: 提出了 Popularity-Aware Denoising (PAD) 框架，该框架根据物品流行度动态调节去噪强度，对头部物品实施强去噪，对尾部物品采取保守策略。

Result: 在三个数据集和三种骨干网络上的实验表明，PAD 优于主流去噪基准，并在保持高准确度的同时实现了更好的多样性（accuracy-diversity tradeoff）。

Conclusion: 基于流行度的去噪方法（PAD）能有效平衡去噪需求与长尾信号保留，在提升模型准确性的同时优化了推荐多样性。

Abstract: Implicit feedback is the dominant data source for recommender systems, but behavioral logs are often contaminated by false-positive interactions caused by mis-clicks, biased exposure, and interface effects. Denoising recommendation methods improve robustness by down-weighting or filtering interactions suspected to be noisy, often relying on the small-loss heuristic. We revisit this heuristic through the lens of popularity bias. Tail-item positives can be harder to fit because they are sparsely observed, and thus may receive larger losses even when they reflect genuine user preference. Under such popularity-dependent loss patterns, monotone loss-based reweighting can suppress clean-but-hard tail signals and increase the head-tail imbalance in effective supervision.
  We formalize this interaction through the effective head-tail signal ratio induced by denoising weights and derive a conditional reallocation result: when the loss distribution of tail positives is right-shifted relative to that of head positives, small-loss reweighting increases the effective head-tail signal ratio compared with ERM. Motivated by this analysis, we propose Popularity-Aware Denoising (PAD), a lightweight plug-in framework that modulates denoising strength by item popularity. PAD applies stronger denoising to highly exposed items while being more conservative on tail items, preserving more clean-but-hard long-tail signals. Experiments on three datasets and three backbones show that PAD generally improves over representative denoising baselines and provides favorable accuracy-diversity tradeoffs, especially on MF-style recommenders.

</details>


### [205] [Knowledge Graph Enhanced Memory-Augmented Retrieval for Long Context Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14047)
*Ghadir Alselwi,Basem Suleiman,Hao Xue,Shoaib Jameel,Hakim Hacid,Flora D. Salim,Imran Razzak*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对长文本建模中实体关系理解的挑战，KGERMAR 通过推理阶段实时构建动态知识图谱并融合多维记忆，实现了更高的困惑度性能与显著的内存效率优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长文本建模中，仅靠语义相似度难以维持对大规模文本中实体状态及复杂关系的连贯理解，限制了模型处理长上下文的能力。

Method: 提出一种结合上下文知识图谱（KGs）的内存增强语言建模框架。包括：1. 实时实体与关系抽取以构建动态图；2. 包含上下文、语义、结构三个维度的多组件记忆架构；3. 通过学习到的权重融合不同类型的检索信号。

Result: 在多个大规模数据集（SlimPajama, WikiText-103等）上，相比基线模型，困惑度（Perplexity）降低至多 8.5%，内存效率提升 2-2.5 倍，并在各类 NLU 任务中展现出更强的上下文学习能力。

Conclusion: KGERMAR 通过动态图构建与多维记忆融合，有效解决了长文本建模中实体关系理解不足的问题，在保持高准确性的同时实现了显著的内存效率提升，证明了基于输入上下文动态构建知识图谱优于依赖静态知识库的传统方法。

Abstract: Long-context language modeling requires not only extending context windows but maintaining coherent understanding of entity states and relationships across thousands of tokens -- a challenge that semantic similarity alone cannot address. KGERMAR addresses this by constructing dynamic, context-specific knowledge graphs from input text during inference, enabling domain-adaptive retrieval that leverages both semantic similarity and explicit entity relationships. The framework performs real-time entity and relation extraction to build contextual knowledge graphs, then integrates graph-structural embeddings with textual semantics through a multi-component memory architecture. Three memory banks -- contextual, semantic, and structural -- are maintained with retrieval signals fused via learned weights to capture both surface-level semantics and deeper relational patterns. Evaluated on SlimPajama (84.7K training examples), WikiText-103 (4,358 examples), PG-19 (100 examples), and Proof-pile (46.3K examples), KGERMAR achieves up to 8.5\% lower perplexity and 2--2.5x better memory efficiency than memory-augmented baselines across context lengths from 1K to 32K tokens, with superior in-context learning performance across five NLU tasks. The dynamic knowledge graph construction approach advances memory-augmented language modeling by enabling domain-specific knowledge representation that adapts to input contexts rather than relying on fixed knowledge bases.

</details>


### [206] [CoRe: A Continuously Reward-Finetuned LLM Query Rewriter for Multi-Stage Context-Aware Relevance in Web-Scale Video Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14127)
*Yilin Wen,Rong Yang,Xiaojia Chang,Hong Sun,Gefu Tang,Chunhui Liu,Jeffrey Chen,Zeyu Ma,Lisong Qiu,Xiaochuan Fan,Congjia Yu,Quan Zhou,Yuheng Chen,Zian Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CoRe是一个经济高效的生产级LLM查询重写系统，通过将生产检索器的实际评分引入奖励机制并利用半在线DPO优化，解决了离线训练与线上评估的偏差，在短视频搜索场景中实现了性能与稳定性的双重提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM查询重写面临核心权衡：训练奖励需精准契合生产检索器的消费行为，但训练流程又必须足够低成本以支持随数据漂移的持续重训练和部署。

Method: 1. 闭环奖励设计：使用生产多模态相关性模型作为奖励来源，并采用与生产融合代数一致的乘法比例形式；2. 半在线混合偏好优化 (Mixed Preference Optimization)：基于DPO的成对目标，仅针对采样的top-k/bottom-k子集进行梯度更新，并通过分阶段参数同步机制降低计算成本；3. 安全保障：引入自动提升门控（Promotion Gate）监控奖励偏移，并建立分级（recall/rawrank/finerank）并行信号消费机制以控制失败风险。

Result: 在长达五个月的生产环境部署中，通过两次A/B测试验证，显著降低了重写冲击查询的“更改查询率”（change-query rate），且所有核心相关性和用户参与度指标均得到正面改善。

Conclusion: CoRe证明了一种经济高效且鲁棒的LLM查询重写优化框架，通过闭环的生产反馈机制（模拟生产融合代数）和半在线优化策略，成功在短视频搜索引擎中实现持续部署并显著提升了检索质量和用户参与度。

Abstract: LLM-based query rewriters in production face a tension: the training reward must reflect how the rewrite is consumed by the production ranker, yet the training procedure must be cheap enough to support continuous redeployment as data drifts. We present CoRe (Context Relevance), such a system, redeployed weekly for over five months in a major short-video search engine. Our reward uses the deployed multimodal relevance model as its source and a multiplicative ratio form mirroring the production fusion algebra, closing the simulation-production gap that offline reward proxies leave open. A semi-online Mixed Preference Optimization loop makes this reward affordable at multi-million-instance weekly scale: a DPO-style pairwise objective restricts the gradient pass to a small top-k/bottom-k subset of sampled trajectories, and a phase structure reduces trainer/inference-server parameter syncs from per-step to per-phase. An automated promotion gate over reward-like and stability metrics detected and recovered from a real reward-hacking incident in production. Rewriter output is consumed as parallel relevance signals at recall, rawrank, and finerank without displacing the original signals, bounding rewriter-failure blast radius. Online A/B from two sequential production launches, first deploying the rewriter at finerank, then extending consumption to recall and rawrank, delivers statistically significant reductions in change-query rate on rewrite-impacted queries, with all headline relevance and engagement metrics moving in the expected direction.

</details>


### [207] [ChronoID: Infusing Explicit Temporal Signals into Semantic IDs for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14260)
*Dongdong Nian,Dongqi Fu,Chenliang Xu,Yinglong Xia,Hong Li,Hong Yan,Jian Kang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 为了解决生成式推荐中语义ID缺乏时间感知这一局限性，本文提出了ChronoID框架，通过在语义ID学习中显式整合时间维度，有效捕捉动态的用户偏好，并建立了相应的评价基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成式推荐中的语义ID学习是时间不可知的（time-agnostic），这导致系统错误地假设用户偏好和物品语义是时间平稳的（stationary），忽略了现实世界中交互节奏的演变对推荐的影响。

Method: 1. 系统化地界定了时间信号在语义ID设计中的三个正交维度。 2. 提出了一个统一的“ChronoID”框架，用于实现时间感知的语义ID学习。 3. 构建了一个新的时间显式生成式推荐基准测试（Benchmark），用于评估不同时间融入方式的有效性。

Result: 通过ChronoID框架及配套的基准测试，明确了时间信息注入的高效路径，优化了相应模型架构，并论证了引入显式时间信号对提升生成式推荐性能的显著增益。

Conclusion: 该研究通过将时间显式编码引入语义ID，证明了在生成式推荐中处理时间动态性的必要性。研究表明，通过ChronoID框架整合时间信号能够显著捕捉用户意图与物品语义随时间的演变，从而提升推荐效果。

Abstract: Semantic IDs are crucial in generative recommendation, but with a fundamental limitation: temporal information is not well incorporated into semantic IDs. Instead, time influences recommendation only implicitly (e.g., through session construction heuristics, preference alignment, or sequence order), while existing semantic ID learning remains entirely time-agnostic. This design conflates interactions occurring under distinct temporal contexts into identical semantic representations, implicitly assuming that item semantics and user intent are temporally stationary. Such an assumption is misaligned with real-world recommendation scenarios, where evolving interaction rhythms play a central role. In this work, we investigate where and how the explicit time should be incorporated into semantic ID for generative recommendation. First, we systematically characterize the design space along three orthogonal dimensions of temporal signals and present a unified framework, ChronoID, for time-aware semantic ID learning. Then, by contributing a new time-explicit generation recommendation benchmark, ChronoID answers the questions: what is the effective way of infusing time, how to design the architecture, and where does the gain come from.

</details>


### [208] [ScoreGate: Adaptive Chunk Selection for Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Dual-Score Statistical Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14269)
*Karamvir Singh,Arvind Jain*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ScoreGate通过结合双编码器和交叉编码器的分数，实现了检索量的动态自适应，解决了固定Top-K检索在效率与质量间难以平衡的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的固定数量检索机制（Fixed-cardinality retrieval）存在缺陷：无法根据查询复杂度动态调整，导致窄查询过度检索（浪费资源）和组合查询检索不足（影响召回）。

Method: ScoreGate：一种轻量级的评分空间决策机制。它利用现有检索流程中的两个既有分数（双编码器相似度 s_i 和交叉编码器重排序分数 r_i），无需额外模型推断，动态决定是否保留检索到的 Chunk。

Result: 在MS MARCO数据集上，在减少35%检索块的情况下，MRR@10达到0.401；在内部基准测试中，保持97.77-99.34%高召回率的同时，减少了34.8%的Token消耗，且额外延迟仅31ms。

Conclusion: 自适应检索数量策略（如ScoreGate）能够有效提升检索效率，在大幅减少处理Token数量的同时，保持甚至提升检索质量。

Abstract: Fixed-cardinality retrieval injects a constant top-K chunks into the generator regardless of query complexity, causing over-retrieval for narrow queries and under-retrieval for compositional ones. We describe ScoreGate, a lightweight score-space decision mechanism that controls retrieval cardinality at inference time using two scores already produced by the standard pipeline: bi-encoder similarity s_i and cross-encoder reranker score r_i, with no additional model inference calls required. Its core insight is that cross-encoder affirmation can rescue semantically relevant chunks that bi-encoder retrieval ranks poorly due to vocabulary mismatch -- a failure mode unaddressed by fixed-K or single-score thresholding. On MS MARCO (200 dev queries), ScoreGate achieves MRR@10 = 0.401 with 35% fewer retained chunks than Standard Top-K. On an internal benchmark (n=300, Fleiss' kappa=0.87), ScoreGate observed zero false positives (95% CI [96.4%, 100%]) at 97.77-99.34% recall, with 34.8% fewer tokens per query and only 31ms added latency. Results on both MS MARCO and real-world production traffic suggest that adaptive retrieval cardinality can improve retrieval efficiency without degrading retrieval quality.

</details>


### [209] [Verifiable User Simulation for Search and Recommendation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14474)
*Chenglong Ma,Xinye Wanyan,Danula Hettiachchi,Ziqi Xu,Yongli Ren,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 针对LLM用户模拟器的透明度和偏见问题，本教程引入了一个包含七项审计指标的系统设计框架，并通过动手实验室强化研究人员对AI模拟器的可验证性与公平性审计能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM用户模拟器往往是不透明的（“黑盒”），难以理解其决策逻辑；此外，模拟器在处理不同背景的人群时存在偏见与歧视风险，缺乏针对性的质量评估手段。

Method: 提出了一个由七个审计组件组成的“设计与审计框架”（Persona, Contract, Execution, Trace, Verification, Feedback, Refinement loop），并包含针对推荐系统评估和搜索查询生成的动手实践环节。

Result: 通过该教程的学习，参与者能够掌握对用户模拟器进行端到端审查的方法，区分诊断性差异分析与统计验证，并能有效检测模拟器行为的忠实度、可信度及是否存在人口统计学偏见。

Conclusion: 该教程提供了一套标准化的框架，旨在实现大规模语言模型（LLM）用户模拟器的透明化、可审计性，并确保在公平性和一致性方面达到更高标准。

Abstract: Large-language-model (LLM) based user simulation is increasingly adopted for evaluating search engines, recommender systems, and retrieval-augmented generation pipelines, yet most simulators remain opaque: it is difficult to determine why a simulated user made a particular choice or whether that choice is consistent with the intended user profile. Compounding this, recent research shows that LLMs can produce biased or discriminatory responses depending on user background characteristics such as language, education level, and cultural context, raising concerns about the equitable treatment of minority and disadvantaged groups. This half-day, in-person tutorial introduces a proposed design-and-audit framework that treats a user simulator as a verifiable engineering artefact composed of seven auditable components - structured Persona, task-aware Contract, matched human-vs-agent Execution, auditable Trace, persona-aligned Verification, structured Feedback, and a Refinement loop that updates personas and contracts. Through two hands-on mini-labs on recommendation-list evaluation and search-query formulation, participants will inspect simulator behaviour end-to-end, distinguish diagnostic discrepancy analysis from statistical validation, and apply checks for fidelity, credibility, and demographic bias. The tutorial targets information retrieval and recommender systems researchers and practitioners interested in user behaviour simulation and responsible AI.

</details>


### [210] [Private Information Retrieval for Large-Scale DNA-Based Data Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14557)
*Gökberk Erdoğan,Daniella Bar-Lev,Rawad Bitar,Antonia Wachter-Zeh,Zohar Yakhini*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了DNA存储中的私有信息检索（PIR）挑战，提出了两种适配双服务器PIR协议的方法，平衡了隐私性、效率与生物化学实现可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字数据库中的PIR技术研究虽已成熟，但将其扩展至DNA存储时面临独特的生物化学查询机制及复杂性挑战，需要针对性解决方案。

Method: 针对DNA存储的特性，提出了两种旨在适配双服务器PIR协议的方案，并分析了其在信息论隐私权衡上的表现。

Result: 成功验证了双服务器PIR协议在DNA存储框架下的适应性，并揭示了该场景下隐私保护与系统性能之间的权衡机制。

Conclusion: 在DNA存储系统中实现私有信息检索（PIR）是可行的，且可以通过调整双服务器PIR协议在隐私保护、计算效率与生物化学可行性之间取得平衡。

Abstract: We investigate Private Information Retrieval (PIR) in the context of synthetic DNA-based data storage. While PIR is a well-studied primitive for digital databases, extending it to DNA-based databases presents unique challenges arising from biochemical query mechanisms and their complexity. We propose two approaches for adapting two-server PIR protocols to DNA-based storage, balancing privacy, efficiency, and feasibility. These approaches illustrate how information-theoretic privacy trade-offs manifest in DNA-based storage systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.PF'></div>

# cs.PF [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Solving Subgraph Extraction Problems Using $Δ$Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13834)
*Rebin Silva Valan Arasu,Rajiv Gupta*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ΔSearch，一个通用的启发式框架，通过 Reward-Penalty 优化机制解决多类 NP-hard 子图抽取问题，具备易用性且在多种经典图问题上表现出了与领域专用算法媲美的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多不同领域（如网络设计、物流规划等）的 NP-hard 图问题本质上都是带有约束的子图抽取问题，但目前主要依赖针对特定问题的启发式算法，缺乏统一的通用方法。

Method: 提出一种基于“奖励-惩罚”（Reward-Penalty）优化机制的通用启发式框架 ΔSearch。用户仅需定义可行性约束和最优性准则，该框架即可自动求解。此外，还展示了如何利用该框架通过激进的空间剪枝来增强精确算法的效果。

Result: 在多种单调与非单调图问题中进行了评估，在 MPS、UFLP 和 PCVC 问题上与现有最高水平启发式算法持平或超越，在其余问题上无需特定优化也可达到现有最优方法约 89% 的解质量。

Conclusion: ΔSearch 是一个通用且高效的启发式框架，由于其无需特定领域调整即可提供高质量解，证明了 Reward-Penalty 优化范式解决各类子图抽取问题的有效性。

Abstract: Many NP-hard graph problems can be modeled as optimal subgraph extraction problems with feasibility constraints. From Network Design to Facility Location, from Robotics to Graph Drawing, the subgraph extraction pattern emerges across diverse domains. Despite this commonality, these problems are typically solved with domain-specific heuristics. Usually, these problems balance competing objectives such as maximizing coverage or minimizing cost while satisfying structural constraints such as connectivity, planarity and reachability. In this work, we introduce $Δ$Search, a general and fast heuristic framework that exploits the insight of Reward-Penalty optimization for solving a large class of subgraph extraction problems. The framework is easy to use as it only requires feasibility constraints and optimality criteria to be provided by the user to express the subgraph extraction problem. We also show how exact methods can be augmented with $Δ$Search to improve their performance by aggressive pruning of the search space. We evaluate our framework on monotone graph problems such as Maximum Planar Subgraph (MPS) and Minimum Connected Dominating Set, Weighted Monotone problems such as Maximum Weighted Independent Set and Minimum Weighted Steiner Tree, and non-monotone graph problems such as Prize Collecting Vertex Cover (PCVC) and Uncapacitated Facility Location Problem (UFLP). Our results show that $Δ$Search matches or surpasses state of the art heuristics for MPS, UFLP and PCVC problems with similar runtime. For the remaining problems, $Δ$Search achieves approximately 89% of the solution quality of the state-of-the-art algorithms without any problem-specific tuning

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [212] [An Agentic Retrieval Framework for Autonomous Context-Aware Data Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13692)
*Hadi Fadlallah,Ibrahim Dhaini,Fatima Mubarak,Rima Kilany*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向数据质量评估的自主智能体检索框架，通过多智能体协作、情境感知策略生成及可行性预验证机制，解决了在大模型辅助评估数据质量时面临的可靠性和场景适配性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据质量评估多依赖静态规则或人工策略，难以适应多变的上下文场景，且在大规模自动化方面存在局限。尽管大模型提供了自动化机会，但其可靠性、落地性和执行安全性仍有待解决。

Method: 提出了一个统一的智能体检索框架（agentic-retrieval framework），包含以下核心步骤：1. 利用大模型解释自然语言描述的数据使用意图；2. 派生上下文感知的评估策略；3. 通过多智能体工作流生成可执行验证逻辑；4. 引入可行性验证阶段（feasibility validation）对生成逻辑进行预评估和迭代优化；5. 执行确定性的逻辑验证以确保结果的可审计性。

Result: 评估结果显示，该框架能根据不同的使用意图产生相应的数据质量评估结果；引入可行性门控机制有效减少了不切实际或无法执行的规则生成，提升了系统的稳健性。

Conclusion: 该框架为现代数据驱动环境提供了一种可靠、可控且自主的数据质量评估实践方案，证明了通过智能体与检索增强技术结合，可以有效地根据使用场景动态调整数据质量评估策略。

Abstract: Data quality assessment is a critical prerequisite for effective data analytics and data-driven decision-making, yet it remains a challenging task due to the inherently context-dependent nature of data quality. Existing approaches often rely on static rules or manual assessment strategies, limiting their adaptability to diverse usage scenarios and constraining automation at scale. Recent advances in artificial intelligence, particularly large language models, offer new opportunities for automating data quality assessment, but raise concerns related to reliability, grounding, and execution safety.
  In this paper, we propose a unified agentic-retrieval framework for autonomous context-aware data quality assessment. The framework interprets natural-language descriptions of intended data usage, derives context-aware assessment strategies, and generates executable validation logic through a multi-agent workflow. To ensure operational reliability, the framework introduces a feasibility validation stage that evaluates the realism and executability of generated assessment specifications before execution, enabling iterative refinement when necessary. Accepted validation logic is executed deterministically to guarantee reproducible and auditable results.
  We implement the proposed framework as an end-to-end prototype and evaluate it across multiple usage scenarios applied to the same dataset. The results demonstrate that assessment outcomes adapt meaningfully to different intended uses, while feasibility-gated execution reduces unrealistic or non-executable rule generation. The proposed approach provides a practical foundation for deploying autonomous yet controlled data quality assessment in modern data-driven environments.

</details>


### [213] [A Multimodal Machine Learning Framework for Enterprise Database Workload-Aware Root Cause Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13699)
*Ruchi Pakhle,Siddhant Pawar*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出一种利用多模态遥测数据和工作负载感知特征的机器学习框架，旨在解决传统数据库故障分析中上下文信息缺失及依赖人工的问题，实验证实该方法能有效提升根因分析的自动化水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业级数据库故障的根因分析通常依赖人工，效率低下，且现有方法多局限于单一来源的证据，难以捕捉如CPU饱和、I/O瓶颈、锁争用等复杂故障背后的全局操作上下文。

Method: 提出了一种多模态机器学习框架。通过工程化构建“工作负载感知”特征，结合计算、存储和加速器相关的遥测数据与操作信号，评估了随机森林、LightGBM和前馈神经网络模型。

Result: 实验表明，工作负载感知特征工程显著提升了模型对工作负载的可分性，LightGBM在分类任务中表现最优，证明了多模态数据与工作负载结合的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过整合多模态遥测数据与工作负载上下文，构建工作负载感知特征是实现自动化、可解释数据库根因分析的有效路径。其中，LightGBM在预测性能与可解释性之间表现出最佳平衡。

Abstract: Root cause analysis for enterprise database incidents is often a manual and time consuming process that requires operators to inspect logs, performance metrics, and workload behavior. Existing approaches commonly focus on a single source of evidence, which limits their ability to capture the broader operational context behind incidents such as CPU saturation, I/O bottlenecks, lock contention, deadlocks, and slow query execution.
  This paper presents a multimodal machine learning framework for workload-aware root cause analysis in enterprise database environments. The proposed approach combines workload characteristics, system telemetry, and operational signals from compute, storage, and accelerator oriented datasets. Engineered workload aware features are used to classify workload behavior and support downstream diagnosis of likely incident causes.
  The framework evaluates Random Forest, LightGBM, and feedforward neural network models for workload classification and root cause analysis support. Experimental results show that workload aware feature engineering improves workload separability, with LightGBM providing the strongest balance of predictive performance and interpretability. The results suggest that combining multimodal telemetry with workload context can provide a practical foundation for automated and explainable root cause analysis systems.

</details>


### [214] [Towards an open registry of Earth observation instruments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13923)
*David Montero,César Aybar,Miguel D. Mahecha,Luis Gómez-Chova*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出了“Awesome Earth Observation Instruments”，一个开放、标准化的地球观测（EO）仪器元数据注册中心，旨在解决行业内缺乏集中化、机器可读的仪器特性描述的问题，以改善EO数据的发现与分析效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据目录大多侧重于描述数据产品，缺乏针对产生这些数据的仪器本身的持续、精选的元数据描述，导致用户难以获取仪器特性信息，影响了EO数据的分析和利用。

Method: 构建了一个基于GitHub托管的、开放且可扩展的仪器元数据注册库；设计了一套结合“轻量级核心+模块化扩展”的标准化元数据架构；实施了自动化模式验证与人工审核相结合的质量控制流程。

Result: 提供了一个包含机器可读元数据的开放式目录，支持光谱、几何及数据访问等维度的标准化描述，并通过版本控制和可扩展架构确保了系统的持续进化与互操作性。

Conclusion: 通过建立“Awesome Earth Observation Instruments”这一开源、标准化、社区驱动的注册库，填补了现有地球观测（EO）领域缺乏统一仪器元数据描述的空白，促进了EO数据的有效发现、解释和分析。

Abstract: Earth observation (EO) is essential to understanding the Earth system, enabling the transformation of planetary properties into measurable variables that can be analysed, compared, and modelled. In recent decades, EO capabilities have grown rapidly, accompanied by an even faster expansion in the number and variety of available EO instruments. Today, EO includes instruments deployed on satellites, airborne platforms, and terrestrial or in-situ systems. However, despite this proliferation of instruments, users often lack a single, reliable source describing their existence and key characteristics. Although existing data catalogues have substantially improved dataset discovery, they primarily describe data products rather than providing persistent, curated metadata about the instruments that produced them. Here we present Awesome Earth Observation Instruments, an open, standardized, and community-oriented registry providing machine-readable metadata for EO instruments. The catalogue is hosted on GitHub and allows contributors to submit instrument metadata following a common schema. The schema combines a lightweight core with modular extensions covering spectral, geometric, and data access-related metadata, enabling both standardization and flexibility across diverse EO systems. All submissions undergo automated schema validation and human review. Because the schema is open, versioned, and extensible, the catalogue can continuously evolve as new instruments and metadata requirements emerge. This facilitates the discovery, interpretation, and analysis of EO data in light of instrument characteristics. To support programmatic access and interoperability, we further envisage an API for integration within common EO analysis environments. The catalogue is openly available at https://github.com/awesome-spectral-indices/awesome-earth-observation-instruments.

</details>


### [215] [Vivace: Exact Temporal OLAP over Interval Histories via Independent Serverless Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14069)
*Woohyeok Park,Taeyoon Kim,Hyunjoon Kim,Kungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Vivace 是一个在无服务器环境下运行精确时态 OLAP 的系统，通过预处理数据分区和查询后的特定规则合并，解决了分布式计算带来的数据缺失与聚合难题，实现了大幅度的降本增效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统时态 OLAP 常用持续运行的集群，成本过高；而直接将计算拆分至无服务器（Serverless）函数会导致数据上下文缺失和聚合错误，现有 SQL 引擎未能妥善处理这些挑战。

Method: 1. 预查询布局（Pre-query Layout）：对时间间隔历史数据进行分区及边界区间复制，确保每个函数能独立计算其时间范围；2. 查询时合并（Merge Step）：根据算子特性（结合律聚合或排名重排序）对函数输出的中间结果进行最终合并。

Result: 在 AWS Lambda 上测试显示，相比传统的查询直接历史数据的方法，Vivace 可将延迟降低高达 82%，成本降低高达 84%。

Conclusion: Vivace 通过预查询布局与运行时合并两阶段处理，证明了在无服务器环境下实现精确的时态 OLAP 是可行且高效的，验证了其在降低延迟和成本方面的显著优势。

Abstract: Temporal online analytical processing (OLAP) analyzes past states of data whose values change over time. Such histories are naturally stored as interval histories, in which each row records the period during which a value remained valid. Because temporal analyses typically arrive in infrequent, intermittent bursts, serverless execution that launches functions only at query time offers a cost advantage over always-on clusters. Splitting a computation that a single process performs as a whole across independent serverless functions, however, breaks correctness in two ways. A function may not receive the rows that determine the state of its time range, and naively summing partial results yields incorrect answers for duration-weighted and cumulative-threshold queries. Existing SQL engines and serverless analytics do not address both problems together.
  This paper presents Vivace, a serverless system for exact temporal OLAP over interval histories. Vivace resolves the two problems in separate stages. Before any query arrives, a pre-query layout step partitions the interval history, replicating boundary-crossing intervals so each function computes its range completely from a single file. At query time, a merge step combines partial results under operator-specific rules. Associative aggregates merge intermediate values, and ranking re-orders candidates within each time range. We prove that this partitioned execution matches single-process computation up to canonical form. Evaluated on AWS Lambda with real-world datasets, Vivace reduces latency and monetary cost by up to 82% and 84%, respectively, against an equivalent SQL baseline that queries the history directly, demonstrating robust generality and efficiency.

</details>


### [216] [Revisiting Filtered ANN Benchmarks: A Hardness-Controlled Benchmark Generator for Realistic Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14193)
*Mintaek Lim,Dogeun Kim,Minwoo Kim,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现有 FANN 搜索基准测试真实性不足的问题，本文提出了基于执行难度度量的 α-Hardness 评估指标及对应的生成器 HCBGen，通过精准控制查询难度，揭示了传统基准的局限性并实现了更科学的性能评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的过滤近似最近邻（FANN）搜索基准测试主要依赖临时构建，无法真实反映系统在现实混合负载下的执行瓶颈（如过滤失败导致的过提取与延迟波动），导致性能评估存在脆弱性和片面性。

Method: 1. 提出 α-Hardness：一种基于执行链（过提取因子 over-fetch factor）的查询级硬度度量模型。
2. 提出 HCBGen：一种基于α-Hardness作为控制信号的硬度可控工作负载生成器。
3. 对比分析：将提出的度量标准与传统查询选择性（selectivity）等指标在不同数据集和混合策略下进行性能对齐评估。

Result: 1. α-Hardness 在多种数据集和策略中展现出与经验性能的强单调一致性，优于传统的选择性或属性-向量相关性指标。
2. 实验发现现有基准测试仅覆盖了硬度谱中较简易的部分，掩盖了系统在硬查询下的鲁棒性缺陷。
3. 证明了通过匹配硬度分布，可以生成能够精准复现性能趋势的隐私保护型代理负载。

Conclusion: 本文提出的 α-Hardness 度量标准和 HCBGen 生成器能够有效量化与模拟 FANN 搜索的性能特征。研究表明，现有基准测试往往过于简单，无法反映系统在复杂查询下的真实鲁棒性；通过匹配硬度分布，研究者可在保护隐私的同时生成具有代表性的代理负载。

Abstract: Filtered approximate nearest neighbor (FANN) search must satisfy both vector similarity and structured predicates, yet evaluations remain brittle because real hybrid workloads are rarely shareable and existing benchmarks rely on ad-hoc synthetic or semi-real constructions. We argue that realism hinges on execution-driven query difficulty: failures in early filtering trigger over-fetching of additional candidates, shaping latency, throughput, and recall. Building on this insight, we propose $α$-Hardness, a query-level hardness metric that models the conditional execution chain via the over-fetch factor and extends naturally to strategy-conditioned settings. Across diverse datasets and hybrid strategies, $α$-Hardness exhibits strong monotonic alignment with empirical performance, while common proxies such as selectivity or attribute-vector correlation are frequently unstable or strategy-inconsistent. We further introduce HCBGen, a hardness-controlled benchmark generator that uses $α$-Hardness as an explicit control signal to synthesize workloads under coarse bias modes or to match a target hardness profile. Our experiments show that widely used benchmarks occupy a narrow, relatively easy portion of the hardness spectrum, masking robustness gaps that emerge under harder queries. Finally, we demonstrate that matching hardness distributions enables privacy-preserving proxy workloads that closely reproduce performance trends, bridging research benchmarks and real evaluation.

</details>


### [217] [TACO: A Benchmark for Open-Domain Text-to-SQL with Ambiguous and Cross-Database Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14201)
*Chao Deng,Ju Fan,Yuyu Luo,Qinliang Xue,Meihao Fan,Yuxin Zhang,Min Zhang,Xiaofeng Jia,Jing Zhang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 文章提出了 TACO，一个面向含糊查询与跨数据库操作的开放域 Text-to-SQL 基准测试，旨在解决现有基准在真实场景下的局限性，并证实了当前方法在该任务上的巨大提升空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Text-to-SQL 基准主要集中在封闭域、固定模式和明确问题，难以应对现实世界中模糊问题、未指定数据库及跨数据库查询的复杂挑战。

Method: 1. 构建数据集：涵盖真实世界智能城市数据（1,500例）和大规模合成数据（13,000例，跨多领域）；2. 开发数据合成流水线以保持查询复杂度；3. 提出 TACO-SQL 基准模型（包含问题重写、表链接及查询规划步骤）。

Result: TACO-SQL 基准方法表现最优，但与人类编写的 SQL 之间仍存在巨大性能差距，验证了任务的难度与基准的有效性。

Conclusion: TACO 揭示了现有 Text-to-SQL 方法在处理开放域、模糊查询及跨数据库任务时仍存在显著的性能差距，为相关领域的研究提供了具有挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Text-to-SQL aims to translate natural language questions into executable SQL queries over structured databases. Existing benchmarks mainly focus on closed-domain settings with predefined database schemas and well-specified questions, but they fall short in addressing the challenges of open-domain scenarios, such as ambiguous questions, unspecified databases, and cross-database querying. To bridge this gap, we introduce TACO, a benchmark for open-domain Text-to-SQL with Ambiguous and Cross-database queries. TACO consists of 1,500 real-world Text-to-SQL examples based on a smart city data service and 13,000 high-quality synthetic examples generated based on large-scale open data portals, covering diverse domains such as transportation, healthcare, and finance. To construct the synthetic examples, we develop an effective data synthesis pipeline that preserves the complexity of real-world queries. To demonstrate the utility of TACO, we introduce a baseline TACO-SQL composed of question rewriting, table linking, and query planning, to illustrate the challenges posed by TACO and to better understand the limitations of existing Text-to-SQL approaches. Extensive experiments on TACO using a variety of recent Text-to-SQL approaches show that, while TACO-SQL achieves the best results, a significant gap still remains between the existing approaches and human-written SQL. These findings highlight the difficulty of open-domain Text-to-SQL and position TACO as a valuable benchmark to drive future research.

</details>


### [218] [WikiKV: Schema-Evolving Path-Indexed Storage for Hierarchical Knowledge Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14275)
*Feifei Li,Haoliang Ming,Zihan Li,Hang Liao,Xingyu Fan,Xiaoqing Wu,Chenggong Wang,Wenhui Que*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: WikiKV是一个为LLM层级化知识库量身定制的路径索引存储模型，通过优化的存储架构和导航算法，解决了传统数据库在处理动态树状知识库时的性能短板，显著提升了检索效率和问答准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的存储系统（如关系型数据库、图数据库、文件系统）未能原生适配LLM生成的层级化知识库（树状Wiki）的特性，即：层级结构、高查询吞吐量以及知识库的持续演进性。

Method: 提出了WikiKV存储模型，包含三个核心组件：(1) 基于“意图锚定模式归纳”和“持续演进算子”的数据驱动模式；(2) 一种无需读路径锁的路径索引一致性协议，防止并发重写时的读取错误；(3) 预算导航算子（budgeted navigation operator），通过搜索加速路由将LLM下降步数从O(d)降低至O(1)，同时支持渐进式回答。

Result: 在WeChat AI Assistant的实际部署及AuthTrace数据集测试中，WikiKV在四种查询算子下均实现了均衡的低延迟，端到端答案正确率达到63.2%，在处理多文档查询时显著优于现有的RAG检索基线。

Conclusion: WikiKV 是一种针对LLM构建的层级化知识库优化的新型键值存储模型。通过路径索引设计、专用的数据模式和高效的导航算子，它有效解决了现有存储架构在处理动态、层级化且频繁查询的LLM知识库时的性能瓶颈，并在实际应用中展现了优于传统存储方案的检索精度与响应速度。

Abstract: LLM-curated hierarchical knowledge bases, namely a tree-structured wiki whose nodes summarize an underlying corpus, have become a dominant substrate for retrieval-augmented applications, yet their storage layer is still treated as an implementation detail. This workload is hierarchical, query-intensive, and continuously evolving, and no existing storage model natively captures all three properties at once. We present WikiKV, a path-indexed key-value storage model purpose-built for this workload, comprising three components: (i) a data-driven schema that bootstraps the hierarchy via Intent-Anchored Schema Induction and refines it through Continuous Evolution Operators; (ii) a consistency protocol for the path-indexed storage model that precludes partial-read observations under concurrent offline rewrites without read-path locking; and (iii) a budgeted navigation operator whose search-accelerated routing reduces the expected number of LLM-assisted descent steps from d to O(1) while preserving anytime semantics with progressively refined answers. We evaluate WikiKV through real-world deployment for the WeChat Official Account AI Assistant and benchmark it against diverse baselines on the AuthTrace dataset, where it achieves balanced low per-operator latency across four query operators against relational, graph, and FS backends, and reaches 63.2% end-to-end answer correctness, exceeding multiple RAG baselines, with the gap widening on low- and high-fan-in multi-document questions. Ablation study further confirms the effectiveness of WikiKV's components.

</details>


### [219] [Transforming Shape Schemas with Composable Property-Graph Queries (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14309)
*Philipp Seifer,Daniel Hernández,Ralf Lämmel,Steffen Staab*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对属性图转换后的模式推断问题，通过构建RDF、SHACL及SPARQL的映射层，利用描述逻辑推理机解决了属性图复杂结构在模式推断中的难题，实现了无实例数据的自动化模式预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 属性图作为重要的数据模型，需要模式约束来规范数据；然而，现有研究缺乏对属性图转换过程（查询操作）后输出模式的自动化推断机制，且传统描述逻辑难以直接处理属性图特有的标签、属性及边等复杂结构。

Method: 提出了一种包含三层映射的推理框架：1. 将属性图重构（Reify）为RDF模型；2. 将ProGS模式映射为SHACL；3. 将G-CORE查询映射为SPARQL CONSTRUCT查询。利用高效的描述逻辑（DL）推理机分析转换后的查询与模式，从而推断输出模式。

Result: 实现了一种无需通过实例数据、仅依据输入模式和查询语句即可推断输出模式的自动推理程序，且该方法具备理论上的完备性和可靠性。

Conclusion: 该研究通过建立多层映射框架，成功实现了属性图查询的模式推断，并在理论上证明了推断约束的可靠性及映射的语义等价性，为复杂属性图系统的数据治理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Property graphs may be constrained by schemas that inform both query engines and human users about the shape of valid data, enforcing a contract between data provider and consumer. Composable property-graph queries transform input graphs into output graphs. Then, the question arises of which schema can be expected after one (or several) transformation steps. We investigate how schema constraints can be inferred given an input schema and a transforming query. Specifically, we propose a reasoning procedure that, given an input schema in ProGS and a query in G-CORE infers an output schema. Since graph updates will happen frequently, our inference procedure does not rely on graph instances, such that the computed output schema applies to all graphs originating from any input graph complying with the input schema. Related work has addressed this problem for SPARQL CONSTRUCT queries, encoding it in Description Logics (DLs) so that the output schema is entailed by axioms inferred from input schema and queries. Property graphs and their queries, however, complicate the matter, as property graphs feature label and property annotations as well as first-class edges. Thus, reification has to be used in one way or another, though available DLs lack the means to encode such features directly. We approach this novel challenge via a family of mappings for i) property graphs reified in RDF, aligned with ii) a mapping from ProGS to SHACL and iii) a mapping from G-CORE to SPARQL CONSTRUCT queries. In this manner, schema inference for property graphs becomes manageable, as we break apart the problem through the extra mapping layer and utilize efficient DL reasoners. We develop the metatheory regarding the soundness of inferred schema constraints and the semantic equivalence of mapped schemas and queries.

</details>


### [220] [PLRTune: Importance Pre-Sampling and LLM-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Automatic Database Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14312)
*Xinyue Yang,Chen Zheng,Yaoyang Hou,Renhao Zhang,Yinyan Zhang,Heng Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PLRTune 是一个通过缩减搜索空间、优化提示质量并结合 TD3 强化学习的数据库调优系统，显著提升了调优质量与运行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数据库自动调优方案在处理高维参数空间时效率低下，且现有的基于 LLM 或文档的提示（Hints）往往不可靠，手动初始化后缺乏进一步挖掘性能优化的手段。

Method: 提出了一种分阶段调优系统 PLRTune，包含三个核心组件：1. 基于重要性预采样和重排序的参数空间缩减策略（识别关键参数集）；2. 基于执行引导的提示优化技术（提升初始化建议的质量）；3. 基于 TD3（Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient）的后续调优阶段（在关键子空间内进一步深挖优化空间）。

Result: 在 MySQL 和 PostgreSQL 上，PLRTune 相比最先进的基准方法，平均性能提升 9.50%，达到最优基准性能的速度平均快 9.03 倍。

Conclusion: PLRTune 通过分阶段的策略有效地解决了自动数据库调优中搜索空间高维、在线调优成本高以及现有调优建议不可靠的问题，实现了性能提升与优化效率的平衡。

Abstract: Configuration tuning is critical to database performance, yet automatic database tuning remains challenging due to high-dimensional knob spaces, substantial online tuning cost, unreliable textual hints derived from Large Language Models (LLMs) or community documents, and the difficulty of exploiting the remaining optimization room after initialization.
  Hence, we propose PLRTune, a staged database tuning system that leverages workload-specific domain knowledge to identify a reduced search space and further optimize within this promising region.
