First, we develop an importance pre-sampling and reranking strategy to identify the dominant knob subset in a workload-specific manner and derive a compact state representation. Second, we design an execution-guided hint refinement technique to improve the initialization quality of documentation-guided tuning. Finally, we propose a post-tuning refinement stage that leverages Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) to explore the dominant knob subset and further exploit the remaining optimization room.
  We evaluate PLRTune on MySQL and PostgreSQL across diverse benchmark workloads. Compared with state-of-the-art approaches, PLRTune achieves the best final result on all tested workloads, improving over the corresponding best-performing alternative by 9.50% on average. Moreover, PLRTune reaches the strongest baseline's best performance level 9.03 times faster on average across workloads, demonstrating its practical runtime efficiency without sacrificing final tuning quality.

</details>


### [221] [Evaluating and Generating Query Workloads for High Dimensional Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14511)
*Matteo Ceccarello,Alexandra Levchenko,Ioana Ileana,Themis Palpanas*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 该论文指出了现有相似度搜索基准测试忽视工作负载难度的问题，并提出了 HephAnn 和 HephGrad 两种工具，支持生成用户指定难度的查询负载，以更全面地评估算法性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的相似度搜索基准测试通常缺乏对查询工作负载难度的有效控制，导致评估结果可能无法准确反映索引结构在处理不同难度任务时的性能表现。

Method: 1. 评估现有的查询难度度量指标，以确定其衡量索引结构计算开销（经验难度）的能力；2. 提出两种工作负载合成方法：HephAnn（基于近似方法，假设较少）和 HephGrad（基于梯度下降，速度更快），用于生成符合用户指定难度目标的查询集。

Result: 成功实现了对查询难度的可控生成；HephGrad 在合成速度上表现更好，而 HephAnn 在处理不同难度度量指标的选择上更加灵活。生成的任务负载能有效揭示相似度搜索算法的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过引入 HephAnn 和 HephGrad 两种工作负载合成算法，研究人员能够针对特定难度目标生成查询集，从而更深入地理解并评估相似度搜索算法在不同场景下的表现，解决了现有基准测试缺乏难度控制的问题。

Abstract: Similarity search lies at the heart of many modern applications, ranging from databases to deep learning to data series analysis. As such, a vast effort has been invested in developing algorithms, data structures and implementations to speed up this crucial subroutine. To empirically validate these approaches, several benchmarking efforts have been initiated covering a wide array of datasets. In this paper, we observe that usually little control is exercised on the hardness of the workloads with which methods are tested and compared. To address this issue, we first evaluate several query hardness measures with respect to their ability to capture the empirical hardness of a query, i.e. the effort invested by an index data structure to provide an answer. Then, we propose two methods, deemed \HephAnn and \HephGrad, for synthesizing query workloads so that they meet a user-specified hardness target. Both methods allow to produce workloads with the desired hardness: we find that \HephGrad is faster, while \HephAnn makes fewer assumptions on the target hardness measure. The resulting workloads can be used to gain insights into the behavior of similarity search algorithms.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [222] [Lattice Aggregation in Distributed Verification under Crash and Byzantine Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13954)
*Gilde Valeria Rodríguez,Borzoo Bonakdarpour,Armando Castañeda,Sergio Rajsbaum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了 c-Lattice Aggregation 系统性框架，解决了分布式环境下在崩溃和拜占庭故障中的执行重构问题，给出了严谨的可行性阈值，并证明了其在验证全局依赖分布式协议中的关键基础作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异步分布式系统中，面临崩溃或拜占庭故障时，如何确保多个监督节点能够协作重构出一致且完整的并发执行历史。现有研究（如 Lattice Agreement）在处理复杂容错验证场景时存在局限性，需要一种更稳健的理论表征来实现监控系统的可靠性。

Method: 1. 形式化定义 c-Lattice Aggregation 问题，并将其作为分布式验证的核心原语。
2. 引入冗余参数 x 对输入进行参数化建模。
3. 利用 SCD-broadcast 技术构建匹配协议，并通过基于不可区分性（indistinguishability）的理论分析证明下界。
4. 定义“全局依赖语言”类，并将此类协议的可靠性与 c-Lattice Aggregation 的可解性建立等价关系。

Result: 1. 在崩溃故障下，Lattice Aggregation 的可行性阈值为 x ≥ t + 1。
2. 在拜占庭故障下（t < n/3），c-Lattice Aggregation 的可行性阈值为 x ≥ 2t + c。
3. 证明了对于共识、线性一致性等“全局依赖语言”，其监控系统的可靠性与 c-Lattice Aggregation 的可解性完全等价。

Conclusion: 本文通过 c-Lattice Aggregation 这一原语，建立了分布式系统中故障容忍验证的完整理论框架。证明了该问题是实现各种分布式监控协议（如共识、线性一致性等）的必要且充分条件，并给出了针对崩溃和拜占庭故障的精确可行性界限。

Abstract: We introduce c-Lattice Aggregation, a fault-tolerant reconstruction problem for distributed verification under crash and Byzantine failures. In our setting, n asynchronous processes supervise a concurrent execution I: each process holds a local sample, and must collaboratively reconstruct I from partial, potentially overlapping observations. A protocol solves c-Lattice Aggregation if at least c correct processes output the complete execution I, while all correct outputs are comparable and bounded by I. This strengthens Lattice Agreement [Attiya, Herlihy and Rachman, 1995] and Byzantine Lattice Agreement [Di Luna et al., 2020; Zheng and Garg, 2020].
  We parameterize inputs by a redundancy parameter x -- every element of I appears in at least x initial samples -- and establish tight feasibility thresholds. Under crash failures with at most t faulty processes, Lattice Aggregation is solvable if and only if x >= t + 1. Under Byzantine failures with t < n/3, c-Lattice Aggregation is solvable if and only if x >= 2t + c. All bounds are tight: we present matching algorithms based on SCD-broadcast [Imbs et al., 2018; Khanchandani and Wattenhofer, 2024] and indistinguishability-based lower bounds.
  Finally, we define globally dependent languages -- those for which no partial view can certify correctness, including consensus, linearizability, k-set agreement, and leader election -- and prove that soundness of any monitoring system is achievable if and only if c-Lattice Aggregation is solved, yielding the first complete characterization of fault-tolerant verification under Byzantine failures.

</details>


### [223] [STREAM: Multi-Tier LLM Inference Middleware with Dual-Channel HPC Token Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13968)
*Anas Nassar,Steve Mohr,Leonard Apanasevich,Himanshu Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: STREAM是一款智能模型路由系统，旨在整合本地端、机构HPC集群与商业云API。它通过创新的双通道中继机制解决了HPC内网穿透与延迟问题，不仅实现了隐私保护下的资源动态统筹，还通过OpenAI兼容接口极大降低了高性能计算的使用门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的LLM生态碎片化严重：本地设备算力有限，机构HPC环境交互性差（多为批处理任务且防火墙阻隔），商业云API存在隐私与高昂成本问题。现有系统无法统筹利用这三种资源。

Method: 1. 三层路由架构：利用本地LLM判断任务复杂度，动态分配至本地、HPC或云端。2. 双通道HPC流媒体架构：分离控制平面（Globus Compute）与数据平面（WebSocket中继），结合AES-256-GCM加密，绕过防火墙限制并实现低延迟。3. 上下文层级摘要：防止长会话无效占用昂贵资源。4. HPC-as-API代理：提供OpenAI兼容接口，简化HPC访问。

Result: 在1,200个查询的基准测试中，Llama 3.2 3B实现了85.1%的本地层保留率。性能方面，HPC中继的均值首字延迟（TTFT）仅为0.54秒，较传统批处理模式提升了21.1倍，接近本地推理（0.26秒）效率。

Conclusion: STREAM成功统一了本地、高性能计算(HPC)中心和商业云三种推理来源，在保障隐私与安全的前提下，通过智能路由实现了成本、隐私与推理质量的平衡。HPC-as-API模式极大地降低了HPC计算资源的门槛。

Abstract: Researchers and practitioners working with large language models face a fragmented landscape: local models are free and private but hardware limits the model size and context windows a researcher can use; institutional HPC centers offer powerful GPU resources at no marginal cost and keep data within institutional boundaries, but operate behind firewalls and are designed for batch jobs rather than interactive use; commercial cloud APIs provide frontier-model quality on demand but impose significant cost and data retention policies unsuitable for sensitive research data. No existing system unifies all three. STREAM (Smart Tiered Routing Engine for AI Models) addresses this gap with four contributions: (1) a three-tier routing architecture combining local, HPC, and cloud inference with a local LLM-based complexity judge; (2) a dual-channel HPC streaming architecture that separates the Globus Compute control plane (authentication and job dispatch) from a WebSocket relay data plane (token delivery), enabling sub-second TTFT (0.54 s median, 21.1x over batch mode's 11.40 s) through institutional firewalls without VPN or firewall rule changes, with end-to-end AES-256-GCM encryption ensuring the relay operator cannot read token payloads; (3) tier-aware context summarization that prevents long conversations from forcing simple queries onto expensive tiers; and (4) an HPC-as-API proxy mode that exposes HPC inference as an OpenAI-compatible endpoint callable from any standard client with no HPC expertise, a deployment pattern made practical only by the sub-second TTFT of contribution (2). Llama 3.2 3B achieves 85.1% free-tier retention on a 1,200-query benchmark spanning ten domains. Measured TTFT: 0.26 s local, 0.54 s HPC (relay), 1.68 s cloud.

</details>


### [224] [VaultxGPU: GPU-Accelerated Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14007)
*Samuel Taiwo Fatunmbi,Om Amit Gandhi,Luke Logan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: VaultxGPU通过GPU加速和核函数融合优化，在Proof-of-Space绘图效率上大幅超越现有CPU方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Proof-of-Work机制能耗极高，现有的Proof-of-Space机制中绘图（plotting）过程受到CPU哈希吞吐量的限制，亟需提高绘图性能以降低资源消耗。

Method: 开发了VaultxGPU，将Blake3哈希流水线卸载至GPU；利用CUDA（NVIDIA）和SYCL（AMD/Intel）实现跨平台支持；将Table 1常驻GPU显存，并将排序与匹配阶段融合为单一核函数（kernel），以最小化数据传输开销。

Result: VaultxGPU在K=31的任务中，SYCL实现版本比单线程CPU快59.2倍（45.4秒 vs 2688秒），且表现优于最优的384线程CPU配置。

Conclusion: GPU加速是突破CPU并行计算瓶颈、扩展空间证明（Proof-of-Space）绘图能力的正确路径，能够显著提升绘图速度和效率。

Abstract: Blockchain consensus mechanisms based on Proof-of-Work consume significant energy, with Bitcoin alone estimated at approximately 150 TWh per year. Proof-of-Space reduces this cost by replacing repeated computation with storage, but plot generation remains bottlenecked by CPU hashing throughput. Prior work on VaultX demonstrated a high-performance CPU-based Proof-of-Space plotter using multi-threaded Blake3 hashing, achieving plotting speeds 4 to 50x faster than Chia depending on hardware configuration. In this paper, we present VaultxGPU, a GPU-accelerated extension of the VaultX plotter that offloads the Blake3 hashing pipeline to the GPU using custom kernels. We implement the plotter in both CUDA for NVIDIA hardware and SYCL for AMD and Intel GPUs, keeping Table 1 entirely in GPU VRAM and fusing the sort and match stages into a single kernel to minimize data movement. We evaluate VaultxGPU across K-values 27 through 31 against CPU baselines. Our SYCL GPU implementation achieves a 59.2x speedup over a single-threaded CPU baseline, completing a K=31 plot in 45.4 seconds compared to 2688 seconds, and outperforms even the best 384-thread CPU configuration. These results confirm that GPU acceleration is the correct direction for scaling Proof-of-Space plotting beyond what CPU parallelism can achieve.

</details>


### [225] [Extreme-Scale Atomistic Simulation of Real-Temperature Magnetic Skyrmion Dynamics by Coupled Spin-Lattice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14073)
*Pin Chen,Cheng-bing Chen,Hai Liu,Yuewen Huang,Kangyou Zhong,Hai-Jun Zhao,Liu-Liu Han,Guixin Guo,Jiang Li,Dan Huang,Ben Xu,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 通过将机器学习势函数与高性能计算架构结合，研究者实现了在万亿原子/自旋尺度上对晶格-自旋耦合拓扑磁性的实时物理模拟，实现了性能七个数量级的突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的计算方法难以在接近室温的装置尺度下，精确模拟功能材料中晶格与自旋相互耦合的拓扑磁性动力学过程（以FeGe的螺旋-斯格明子转变为例）。

Method: 提出了一种结合自旋约束密度泛函理论（DFT）训练的神经进化势函数与结构保持自旋-晶格积分器的机器学习框架。通过架构级优化（如内核融合、SVE2向量化、NUMA感知数据布局）实现极高并行效率，并利用LineShine超算系统进行扩展。

Result: 在LineShine超级计算机上实现了1.245亿核心的扩展，弱扩展效率达89.7%，计算规模达到1.34万亿个原子及等量自旋，双精度性能达到48.5 PFLOPS，对比传统方法获得七个数量级的加速，成功解析了真实温度下的斯格明子成核与重组过程。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了一个高性能的机器学习框架，实现了在原子尺度下对宏观尺度拓扑磁性动力学的预测，为研究复杂功能材料中的热驱动磁结构演变开辟了新的计算路径。

Abstract: Real-temperature topological magnetic dynamics in functional materials is governed by coupled lattice and spin evolution, yet remains inaccessible to predictive simulation at device-relevant scales. As a flagship example, thermally driven helix-to-skyrmion transformation in FeGe requires atomistic resolution, explicit lattice motion, and micrometer-scale domains to resolve device-scale topological texture formation. We combine a spin-constrained density-functional-theory-trained neuro-evolution potential with a structure-preserving spin-lattice integrator within one machine-learned framework. Architecture-specific optimizations, kernel fusion, SVE2 vectorization, and NUMA-aware data layout deliver a seven orders-of-magnitude speedup over prior spin-aware methods. Deployed on LineShine exascale supercomputer, the full application scales to 12.45 million CPU cores with 89.7% weak-scaling efficiency, enabling simulations of 1.34 trillion atoms and an equal number of spins while reaching 48.5 PFLOPS in double precision. The simulations directly resolve real-temperature skyrmion nucleation and reorganization at previously inaccessible scales, establishing a new regime for predictive simulation of coupled spin-lattice topological magnetic dynamics.

</details>


### [226] [On the Limits of Causal Observation in Shared-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14093)
*Gilde Valeria Rodríguez,Armando Castañeda,Miguel Piña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了因果可观测性问题（COP），证明了在并发系统观测中无法同时实现完备性和健全性，并证明监测器的性能和行为主要由监测事件的放置位置决定，而非具体的一致性实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在并发系统中，确定操作的先后顺序是推理正确性的基础，但由于观测上的局限性，如何精确地在边界上捕获操作真实的时间顺序存在挑战。

Method: 1. 定义了因果可观测性问题（COP）；2. 从理论上证明了在观测边界上无法同时实现强一致性（同时满足完备性和健全性）；3. 提出基于监测事件放置位置（内部 vs 外部）的权衡框架；4. 设计并实现了三种因果监测器（FAInc、Striped、Collect）；5. 在64核NUMA架构上进行实验验证。

Result: 1. 证明了监测事件放置位置决定了COP的特性：内部放置（internal）保证完备性，外部放置（external）保证健全性；2. 实验表明，Striped实现方案在维持线性一致性的前提下，相比FAInc显著提升了高并发下的吞吐量。

Conclusion: 因果可观测性（COP）中存在基本权衡：监测器的完备性（ completeness）与健全性（soundness）不可兼得。监测事件相对于操作边界的放置位置决定了监测器的保证，与底层时间戳实现机制或一致性级别无关。通过合理的分散式实现（如Striped），可以在保证线性一致性的同时实现高性能。

Abstract: Determining whether one concurrent operation completed before another began is a fundamental prerequisite for reasoning about the correctness of concurrent systems. We formalize this challenge as the Causal Observability Problem (COP): assign timestamps to the observable boundary events of a concurrent execution, invocations and responses, that faithfully reflect real-time operation order. A solution is complete if it never misses a genuine precedence, and sound if it never reports a spurious one. We prove that a strongly consistent solution, one that is simultaneously complete and sound, is unachievable at the observable boundary. We then show that the placement of instrumentation events relative to operation boundaries deterministically governs what a monitor can guarantee: internal placement yields completeness, external placement yields soundness, and neither achieves both. This dichotomy holds independently of the underlying timestamp mechanism. We instantiate this framework with three non-blocking implementations of a Causal Monitor object: FAInc (centralized atomic counter), Striped (decentralized counter), and Collect (iterative register snapshot). FAInc and Striped are linearizable; Collect is only quiescently consistent. Despite this internal consistency gap, we prove that all three provide identical COP guarantees: placement alone determines observable behavior. We validate these claims empirically on a 64-core NUMA architecture, showing that Striped matches Collect in throughput while preserving linearizability, resolving the cache-contention bottleneck of FAInc at high thread counts.

</details>


### [227] [Design Methodology and Performance Trade-offs Management for Distributed and Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14350)
*Milos Gravara,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了复合AI系统（Compound AI Systems）设计方法，通过编排多个模型和算法，在满足服务级别目标（SLO）的同时，显著优化了AI系统的延迟与成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的“以模型为中心”的设计模式（即单一、固定模型）无法满足多样化输入带来的不同需求，导致性能折损且成本高昂，迫切需要向“以系统为中心”的复合AI架构转型。

Method: 建立了一套两维度的系统设计方法论（工作流拓扑+配置选择），并归纳出八种针对单一模型缺陷的设计模式；通过三个案例研究验证了该方法在处理复杂逻辑和提升运行效率方面的有效性。

Result: 相较于单一模型，复合AI配置在保持高准确率（差距缩小至2.5-4个百分点以内）的同时，延迟降低最高可达60%，成本降低最高可达71%，并指出了迈向自动化部署的五大开放挑战。

Conclusion: 复合AI系统（Compound AI systems）设计方法能够显著提升系统性能并降低成本；该领域已从单一模型设计转向系统级编排，未来研究重点应在于实现自动化配置以适应多变的运行环境。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems must typically satisfy service-level objectives including accuracy, latency, and cost. The prevailing model-centric approaches select a monolithic model at design time and apply identical computation regardless of input difficulty, cannot decompose tasks across specialized components, and have knowledge that is fixed at training time. During runtime, this can lead to performance degradation and increasing costs. Because the model is the main design variable, it determines the majority of system behavior, coupling operational objectives to a single design-time choice. Addressing these limitations requires shifting from model-centric to system-centric design. Compound AI systems realize this shift by orchestrating multiple models, algorithms, and tools as distributed AI systems through explicit control logic. The performance of such systems depends on their workflow topology, the models assigned to each task, and the parameters governing runtime behavior. We present a design methodology that organizes this space along two dimensions, workflow topology and configuration selection, and identifies eight design patterns, each consolidating techniques to address a specific limitation of monolithic deployment. We validate our methodology through three case studies. Across our case studies, Compound AI configurations approach accuracy of monolithic models within 2.5 to 4 percentage points while reducing latency by up to 60% and cost by up to 71%. We show that model selection and parameter configuration jointly determine system performance, but the resulting design space grows combinatorially, as workflows compose more patterns and components. Thus, we identify five open challenges that define a roadmap from manually configured prototypes towards systems that automatically discover and maintain SLO-compliance in Compound and Distributed AI systems.

</details>


### [228] [PLAIground: SLO-Driven Runtime Model Selection for Compound AI Systems in the Edge-Cloud-Space Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14356)
*Milos Gravara,Cynthia Marcelino,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PLAIground 是一个旨在 3D 计算连续体中实现复合 AI 系统运行时模型选择的框架，通过 CAIM 抽象和 Pixie 调度算法，在保障严格 SLO 的同时动态优化 AI 任务的准确性与资源成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在边缘、云和空间组成的 3D 计算连续体中，复合 AI 系统面临严格的准确性、延迟和成本（SLO）限制。现有的分布式 AI 框架缺乏对运行时动态模型切换的原生支持，导致在面对多变任务负载时无法平衡性能与指标要求。

Method: 1. 提出 CAIM (Compoundable AI Model) 抽象层，通过任务与数据契约解耦任务语义与模型实现；2. 设计 Pixie 算法，这是一种基于 SLO 的运行时模型选择策略，能够根据实时运行情况动态调度最优模型。

Result: 在两个真实场景的复合 AI 工作流测试中，Pixie 算法在维持 SLO 合规的前提下实现了高达 91.3% 的准确率；相比之下，固定模型策略要么违反成本和延迟约束（高达 21 倍），要么在准确率上出现显著下滑（4%）。

Conclusion: PLAIground 通过 CAIM 抽象和 Pixie 算法有效解决了复合 AI 系统在 3D 计算连续体中实现实时模型选择的难题，在满足严格 SLO 的前提下，显著优于传统的固定模型策略，验证了其在工业级复合 AI 系统中的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: Applications in the 3D Computing Continuum, which unifies edge, cloud, and space, require combining multiple AI tasks such as object detection, time-series analytics, and natural language processing into Compound AI systems. These systems must satisfy stringent Service Level Objectives (SLOs) on accuracy, latency, and cost. A key mechanism for maintaining SLO compliance of Compound AI systems is runtime model selection, where AI models are dynamically switched for each workflow task. However, existing distributed and compound AI frameworks do not natively support runtime model selection. We present PLAIground, a framework that enables runtime model selection for Compound AI systems. PLAIground introduces Compoundable AI Model (CAIM) abstraction, which decouples task semantics from AI model implementations via Task and Data Contracts, enabling model switching without workflow changes. Additionally, PLAIground introduces Pixie, an SLO-driven runtime model selection algorithm, which dynamically selects the most suitable model for each task during execution. Our evaluation on two realistic Compound AI workflows demonstrates that Pixie achieves up to 91.3% accuracy while maintaining SLO compliance where fixed-model strategies either violate cost and latency budgets up to 21x or miss accuracy targets by 4%.

</details>


### [229] [Simulation-Based Performance Evaluation of Sharded Blockchain Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14394)
*Om Amit Gandhi,Ioan Raicu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文开发了一款基于SimPy的区块链分片模拟器，研究表明分片可显著提高吞吐量，但受限于跨分片通信与网络开销，存在性能扩展的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共区块链受限于区块大小和间隔，无法简单通过参数调整提升吞吐量；为了解决可扩展性瓶颈，研究者提出通过分片技术并行处理交易，但缺乏评估不同分片方案性能的标准化分析工具。

Method: 基于SimPy构建了一个可配置的离散事件模拟器，对不同分片架构下的挖矿、验证、跨分片协调、区块传播等过程进行建模。

Result: 在本地数据中心环境下实现了160万TPS，在全局WAN环境下实现了60万TPS，验证了分片架构在高并发场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 分片技术能够显著提升区块链吞吐量，但存在扩展瓶颈。随着分片数量增加，协调开销、同步成本和网络延迟最终会抵消并行执行带来的增益，导致性能边际递减。

Abstract: Public blockchains continue to struggle with scalability because improving throughput is not as simple as increasing block size or reducing block interval. Larger blocks increase validation and transmission cost, while shorter intervals raise the likelihood of propagation delays, forks, and stale blocks. These limits motivate sharding, where transaction processing is divided across multiple parallel shard groups. In this work, we present a configurable SimPy-based discrete-event simulator for evaluating sharded blockchain architectures under controlled workload and network assumptions. The simulator models mining, verification, inter-shard coordination, block dissemination, measured throughput, average block time, and communication overhead. Our simulator achieves 1.6M TPS at 256 shards under a local datacenter-like setup and 0.6M TPS in a global WAN setup, showing strong throughput gains from parallel execution. However, the gains are not unbounded: beyond a certain number of shards, coordination traffic, synchronization, and network overhead begin to dominate, leading to diminishing returns.

</details>


### [230] [Simple-IT: Practical Low-Latency Signature-Free BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14404)
*Qianyu Yu,Juan Villacis,Giuliano Losa,Zhuolun Xiang,Xuechao Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 面对量子计算对现有 BFT 共识协议的威胁，本文提出了 Simple-IT——一个高效、简洁且抗量子的无签名 BFT 共识协议，成功在保证实践可行性的前提下，实现了与主流协议相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的 BFT 共识协议严重依赖抗量子攻击能力较弱的公钥签名方案（如 Ed25519、BLS12-381）。虽然可以使用抗量子签名方案，但成本高昂。研究旨在探讨如何在不使用签名的情况下，实现与现有高性能、量子脆弱协议相媲美的共识性能（特别是延迟方面）。

Method: 提出了一种基于领导者（leader-based）的无签名 BFT 共识协议 Simple-IT。该协议在理论上实现了 4 个消息延迟（最优路径下仅需 3 个），并支持推测流水线（speculative pipelining）等实践优化。

Result: 实验证明 Simple-IT 在地理分布的测试环境中，其吞吐量和延迟均与当前的先进量子脆弱协议具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Simple-IT 协议证明了无签名（signature-free）的 BFT 共识协议在保持低延迟和高吞吐量的同时，能够达到与当前量子脆弱协议相当的性能，且具有更好的实践可行性。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing pose a looming threat to most current Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, which rely on quantum-vulnerable public-key signature schemes such as Ed25519 and BLS12-381. Instead of switching to much more expensive post-quantum secure signature schemes, an alternative is to use signature-free protocols, which rely only on cheap, post-quantum secure authenticated channels.
  In this paper, we ask whether signature-free BFT consensus protocols can match the performance of current state-of-the-art, quantum-vulnerable BFT consensus protocols. While previous work on the Sailfish++ protocol showed that state-of-the-art throughput is attainable signature-free, the question of latency is still open. Several recent signature-free protocols have low latency in theory, but they are all very intricate, and no practical implementation has so far been presented. In this work, we propose Simple-IT, a new leader-based, signature-free BFT consensus protocol that achieves a theoretical latency of 4 message delays (one more than the optimum), and only 3 on its optimistic path. Crucially, Simple-IT is simple enough to be amenable to implementation and to practical optimizations such as speculative pipelining, and, as we show experimentally in a geo-distributed testbed, it achieves both throughput and latency competitive with state-of-the-art quantum-vulnerable protocols.

</details>


### [231] [parRSB: Exascale Spectral Element Mesh Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14659)
*Thilina Ratnayaka,Paul Fischer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: parRSB 是一款针对谱元网格的高扩展性并行图划分工具，通过递归谱平分法优化负载均衡与通信量，并在顶尖超算平台上验证了其卓越的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大规模谱元网格在并行计算中如何高效、高质量进行图划分的问题，以最小化处理器间的通信开销。

Method: 基于输入网格对偶图（dual graph）的递归谱平分（RSB）算法；使用 Lanczos 方法和基于共轭梯度法的逆迭代法计算 Fiedler 向量；并针对计算性能进行了多项优化。

Result: 在 Oak Ridge 国家实验室的 Summit 和 Frontier 超级计算机上成功验证了 parRSB 的高性能表现，展示了其分区质量与算力扩展能力。

Conclusion: parRSB 能够实现高效的串行化图划分，不仅在分区质量上表现优异，而且在 Summit 和 Frontier 等大规模超级计算机系统上具有极佳的横向扩展性（Scalability）。

Abstract: We introduce parRSB - a parallel, highly scalable graph partitioner for spectral element meshes that produce high quality partitions. parRSB is based on Recursive Spectral Bisection (RSB) algorithm implemented on the dual graph of the input mesh. RSB uses the Fiedler vector, which is the eigenvector associated with the smallest non-zero eigenvalue of the Laplacian matrix of the dual graph for making partitioning decisions and tries to minimize the communication volume between the partitions. We implemented two numerical methods: Lanczos, and Inverse iteration using Conjugate Gradient method to compute the Fiedler vector. We present partitioning results using parRSB on Summit and Frontier supercomputers at Oak Ridge National Laboratory to illustrate the quality of the partitions produced by parRSB and the scalability of our implementation. We also present results for some of the optimizations we did to speed up the partitioning process.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [232] [Occupancy-Grounded Room Segmentation for Hierarchical 3D Scene Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13727)
*Carlos Cueto Zumaya,Iacopo Catalano,Jorge Peña-Queralta,Wallace Moreira Bessa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于占用空间分解的3D场景图构建管线，通过几何可验证的房间多边形轮廓提升了房间层级场景图的构建质量，优于传统的基于空间连接性的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的分层3D场景图（3DSG）在构建房间层级时缺乏统一的几何准则（基于不同的空间底座，如聚类、平面等），导致房间节点的定义不一致且难以评估。

Method: 提出了一种“占用基础（Occupancy-grounded）”的3D场景图构建流程，通过从占用空间分解中衍生出的“已追踪自由空间区域”来锚定房间节点，并赋予每个房间明确的多边形轮廓（Polygonal footprint）。

Result: 在12个Matterport3D场景上的测试表明，该方法比现有的Hydra（基于空间连接的基线）恢复了更多的房间实例，但精确度相对较低；同时也证实了准确恢复墙壁边界目前仍是该领域的一大技术难题。

Conclusion: 提出了一种基于占用空间分解的3D场景图构建方法，能够更稳健地识别房间实例。尽管相比基于空间连接性的方法召回率更高，但精确度仍有提升空间，且精确的墙壁边界恢复仍是当前面临的挑战。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D scene graphs (3DSGs) for indoor robots organize geometric and semantic information across spatial scales, with a room layer that connects object-level perception to room-scale reasoning. Existing systems construct this layer from different spatial substrates (\eg{} place clusters, wall planes, or segmentation outputs), and as a result, room nodes are not evaluated on a common geometric criterion. We present an occupancy-grounded 3DSG pipeline in which room nodes are anchored to tracked free-space regions derived from occupancy decomposition, giving each room an explicit polygonal footprint. We evaluate the pipeline on 12 Matterport3D scenes by matching predicted room polygons to annotated room instances and compare against Hydra, a representative state-of-the-art place-connectivity baseline. The results show that occupancy-grounded anchoring recovers substantially more room instances than place-connectivity construction, at the cost of lower precision, and that wall-accurate room boundaries remain an open problem for both methods. Code is available at https://github.com/crcz25/OccuSG.

</details>


### [233] [Scalable Dynamic Tactile Sensing Enabled by Passive and Flexible Acoustic Waveguides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13746)
*Guimin Long,Changhong Linghu,Chuanping Liu,Ke Xu,Xingjian Jing*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过深亚波长声波导网络，实现了一种无需复杂布线的柔性触觉传感系统，成功解决了大面积触感传感中性能平衡与成本的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉传感技术在实现大面积阵列时，在灵敏度、鲁棒性、柔韧性、布线复杂度和成本之间存在难以权衡的矛盾。

Method: 采用深亚波长声波导技术，构建包含弹性膜覆盖的亥姆霍兹共振器及弹簧增强微管的封闭网络；结合稀疏麦克风阵列布局，利用连续小波变换和轻量级神经网络进行信号推理。

Result: 实现了高达4毫米的空间分辨率，在64节点阵列中定位准确率超过99%，推理延迟仅5.5毫秒；成功应用于指尖触觉阵列、传感手套和大面积皮肤，能够感知从单根毛发接触到脉搏跳动等多种微弱触觉刺激。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可扩展、柔性且低成本的分布式声学触觉传感范式，为下一代人机交互技术提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Artificial dynamic tactile sensing requires sensitivity, robustness, and compliance, yet existing technologies face trade-offs when scaling to large-area arrays, compounded by wiring complexity and cost. Here, we report a passive distributed paradigm using deep sub-wavelength acoustic waveguides that decouples performance from structural flexibility. Elastic-membrane-capped Helmholtz resonators interconnected by spring-reinforced microtubes form an enclosed network with invariant acoustic transmission under macroscopic bending. By sparsely embedding microphones, the system achieves real-time localization (4 mm highest spatial resolution; >99% accuracy in a 4 microphones 64-node sensing array) and waveform reconstruction of low-frequency signals (<100 Hz). Fast Continuous Wavelet Transform and a lightweight neural network enable inference within 5.5 ms. We demonstrate conformable prototypes-fingertip arrays, a tactile glove, and large-area skins-detecting stimuli from single-hair contact to 5-mg particle impacts, arterial pulse waves, feather touches, and finger contact. This establishes a scalable, flexible, low-cost paradigm for next-generation human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [234] [$μ_0$: A Scalable 3D Interaction-Trace World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13769)
*Seungjae Lee,Yoonkyo Jung,Jusuk Lee,Jonghun Shin,Amir Hossein Shahidzadeh,Yao-Chih Lee,H. Jin Kim,Jia-Bin Huang,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为了摆脱具身限制并提升扩展性，μ0通过预测简洁的“3D轨迹”代替传统的像素或动作预测，实现了从视频中高效学习世界模型并成功迁移至机器人控制任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的世界模型存在两极化问题：基于像素的模型消耗过多计算力在外观还原上，而直接建模动作的模型又受限于特定具身的标签，难以扩展。作者旨在寻找一种既能利用广泛视频 priors，又能摆脱具身特定限制的通用动作接口。

Method: 提出了μ0模型和TraceExtract系统。μ0是一种基于3D轨迹的世界模型，通过预测关键交互点的B样条曲线而非预测像素来学习动态；TraceExtract系统则用于从海量视频中自动提取3D关键点轨迹，并结合分层语言标注进行预训练。

Result: 在2D和3D轨迹预测任务中优于现有基准模型；基于该模型训练的轨迹条件策略在机器人操纵任务中表现出与主流VLA模型（如π0）相当的性能。

Conclusion: 3D轨迹是跨具身操作任务中一种可扩展且具有高度迁移性的表征方式，该方法证明了在无动作数据的情况下，预训练的世界模型依然能达到与有监督VLA模型相当的性能。

Abstract: World models that capture how actions induce physical change enable scalable robot learning without reliance on embodiment-specific action labels. Pixel-space video models provide broad visual priors but expend model capacity on dense appearance reconstruction, while direct action models require embodiment-specific labels that hinder scalability. We present $μ_0$, a scalable world model based on 3D traces. Rather than predicting dense pixels or directly modeling actions, $μ_0$ forecasts smooth 3D trajectories for salient interaction points such as objects, tools, hands, and contact regions, yielding a compact, embodiment-agnostic motion interface. To enable training from diverse video sources, our TraceExtract system automatically extracts 3D supervision by selecting keypoints, constructing globally aligned traces, and associating motion segments with hierarchical language captions. This TraceExtract supervision pretrains $μ_0$ by combining a pretrained vision-language backbone with a modular trace expert, which represents each query via B-spline control points and predicts future traces. Experiments show that $μ_0$ outperforms baselines in both 2D and 3D trace prediction, including trace prediction models and tokenized VLM methods. Because $μ_0$ is frozen and reusable, it can be paired with action experts for downstream robot embodiments. Despite action-free pretraining, the resulting trace-conditioned policies achieve performance competitive with VLA models pretrained with action supervision, such as $π_0$. These results establish 3D traces as a scalable and transferable representation for cross-embodiment manipulation.

</details>


### [235] [FlowMo-WM: A World Model with Object Momentum and Hidden Ambient Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13817)
*Yitao Jiang,Luyang Zhao,Muhao Chen,Devin Balkcom*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FlowMo-WM 是一种通过将智能体动作与环境漂移（如水流、风）解耦，从而提升机器人复杂环境下长程预测能力的新型视觉世界模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作条件世界模型在处理受惯性影响及受外部环境漂移（如水流、风）作用的真实世界对象（如水面航行器）时预测能力不足，无法有效区分受控运动与外部扰动。

Method: 提出端到端的视觉世界模型 FlowMo-WM：1. 将历史观测分解为短期的“对象中心运动状态”和长期的“外源性漂移上下文”；2. 设计零上下文残差转换（zero-context residual transition），将受控的基本动力学与上下文相关的漂移效应分离。无需显式流场监督即可推理出隐藏的漂移状态。

Result: 在模拟水面航行器环境中，相比代表性的动作条件潜在世界模型，FlowMo-WM 实现了更高的长程预测准确度；消融实验证实了推断出的上下文信息对于抵御隐藏漂移的必要性。

Conclusion: FlowMo-WM 通过解耦主体动力学与环境漂移（hidden drift），在存在复杂外部干扰（如水流、风）的场景下，显著提升了机器人视觉世界模型的长程预测精度和鲁棒性。

Abstract: World models in robot learning predict future states from visual observations and actions, enabling agents to reason about the consequences of their controls. However, many action-conditioned models are evaluated in settings where motion is dominated by immediate control, whereas aquatic surface vehicles and other real-world objects continue moving under inertia and are displaced by hidden ambient drift, such as water currents or wind. We propose FlowMo-WM, an end-to-end trainable visual world model that infers object-centric motion state and a predictive long-history context associated with hidden drift from image-action histories without direct supervision of flow fields. FlowMo-WM factorizes image-action history into a short-history latent state, trained to summarize object-centric motion, and a longer-history context, trained to summarize slowly varying exogenous influences. A zero-context residual transition separates action-conditioned base dynamics from context-dependent drift effects during latent rollout. In simulated aquatic surface-vehicle environments with diverse hidden flows, disturbances, and randomized vehicle dynamics, FlowMo-WM improves long-horizon rollout accuracy over representative action-conditioned latent world models. Prediction-time context ablations, in which the inferred context is zeroed or shuffled during rollout, show that the ambient context is important for stable prediction under hidden drift, while frozen linear probes characterize information encoded in the learned factors.

</details>


### [236] [Multi-Agent Embodied Autonomous Driving: From V2X Information Exchange to Shared World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13840)
*Senkang Hu,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Zihan Fang,Sam Tak Wu Kwong,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本综述系统梳理了从单车智能向多智能体具身自动驾驶（MAEAD）的演进，探讨了“共享世界模型”在协同驾驶中的作用，揭示了当前领域在实时安全性、真实环境验证和协同鲁棒性方面的研究缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶正从单车智能向多智能体具身系统演进，亟需探讨如何通过共享信息实现跨智能体的感知对齐、意图推理与行动协同，以应对不确定性环境。

Method: 综述了超过380篇文献，涵盖了V2X通信、协作感知、智能体间认知、协同规划、端到端协同驾驶以及闭环验证与仿真数据引擎等多个领域，通过“共享世界模型（SWMs）”的视角进行整合分析。

Result: 指出当前研究多局限于仿真环境和离线协议，基于基础模型（Foundation Models）的协同方案在开放交通场景中尚缺乏可靠的实时安全保障。

Conclusion: 多智能体具身自动驾驶（MAEAD）的研究重点应转向可验证的共享状态维护、鲁棒的意图与规划对齐，以及在通信、延迟和实际部署约束下的安全协同行动。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is shifting from isolated vehicle intelligence toward multi-agent embodied systems that share perception, infer intent, and coordinate action under uncertainty. This survey examines this transition through the lens of Shared World Models (SWMs): predictive cross-agent representations maintained across vehicles, infrastructure, and other traffic participants. We review more than 380 publications spanning vehicle-to-everything (V2X) communication, collaborative perception, inter-agent cognition, cooperative planning, end-to-end cooperative driving, and simulation and data engines for closed-loop validation. The organizing question is how exchanged observations become aligned state, intent-aware interaction, and coordinated downstream action. Across the surveyed literature, evaluation remains concentrated in simulation, curated benchmarks, and offline protocols. Foundation-model-based coordination also lacks verified real-time safety guarantees in open traffic. These gaps motivate key research priorities for multi-agent embodied autonomous driving (MAEAD): verifiable shared-state maintenance, robust intent and plan alignment, and safe coordinated action under communication, latency, and deployment constraints.

</details>


### [237] [Efficient Domain-Adaptive Policy Learning via Kernel Representation with Application to Quadrotor Control under Non-Stationary Disturbances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13842)
*Hongyu Zhou,Mingtian Tan,Vasileios Tzoumas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于核表示的领域自适应强化学习算法，通过随机傅里叶特征高效建模扰动，实现极速离线训练与实时在线自适应，成功应用于四旋翼复杂环境下的抗干扰控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决领域自适应策略学习中环境表示的复杂性与在线部署计算效率之间的矛盾，特别是在面对四旋翼等复杂动态环境中的Sim-to-Real差距时。

Method: 1. 使用基于随机傅里叶特征（Random Fourier Features）的可微分核近似建模未知环境扰动。
2. 离线阶段：通过随机采样核参数生成多样化扰动，利用可微分仿真进行策略优化。
3. 在线阶段：通过最小二乘法实时更新核参数（系数和带宽）以实现自适应。

Result: 在RTX 4090上仅需50秒即可完成训练。在包含风力、地效、负载变化等复杂干扰的物理实验和仿真中，表现出了卓越的轨迹跟踪性能和实时自适应能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合核表示与可微分仿真，实现了在各种非平稳扰动环境下高效、实时的四旋翼策略自适应，证明了其在解决Sim-to-Real差距方面的有效性。

Abstract: We present an algorithm for efficient domain-adaptive policy learning via kernel representations. Learning domain-adaptive policies is challenging since it requires an environment representation that is both sufficiently expressive to model complex sim-to-real gaps during offline training, and computationally efficient enough to support rapid online adaptation during deployment. For instance, a quadrotor may encounter time-varying, non-stationary disturbances, such as sudden gusts of wind, payload shifts, or transitions between distinct flight regimes with and without ground effects. To address these challenges, we model unknown disturbances using a differentiable kernel approximation based on random Fourier features. During the offline training phase, we randomly sample kernel coefficients and bandwidth parameters to generate a rich diversity of disturbance profiles. We then optimize the control policy via differentiable simulation with analytical gradients, a process that takes only 50 seconds of training time on an RTX 4090 GPU. During hardware deployment, the policy adapts to non-stationary environments in real time by updating both the kernel coefficients and bandwidth through online least-squares estimation. We evaluate our method on quadrotor trajectory tracking tasks across high-fidelity numerical simulations and hardware experiments using Crazyflie, subjected to various disturbances, including complex aerodynamic effects, wind, ground effects, and payload fluctuations.

</details>


### [238] [Output-Level Regularization Eliminates the Seed Lottery in Single-GPU VLA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13856)
*Jeffrin Sam,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA-JEPA微调中出现的“随机种子导致模型性能崩溃”现象，本文证明了传统权重正则化无法解决该问题，并提出采用输出层正则化（如简单调整超参数）即可有效消除此故障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA-JEPA模型在微调过程中存在“种子抽奖”（Seed Lottery）现象：即在完全相同的设置下，个别随机种子会导致模型陷入“输出崩塌”（Output Collapse），且现有常规权重级正则化方法无法预测或解决此问题。

Method: 通过分析雅可比矩阵（Jacobian null-space）揭示权重级正则化为何失效，并对比评估了多种正则化方法（包括L2、EWC、VICReg、Dropout及学习率调整）在LIBERO基准测试下的鲁棒性。

Result: 实验发现，使用输出层正则化方法（VICReg、Dropout、减半学习率）在21次测试中消除了所有灾难性崩溃（0次崩塌），显著优于基准模型的表现（1/13崩塌）。

Conclusion: 通过输出层正则化（VICReg、Dropout、降低学习率）可以有效消除VLA-JEPA微调过程中的“种子抽奖”现象（输出崩塌），而传统的权重级正则化方法（L2、EWC）则对此无效。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a vision-language-action model (VLA-JEPA) on a single GPU should be simple: load a pretrained checkpoint, run training, deploy. There is a hidden danger. Run the same fine-tuning code thirteen times -- same data, same architecture, different random seed -- and twelve runs produce a robot succeeding 91--94% of the time, while one run silently degrades to 65.2%: a 29 pp gap with no error message, no warning, and no way to predict which seed will fail. We call this the seed lottery. We trace the cause to output collapse: the action predictor quietly learns to produce nearly identical outputs regardless of what the robot sees. Existing weight-level methods (L2, EWC) are structurally blind to this collapse -- they penalize weight changes, but collapse occurs in directions weights can move freely without affecting outputs, a gap we formalize via the Jacobian null-space. Across 7 methods x up to 13 seeds x 3 LIBERO benchmarks, three output-level regularizers -- VICReg (n=12 seeds), Dropout (n=4), and a halved learning rate (n=5) -- each eliminate every catastrophic seed (0/21 combined collapses vs. 1/13 Baseline; F(12,11)=28.7, p<0.001), while weight-level methods (L2, EWC) preserve the lottery. The simplest fix is changing one number in your optimizer config.

</details>


### [239] [ContactWorld: What Matters in Vision-Tactile World Models for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13877)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Pokuang Zhou,Kaidi Zhang,Adeesh Desai,Temitope Amosa,Davood Soleymanzadeh,Jiuzhou Lei,Minghui Zheng,Yu She*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ContactWorld基准，研究发现空间结构化、时间连续性以及跨模态兼容的表示（特别是点云与触觉力场的结合）是实现稳定、长程接触丰富型机器人操纵的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究哪些表示属性能够从多模态传感器观察中，为接触丰富型操纵提供基础性的稳定长程规划支持，并解决目前对此尚不明确的问题。

Method: 提出了ContactWorld基准，涵盖12项接触丰富型操纵任务。对视觉-触觉世界模型进行系统性实证研究，对比不同感知维度观察（腕式、前视、点云）和触觉表征方式，评估其在长程规划中的表现。

Result: 1. 空间结构化与时间连续性是核心；2. 点云观察优于传统视角（成功率从约21%提升至32.1%）；3. 触觉性能依赖于跨模态兼容性，而非单纯的模态缩放；4. 点云+触觉力场组合达到最佳（成功率36.1%）；5. 触觉在处理长程规划中的累积误差和接触不确定性方面作用显著。

Conclusion: 本研究确立了空间结构化和时间连续性对于接触丰富型操纵任务的重要性。研究表明，利用点云观察结合触觉力场表示，能通过增强跨模态兼容性，显著提升长程规划的鲁棒性和任务成功率。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires world models to reason over complex contact dynamics from multimodal sensory observations. However, it remains unclear which representation properties fundamentally support stable long-horizon planning in contact-rich settings. In this paper, we present ContactWorld, a benchmark and systematic empirical study of vision-tactile world models spanning 12 contact-rich manipulation tasks, including insertion, disassembly, screwing, and exploratory interaction. Across extensive experiments, we find that representations that are both spatially structured and temporally continuous consistently achieve the strongest planning performance. In particular, point-cloud observations improve average planning success rates from 20.7% with wrist-view observations and 22.0% with front-view observations to 32.1%. We further find that the effectiveness of tactile sensing depends critically on cross-modal representation compatibility rather than modality scaling alone. Combining point-cloud observations with tactile force-field representations, which preserve richer spatial structure and interaction dynamics, further improves performance to 36.1%, yielding the strongest overall planning performance across all evaluated tasks. Moreover, tactile sensing becomes increasingly important under long-horizon planning objectives, where compounding prediction errors and contact uncertainty accumulate over time. Together, these findings highlight the importance of representation structure, multimodal compatibility, and long-horizon robustness in vision-tactile world models for contact-rich robotic manipulation.

</details>


### [240] [AnyGoal: Vision-Language Guided Multi-Agent Exploration for Training-Free Lifelong Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13878)
*MoniJesu James,Marcelino Julio Fernando,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AnyGoal是一个无需训练的、基于VLM和贝叶斯地图的多智能体导航架构，通过持续的证据累积与分布式决策，显著提升了机器人在未知环境下的导航泛化能力与目标寻找效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有导航方法在面对分布外（OOD）场景、类别或目标模态时泛化能力差，且存在模块化管道受限于检测召回率或3D记忆系统计算开销过大等问题。

Method: 提出AnyGoal架构，核心特征包括：1) 利用视觉语言模型（VLM）进行开放词汇目标感知；2) 使用2D高斯贝叶斯价值地图（BVM）进行终身证据累积；3) 基于VLM评分与贝叶斯UCB加权的边际（Frontier）排序；4) 无中心化的贪婪任务分配策略。

Result: 在GOAT-Bench（未见集）评估中，双智能体AnyGoal达成52.4%的子任务成功率（SR），相比Modular GOAT（24.9%）提升了27.5个百分点，刷新了现有物理限制环境下的SOTA记录。

Conclusion: AnyGoal架构通过利用VLM和BVM机制，解决了开放词汇导航中的泛化、记忆和多智能体协同难题，在GOAT-Bench评估中取得了显著优于现有模块化导航方案（如Modular GOAT）的性能。

Abstract: End-to-end navigation policies trained on large simulation corpora degrade sharply when transferred to out-of-distribution scenes, categories, or goal modalities. Modular pipelines such as Modular GOAT are bottlenecked by closed-set object detection recall, while 3D snapshot-memory systems (e.g. 3D-Mem) accumulate dense, view-dependent representations that are heavy to maintain. We present AnyGoal, a training-free multi-robot architecture that places a Vision-Language Model (VLM) at the core of frontier-based exploration and coordinates agents through a shared 2D Gaussian Bayesian Value Map (BVM). The BVM maintains a per-pixel (mu, sigma^2) posterior over goal relevance, updated via precision-weighted fusion of VLM scores through a depth-cone mask, and is never reset between subtasks, yielding lifelong evidence accumulation. Frontiers are ranked by a convex blend of a VLM-as-judge softmax and a Bayesian UCB term on the BVM. A greedy allocator with spatial-separation penalty and commitment hysteresis distributes frontiers across agents without a centralized controller. On the full GOAT-Bench val unseen split (360 episodes, 2,669 subtasks), our dual-agent system achieves 52.4% Subtask SR at 12.7% SPL--state of the art under the strict physical regime (discrete 0.25 m steps, no teleportation, 42 deg HFOV) and a +27.5 pp improvement over Modular GOAT (24.9%). Single-agent AnyGoal achieves 41.9% Subtask SR, showing gains arise from the decision architecture. A four-way perception ablation shows that open-vocabulary detectors shift the dominant failure mode from exploration to goal verification.

</details>


### [241] [Guided Diffusion with Distilled Vision-Language Reliability for Aerial Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13883)
*Ivan Valuev,Iana Zhura,Valerii Serpiva,Didar Seyidov,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无人机端到端导航在复杂/退化环境下表现不佳的问题，本文提出一种可靠性感知扩散规划器，通过蒸馏视觉语言模型获取实时环境置信度，并指导全局轨迹生成，实现了导航安全性与鲁棒性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的模块化导航管线存在误差传递和延迟，而现有的端到端扩散模型缺乏感知可靠性评估能力，难以处理玻璃、遮挡或强光等退化环境，导致碰撞风险。

Method: 1. 引入轻量级网络，通过蒸馏大视觉语言模型（VLM）生成实时可靠性热力图；2. 采用扩散模型进行轨迹生成，并将可靠性条件纳入生成过程；3. 设计两阶段ESDF（欧几里得符号距离场）代价函数，用于指导去噪过程，统一处理物理障碍物与不可靠虚拟障碍物。

Result: 在仿真及真实无人机实验中，将障碍物碰撞率从40.3%大幅降低至9.6%；穿越区域的平均可靠性指数从0.588提升至0.925；系统运行效率较完整VLM框架提升至2倍。

Conclusion: 提出了一种可靠性感知扩散规划器，通过结合场景置信度热力图和物理/虚拟障碍物代价引导，解决了端到端导航模型在面对感知退化区域（如玻璃、强光等）时的鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Autonomous UAV navigation is conventionally solved by pipelines that separate perception, mapping, and planning into distinct stages, which propagates errors, accumulates latency, and requires environment-specific retuning. End-to-end generative models remove these interfaces by mapping raw observations directly to trajectories, but inherit a subtle failure mode: trained on clean data, they cannot recognise when an observation is unreliable, and treat degraded regions such as glass, mirrors, and overexposed surfaces as valid evidence for planning. We present a reliability-aware diffusion planner for 3D UAV navigation. It conditions trajectory generation on the observation together with a scene-level reliability heatmap that marks where perception cannot be trusted, produced by a lightweight network that distils the open-vocabulary reasoning of a vision-language model within the real-time planning budget. To generalise to unseen environments without retraining, we steer the denoising process with a differentiable two-stage ESDF cost that treats physical obstacles from depth and virtual obstacles from highly unreliable regions on equal footing. In simulation and on a real quadrotor, our planner produces markedly safer trajectories than a state-of-the-art diffusion baseline, reducing the obstacle-violation rate from 40.3% to 9.6% and raising the mean reliability of traversed regions from 0.588 to 0.925. Ablating the reliability term alone drops mean reliability from 0.898 to 0.783, confirming it as the decisive component, while distillation runs the framework up to 2 times faster than the full vision-language model.

</details>


### [242] [PhysVLA: Towards Physically-Grounded VLA for Embodied Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13886)
*Namai Chandra,Shriram Damodaran,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PhysVLA 是一种即插即用的推理时辅助框架，能在不修改 VLA 模型权重的前提下，通过动力学约束和任务状态机修正动作输出，显著提升机器人的控制稳定性与任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型主要通过拟合演示数据训练，缺乏对物理定律（如刚体动力学、接触约束）的显式约束，导致基于插值或平滑的处理难以解决由动力学不一致带来的任务失败。

Method: 提出 PhysVLA 框架，该框架作为冻结 VLA 主干模型的外部包装器：1. 采用感知阶段的有限状态机划分任务片段；2. 结合基于 Euler-Lagrange 方程的动力学门控机制，仅在检测到运动学不一致时进行修正。

Result: 在多个 VLA 基线模型（OpenVLA 等）上测试，成功率提高达 17%，稳定性提升达 19%，轨迹抖动稳健性提升最高达 10 倍；在真实硬件实验中成功率提升最高达 50%，且推理延迟低于 1ms。

Conclusion: PhysVLA 证明了物理感知可以作为一个与模型无关（backbone-agnostic）、即插即用的推理时模块存在，在无需重新训练的情况下显著提升了 VLA 模型在模拟环境及真实硬件上的成功率与稳定性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel at mapping visual inputs and natural language instructions directly to robotic control policies. However, because they are trained primarily to fit behavioural demonstration data, they do not explicitly enforce fundamental physical principles such as rigid-body dynamics or contact constraints. This exposes a critical physics gap: standard temporal smoothing applied on top of single-step or chunked VLAs trades trajectory quality for added failures that short-term memory cannot resolve. To bridge this gap, we introduce PhysVLA (Physics-VLA), a plug-and-play, inference-time framework designed to wrap any frozen VLA backbone without retraining, fine-tuning, or weight access, with less than 1 ms of overhead per control step. PhysVLA intercepts the predicted control action, captures only the simulator or system state, and applies a dual-layered correction: (i) a phase-aware finite-state machine that structures discrete task segments (approach, grasp, transport, and place), and (ii) a selective Euler-Lagrange gate that activates only when a dynamics oracle detects kinodynamic inconsistency. Evaluated across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, Force-VLA, and Generalist-VLA on LIBERO-Spatial with a 7-DoF Franka Panda, the framework delivers absolute success rate increases of up to 17% and stability increases of up to 19% with no per-task regressions, improves trajectory efficiency by up to 15% across all four backbones, and shows up to a 10x improvement in trajectory jerk robustness on a Robosuite Lift cross-simulator sweep. We further validate the framework on a real Agilex Piper arm with a pick-and-place task, confirming that PhysVLA transfers to physical hardware without retraining, with success-rate improvements of up to 50%, establishing physical awareness as a composable, backbone-agnostic runtime module.

</details>


### [243] [Learning Dynamic Swing-Up of an Inverted Pendulum using Remote Magnetic Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13915)
*Viacheslav Sydora,Jasan Zughaibi,Denis von Arx,Quentin Boehler,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合轨迹优化、LQR反馈和迭代学习控制（ILC）的方法，首次实现了磁驱动倒立摆的摆起，有效解决了电磁导航系统在处理非线性动力学和不确定性时的控制难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的电磁导航系统（eMNS）研究多集中于准静态控制，在非平衡态下的动态轨迹跟踪研究不足；传统的LQR控制在面对磁驱动复杂的动力学非线性和模型不确定性时表现不佳。

Method: 1. 轨迹优化：考虑电磁导航系统（eMNS）的内部动力学；2. 时变线性二次型调节器（LQR）：用于状态反馈控制；3. 迭代学习控制（ILC）：利用历史试验数据和动力学模型优化前馈命令，以修正磁驱动系统特有的非线性误差。

Result: 在临床级Navion eMNS设备上成功实现了磁驱动倒立摆的摆起；ILC仅需6次迭代即实现成功控制；实验证明所学习的补偿项与高保真磁场模型预测的力矩偏差高度匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究证明了通过ILC结合轨迹优化和LQR，能够有效克服磁性驱动系统中复杂的非线性动力学和模型不确定性，成功实现动态轨迹跟踪（倒立摆摆起）。这种方法为导管、导丝等临床磁驱动设备在复杂动态环境下的控制提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Electromagnetic Navigation Systems (eMNS) have gained considerable attention for minimally invasive surgery and targeted drug delivery. While most of the literature relies on quasi-static control of these systems, recent work has demonstrated the benefits of dynamic approaches. However, trajectory tracking far from equilibrium states remains largely unaddressed. We close this gap by demonstrating the first swing-up of a magnetically actuated inverted pendulum using the clinically-ready Navion eMNS. Although the inverted pendulum is not clinically relevant in itself, the proposed method utilizes torques and forces as control objectives, making it applicable to other magnetically actuated devices such as catheters and guidewires. Our approach combines trajectory optimization that accounts for internal eMNS dynamics with time-varying Linear Quadratic Regulator (LQR) state feedback and Iterative Learning Control (ILC), which leverages previous trial data and the system's dynamic model to progressively refine the feedforward command. While LQR alone fails due to the complex phenomena of magnetic actuation, ILC enables successful swing-up within six iterations. Furthermore, post-experimental analysis reveals that the learned ILC correction closely matches the torque discrepancy predicted by high-fidelity magnetic field model calibration, suggesting learning and adaptation as a promising tool to deal with uncertainties in electromagnetic actuation arising, e.g., from patient-specific physiological motion patterns and field model calibration inaccuracies.

</details>


### [244] [An Attention-based Model for Robust Forecasting with Missing Modality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13970)
*Zhitian Zhang,Wenjie Zi,Yunduz Rakhmangulova,Saghar Irandoust,Hossein Hajimirsadeghi,Thibaut Durand*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于CVAE和Transformer的多模态模型，解决了机器人学习中训练和推理阶段模态缺失的难题，实现了在不完整数据下对完整模态特征的鲁棒近似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中机器人系统常面临传感器数据不完整的情况，但主流多模态模型大多假设训练和推理时所有模态均可用，这限制了其在实际机器人任务中的应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于条件变分自编码器（CVAE）的Transformer架构。利用注意力机制在模态缺失的情况下学习一个统一的、固定维度的潜在表示，并支持在训练和推理过程中处理缺失模态。

Result: 在包含人类轨迹预测和机器人操作预测的五个数据集上的实验表明，该模型在处理缺失信息方面表现出色，并优于现有的多模态融合方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于CVAE的Transformer架构能够有效应对多模态缺失问题，通过学习统一的潜在表示，在处理缺失模态时不仅具备鲁棒性，任务性能也优于现有的多模态融合方法。

Abstract: Learning with missing modalities is a fundamental challenge in multimodal robot learning, as real-world robotic systems often operate in environments with incomplete sensor data. Attention-based models are appealing for processing multimodal data because they can handle multiple modalities with a single backbone network. However, most multimodal models assume that all modalities are available during both training and inference, limiting their applicability in robotic perception and decision-making. In this paper, we introduce a multimodal model designed to handle missing modalities during both training and inference. The model is formulated as a conditional variational autoencoder (CVAE) and incorporates a transformer-based architecture that leverages attention mechanisms to learn a unified, fixed-dimensional representation, even when some modalities are missing. We show that our proposed model can be trained with missing modalities while approximating a robust representation of all modalities. We evaluate our approach on five multimodal datasets across two robot learning tasks: human trajectory prediction and robot manipulation forecasting. Experimental results demonstrate that our model effectively learns from incomplete data and is superior to prior multimodal fusion approaches.

</details>


### [245] [SplatlessDF: Continuous Distance Field Mapping with Non-Splatting Gaussians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13990)
*Monisha Mushtary Uttsha,Lan Wu,Teresa Vidal-Calleja*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SplatlessDF 通过将各向异性高斯应用于连续距离场建模，填补了高斯溅射在机器人空间导航与几何映射任务中的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高斯溅射（GS）方法主要聚焦于视觉重建与渲染，缺乏对机器人导航等任务所需的空间几何信息（如距离、梯度）的直接支持；作者旨在利用高斯基元的高效性，构建一个既能渲染又能提供连续距离场映射的通用表示架构。

Method: 提出了一种基于各向异性高斯的连续距离场（DF）映射框架，通过空间视角而非光度视角参数化高斯，实现可导距离场查询；并将其与 2D 高斯溅射（2DGS）结合，实现了一个同时支持几何建模与光度渲染的统一框架。

Result: 在独立 DF 建模中实现了高效精确的距离与梯度查询；在联合 2DGS 的设置下，提升了渲染几何质量，证明了该方法在机器人映射领域的应用潜力。

Conclusion: SplatlessDF 成功证明了基于高斯基元的表示方法不仅适用于高质量的场景渲染，还能有效地建模连续距离场，为机器人导航等下游任务提供了高效、可导的空间信息支持。

Abstract: Recent Gaussian splatting (GS) methods have shown that scenes can be represented efficiently with optimisable Gaussians for high-quality reconstruction and rendering. In this paper, building on this principle, we introduce SplatlessDF, a continuous distance field (DF) mapping framework that uses anisotropic Gaussian elements from a spatial rather than photometric perspective. SplatlessDF directly parameterises the Gaussians and optimises to recover a differentiable DF, enabling distances and gradients to be queried in the spatial domain for downstream robotic tasks such as navigation. Furthermore, SplatlessDF can be coupled with 2D Gaussian splatting (2DGS), providing a unified framework based solely on Gaussian primitives that can learn continuous DF and surface models and supports photometric rendering. We consider two settings: a standalone DF-only formulation and a joint DF-rendering formulation coupled with 2DGS. Experiments show that the standalone formulation provides efficient and accurate distance and gradient queries, while the joint formulation improves rendering geometry and simultaneously models a continuous DF. These results highlight the potential of GS-style representations not only for surface modelling and rendering but also for mapping representations suited to robotic navigation.

</details>


### [246] [From Attacks to Curricula: Learnability-Guided Adversarial Training for Safe Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14032)
*Yuewen Mei,Tong Nie,Jie Sun,Haotian Shi,Wei Ma,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AlignADV 提出了一种可学习性导向的闭环对抗训练框架，通过 DPO 技术生成“可解”的对抗场景，并利用行为能力预测构建动态课程，从而实现更高效、更安全的自动驾驶策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动驾驶对抗训练框架过于“攻击导向”，主要存在两点问题：1. 碰撞驱动生成的场景往往过于极端且不可解，导致学习退化；2. 启发式采样方法忽略了策略能力的演进，导致样本效率低下和收敛缓慢。

Method: 1. 将对抗场景生成重新定义为偏好对齐问题，利用直接偏好优化（DPO）确保生成的场景既具挑战性又具有可解性；2. 构建基于行为指纹的行为能力预测模型，无需昂贵的闭环仿真即可评估策略性能；3. 设计了动态课程采样机制，优先选择针对当前策略脆弱性的场景进行训练。

Result: 在 Waymo Open Motion Dataset 上实验显示，AlignADV 相比基线方法减少了高达 40.6% 的训练步骤，并同时降低了碰撞率，提高了正常及对抗交通场景下的路线完成率。

Conclusion: AlignADV 通过从“攻击导向”转向“可学习性导向”的对抗训练，证明了在自动驾驶训练中，通过精细化的场景筛选和动态课程学习，可以显著提升训练效率和策略的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Closed-loop adversarial training improves autonomous driving safety by exposing policies to rare safety-critical scenarios. Standard pipelines first generate adversarial scenarios and then sample them for policy optimization. However, most existing frameworks remain attack-oriented: collision-driven generators often synthesize unsolvable extreme situations, which can degrade learning, while heuristic samplers ignore the evolving capability of the driving policy, causing sample inefficiency and delayed convergence. We propose AlignADV, a learnability-guided closed-loop adversarial training framework that converts adversarial scenarios into resolvable and capability-aligned curricula. First, we reformulate adversarial scenario generation as a preference alignment problem and employ direct preference optimization to guide the generator toward critical yet resolvable scenarios. Second, we introduce behavioral fingerprints to capture the intrinsic characteristics of the evolving policy and construct a multi-modal capability prediction model that estimates policy performance without expensive closed-loop simulations. By combining resolvability-aligned scenarios with capability predictions, AlignADV develops a dynamic curriculum sampling mechanism that prioritizes scenarios targeting the current policy's vulnerabilities. Experiments on the Waymo Open Motion Dataset demonstrate that AlignADV improves convergence efficiency and final performance, reducing training steps by up to 40.6 percent compared with baseline methods while lowering collision rate and improving route completion under both normal and adversarial traffic conditions. These results highlight a shift from attack-oriented scenario generation to learnability-guided policy improvement, offering a principled direction for safer and more efficient autonomous driving training. Project page: https://meiyuewen.github.io/AlignADV/.

</details>


### [247] [ReactSim-Bench: Benchmarking Reactive Behavior World Model Simulation in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14058)
*Zhiyuan Zhang,Yanlun Peng,Jianing Zhang,Xianda Guo,Zehan Huang,Haoran Liu,Qifeng Li,Shaofeng Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Junchi Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ReactSim-Bench，这是首个专注于评估自动驾驶行为世界模型“反应能力”的基准，通过强制智能体对非历史轨迹的 AV 行为做出响应，弥补了现有评估闭环交互能力的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶行为模拟基准多采用联合控制或开环评估，无法直接衡量模型在面对偏离历史日志的 AV 行为时的真实反应能力（Reactive Capability）。

Method: 1. 构建包含 2,636 个场景的基准测试集；2. 解耦 AV 与周围智能体控制，输入偏离日志的 AV 轨迹；3. 通过 AV 规划器生成候选行为并经规则与人工校验筛选；4. 使用碰撞、地图合规及运动学可行性指标评估智能体的反应表现。

Result: 通过该基准对 Transformer、扩散模型及下一 token 预测模型进行了系统性评估，揭示了不同架构在反应式任务中的性能差异，并量化了重规划频率对模拟效果的影响。

Conclusion: ReactSim-Bench 填补了自动驾驶行为模拟评价中“反应能力”指标的空白，指出当前模型在处理非对齐 AV 行为时仍面临挑战，并强调了重规划频率对模拟保真度的重要影响。

Abstract: Reactive capability is a key property of data-driven behavior world model simulators for autonomous driving simulation systems. With this capability, simulated world agents can respond feasibly to autonomous vehicle (AV) behaviors that differ from the log. However, existing behavior simulation benchmarks do not directly measure reactive capability. They often let the simulator jointly control the AV and surrounding agents and evaluate realism through log similarity or open-loop prediction metrics. In this work, we introduce ReactSim-Bench for evaluating the reactive capability of behavior world model simulation in autonomous driving. We decouple the control of agents and the AV, using AV behaviors that differ from the log and require agents to respond as independent AV inputs. To obtain these AV behaviors, we construct a pipeline that uses an AV planner model to generate candidate behaviors and filters the data using rules and manual verification. Collision metrics, map-based metrics, and kinematic feasibility metrics are used to evaluate the safety and rule compliance of reactive responses. We construct 2,636 test scenarios with three categories and conduct a systematic evaluation of state-of-the-art models across multiple architectures, including Transformer-based, diffusion-based, and next-token-prediction-based models. We further analyze how replan frequency affects performance and provide insights for future studies.

</details>


### [248] [Semidefinite Relaxations for Collision-Free Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14063)
*Bernhard Paus Graesdal,Alexandre Amice,Pablo A. Parrilo,Russ Tedrake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于无碰撞运动规划的半正定规划（SDP）松弛方法，通过几何解释证明了其紧致性条件，并利用对称性约简实现了比标准非线性规划求解器快两个数量级的求解速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无碰撞运动规划本质上是非凸且困难的。现有的非线性规划方法常常受限于局部最优解或性能不稳定的问题，缺乏对SDP松弛在这一领域应用的理论分析。

Method: 将无碰撞运动规划问题建模为多项式曲线上的非凸优化问题，并提出了相应的半正定规划（SDP）松弛方法。利用对称性约简技术降低了半正定锥的大小，使其计算量与环境维度无关。

Result: 该方法的计算速度比非线性规划求解器（如SNOPT和IPOPT）快10到100倍，且求解时间方差更小。在RRT规划器中作为凸导向函数使用时，能够有效处理四旋翼的高阶连续路径规划。

Conclusion: 提出的半正定规划（SDP）松弛方法能够在保证全局相关最优性的同时，显著提高无碰撞运动规划的求解效率，特别是处理高维场景和复杂连续性需求时表现优异。

Abstract: We study semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. We focus on a point robot moving from start to goal through spherical obstacles in $\mathbb{R}^n$, subject to path continuity constraints and squared derivative costs; a setting that is conceptually simple yet captures the hardness of collision-free motion planning. We formulate this problem exactly as a nonconvex problem over polynomial curves, and present a natural semidefinite relaxation. We contribute two key theoretical insights; to our knowledge this is the first theoretical analysis of semidefinite relaxations for collision-free motion planning. First, we show that solving the convex relaxation is equivalent to solving, to global optimality, a related motion planning problem in a potentially higher-dimensional space. This geometric interpretation yields necessary and sufficient conditions for tightness, and a clear intuition for when the relaxation is loose. Second, we show that the relaxation admits a symmetry reduction that makes it significantly smaller than one might expect, with positive semidefinite cone sizes that scale linearly with the polynomial degree and are independent of the ambient dimension. The resulting relaxation is 10 to 100 times faster than direct nonlinear programming transcriptions solved with SNOPT and IPOPT, exhibits significantly lower variance in solve times, and reliably finds a locally optimal path for the original problem. We demonstrate its effectiveness as a convex steering function in an RRT planner for minimum-snap quadrotor planning with $C^4$ continuous trajectories.

</details>


### [249] [Development of a 3 in Sewer Pipe Inspection Robot with an Articulated Differential Mechanism using X-shaped Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14070)
*Shoya Umemura,Ryota Taniguchi,Atsushi Kakogawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对小型下水道检测机器人跨障与适应性难题，本文提出一种基于耦合驱动与电流感知反馈的控制方法，大幅提升了机器人跨越管道接头及适应变径的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对初代3英寸下水道检测机器人在管径适应性差、牵引力不足及跨越障碍能力弱的问题，旨在改进结构与控制策略，提升机器人在复杂管网环境下的作业性能。

Method: 1. 结构优化：通过串联耦合推进单元并引入差速机制，利用单线缆控制实现姿态调整，以适应不同管径；2. 控制算法：监测驱动轮电机电流负载变化以判定障碍物接触，通过自动放松线缆来降低阻力，增强跨越管接头等障碍的能力。

Result: 仿真管道实验表明，提出的控制方法能有效识别并在障碍物接触时调整线缆状态，显著提高了机器人的跨越能力，电流波形对比分析验证了负载分配的优化效果。

Conclusion: 通过耦合推进单元设计及基于电流负载感知的自适应松线控制策略，显著克服了管道检测机器人在应对复杂管径变化及障碍物跨越时的局限性，有效提升了机器人的现场通过能力。

Abstract: This paper proposes, an improved version of the 3 in sewer pipe inspection robot equipped with an emergency evacuation mechanism. The low traction force and poor stepover capability, which were challenges of the first version, have been improved by simply connecting the propulsion units. The coupled propulsion units feature a differential mechanism capable of posture changes via a single wire, enabling adaptation to pipe diameter variations. To traverse obstacles like pipe joints, a control method was devised that detects obstacle contact through current load on the drive wheel motors and slackens the wire. This method was verified through simulated pipe experiments. Load comparisons were made using current waveforms applied to the drive wheels. Our proposed control method significantly improved the step-over capability of the new articulated robots.

</details>


### [250] [The N2D Haptic Glove: A Multi-Finger Glove for 2D Directional Force Feedback for Contact Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14083)
*Yao-Ting Huang,Jake Honma,Omar Hernandez,Logan Li,Kaitlin Calimbahin,Bryce Hackel,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为N2D的新型触觉手套，通过卷扬机传动提供平面定向力反馈，解决了传统设备缺乏方向感导致的操控精度问题，并在远程操控实验中验证了其在提升操作精度和一致性方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的穿戴式触觉手套大多仅能提供振动或单轴力，缺乏方向性，导致用户在远程操控时难以推断接触力，易出现过度按压、动作不一致及精度降低等问题。

Method: 开发了N2D触觉手套，采用卷扬机传动（capstan-drive）机构实现指尖平面屈伸力的反馈，并进行了基准测试与机械臂/手的远程操控用户研究。

Result: 实验结果显示，与纯视觉和单轴触觉反馈相比，N2D的平面指尖力反馈显著降低了接触力误差，增强了任务表现的稳定性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，N2D触觉手套通过提供定向力反馈，显著降低了机械臂远程操控中的接触力误差，提升了操作一致性与用户体验，证明了定向指尖力反馈在接触密集型任务中的有效性。

Abstract: Humans rely on directional fingertip forces to probe and regulate contact during manipulation, yet most wearable haptic gloves render only vibration or single-axis force, leaving force direction ambiguous. Without directional cues, users must infer contact force from vision alone, often leading to over-pressing, inconsistent control, and reduced precision in robotic teleoperation. We present the N2D Haptic Glove, a multi-finger wearable device that renders planar flexion-extension fingertip forces using capstan-drive transmissions for high-transparency force feedback. Through benchtop validations and a user study involving haptic teleoperation of a robotic arm and hand, we demonstrate that compared to visual-only and single-axis haptic baselines, planar fingertip feedback significantly reduces contact force error during precise manipulation, improves trial-to-trial consistency, and enhances overall user experience in axial probing tasks. These findings establish the N2D Haptic Glove and directional finger-based haptics devices as a promising modality for contact-rich teleoperation, immersive virtual reality simulations, and robot learning from demonstrations. N2D Haptic Glove's hardware and software system will be fully open-sourced at \href{https://ucsdarclab.github.io/n2d-glove/}{this https URL}.

</details>


### [251] [Self-Improving VLA Policies: Selected Diffusion Noise for Spurious-Robust Action Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14084)
*Duc Minh Nguyen,Bao-Ngoc Dao,Tung M. Luu,Binh Gia Nguyen,Vinh Tong,Anji Liu,Vu N. Duong,Dung D. Le,Daniel Sonntag,Trung Le,Ngan Le,Jan Peter,An Thai Le,Minh Nhat Vu,Mathias Niepert,Khoa D. Doan,Duy M. H. Nguyen,Vien Anh Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型易受干扰的问题，SDN方法通过在推理阶段智能筛选扩散噪声，实现了无需额外训练即可提升机器人操作鲁棒性与动作平滑度的目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽泛化性强，但易受伪视觉相关性和噪声干扰影响，导致在扰动下表现脆弱，缺乏稳定性。

Method: 提出“精选扩散噪声”（Selected Diffusion Noise, SDN）方法。该方法在测试时动态采样出一组噪声向量，通过最大化噪声向量与参考集之间的距离来抑制伪相关性，并筛选出动作轨迹连贯性最高的候选噪声，从而在不修改模型参数的前提下优化输出。

Result: 在Google Robot和Widow-X仿真基准以及多个真实机器人数据集（包含pi_0, Groot系列）上表现优异，成功率分别提升了8%（仿真）和10%（真实世界），同时有效降低了动作抖动。

Conclusion: 通过在推理阶段动态筛选扩散模型噪声，无需重新训练即可显著提升机器人操作策略的鲁棒性、成功率及轨迹平滑度，验证了噪声空间作为增强VLA策略有效控制手段的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies enable strong generalization in robotic manipulation, but remain sensitive to spurious visual correlations and noisy action generation, leading to brittle behavior under perturbations. We introduce Selected Diffusion Noise (SDN), a simple, training-free test-time method that improves both robustness and success rate by leveraging the diffusion noise space as a controllable degree of freedom. SDN dynamically samples noise vectors that are maximally separated from a reference set to mitigate reliance on spurious cues, while selecting candidates that yield more coherent action trajectories. This dual objective encourages stable behavior even under object-masked observations and reduces action jitter without modifying model parameters. We evaluate SDN on two simulation benchmarks (Google Robot, Widow-X) and two real-world robotic datasets across multiple VLA policies, including pi_0, Groot-N1.5, and Groot-N1.6. SDN consistently improves success rates by +8% in simulation and +10% in real-world settings, while producing smoother and more stable actions. Our results highlight that diffusion noise selection can serve as an effective and general mechanism for enhancing VLA policies at test time.

</details>


### [252] [A Modular Dual-Arm Apple Harvesting Robot with Enhanced Field Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14089)
*Keyi Zhu,Kyle Lammers,Chaaran Arunachalam,Kaixiang Zhang,Renfu Lu,Zhaojian Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文设计并验证了一种垂直堆叠双臂模块化苹果采摘机器人，通过引入大模型感知、优化轨迹规划与双臂协同策略，显著提升了果园作业效率与果实完好率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人苹果采摘系统存在吞吐量低和室外非结构化环境下作业表现不佳等问题，限制了其商业化进程。

Method: 提出了一种模块化垂直堆叠双臂采摘机器人系统，包含5项核心技术改进：1. 基于Grounding-DINO和EfficientViT-SAM的基础模型感知流水线；2. 具有CBF安全过滤的7阶加加速度受限轨迹生成；3. 线性扫描采摘策略结合旋转脱离方法；4. 基于时序逻辑的视觉与机械臂异步协调调度策略；5. 在商业果园开展跨品种、跨树形的实地验证。

Result: 实地测试共计1738个机械臂周期，单次尝试成功率为80.0%，单臂平均作业时间为7.53秒；采摘果实质量优良，91.2%达到USDA Extra Fancy最高等级，损伤率控制在2.4%至4.9%之间。

Conclusion: 该新型模块化双臂采摘机器人设计显著提升了作业效率与果实品质，通过技术集成与优化，展现出良好的商业化应用潜力和进一步发展的空间。

Abstract: Robotic apple harvesting offers a promising solution to labor shortages in commercial orchards, but low throughput and poor performance in orchard environments hinder its commercial adoption. This paper presents a modular dual-arm apple harvesting robot that uses a vertically stacked arms to enable simultaneous operation in the upper and lower zones of a single tree, simplifying platform positioning from multi-tree lateral repositioning to single-tree stops. Compared to our prior horizontal dual-arm system, the platform integrates 5 advances: (1)a foundation-model-based perception pipeline combining Grounding-DINO and EfficientViT-SAM for robust fruit localization in unstructured outdoor environments; (2)7th-order jerk-bounded trajectory generation paired with a Control Barrier Function safety filter to achieve fast yet safe arm motions; (3)a linear sweep harvesting strategy with a 10cm approach buffer and rotational detachment that improves picking reliability; (4)a temporal-logic-based dual-arm coordination policy with vision-arm async scheduling that maximizes usage of a shared vacuum source; and (5)field validation in 2 commercial orchards covering different apple varieties and tree architectures during the 2025 harvest season. Across the 1738 arm cycles collected in these field trials, the system achieved an 80.0% per-attempt success rate and a mean per-arm cycle time of 7.53s. Fruit damage assessments confirmed that 91.2% of robotically harvested fruit retained the highest USDA grade (Extra Fancy), with bruise rates between 2.4% and 4.9%. With further improvements in the picking cycle time and handling of heavy foliage occlusions, this new modular robot design holds promise for commercial harvesting of apples.

</details>


### [253] [GAIT: Legged Robot Proprioceptive State Estimation with Attention over Inertial-Leg Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14160)
*Young-Rang Seo,Hajun Kim,Sangmin Kim,Dongyun Kang,Hae-Won Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于IL Tokenization的注意力网络，实现了足式机器人 proprioceptive 状态估计中多模态传感器的动态权重分配，在无需预设接触模型的前提下提升了复杂环境下的估计精度与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的学习型状态估计器通常将所有传感器测量值简单拼接，无法在不同接触状态（如足端触地或腾空）下动态调整对运动学测量值的信任度，且传统模型辅助方案过度依赖对特定接触状态的假设。

Method: 提出了一种基于惯性-腿部(IL)标记化的注意力网络架构，将传感器数据分拆为独立Token而非扁平化向量，利用注意力机制动态学习不同传感器（惯性与运动学）在不同接触状态下的权重，无需显式的接触检测模块。

Result: 在Unitree Go1机器人的实验中，该方法在未见的复杂地形与新颖步态下，在状态估计指标上均优于现有的学习型基准方法及依赖模型的接触辅助方法。

Conclusion: 提出的IL Tokenization方法在不依赖显式接触估计器的情况下，通过注意力机制实现了触觉信息与惯性信息的有效融合，在未知地形和步态下表现出优于现有学习型和模型辅助型估计器的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a method that applies Inertial-Leg (IL) tokenization to an attention-based network for proprioceptive state estimation in legged robots. Unlike existing learning-based state estimators that concatenate all sensor measurements into a single flat vector, the proposed architecture represents inertial measurements and leg-wise measurements as individual tokens and uses an attention mechanism to learn the relative importance of each measurement.This design allows the network to reweight each measurement according to the current contact condition, reflecting the fact that the reliability of forward kinematic measurements depends on whether the corresponding foot is in contact. Unlike conventional contact-aided estimators, however, the proposed method learns this behavior without relying on an explicit contact estimator or on explicit measurement updates based on a stationary contact assumption. To validate the proposed method, we conducted experiments on a Unitree Go1 robot, including debris terrain not modeled in simulation and gait patterns not seen during training. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves better estimation performance than existing learning-based state estimators under unseen gait patterns and also improves performance over contact-aided model-based methods.

</details>


### [254] [Robustness without Wrinkles: Parallel Simulation and Robust MPC for Certified Deformable Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14188)
*Wei-Chen Li,Jeffrey Fang,Sasanka Polisetti,Yuexi Song,Glen Chou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CORD-SLS 是一种用于柔性物体操控的实时安全控制框架，通过 GPU 加速的可微仿真、鲁棒 MPC 和保形预测，实现了存在不确定性环境下的高稳定、高可靠操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在模型不确定性和感知误差的情况下，如何实现柔性物体（如绳索、布料）的实时、安全且高效的操控是一个极具挑战性的高维控制难题。

Method: 1. 核心架构：基于 GPU 并行的可微模拟器（带有接触平滑技术），支持通过间歇性接触进行高效梯度规划。 2. 控制算法：实时、GPU 并行的输出反馈鲁棒 MPC。 3. 安全机制：利用保形预测（Conformal Prediction）校准视觉反馈和感知误差界限，生成可达管（reachable tubes）以确保高概率安全性。 4. 应用扩展：将仿真器用于加速强化学习，以训练神经操作策略。

Result: 在多种模拟及真实硬件任务（如避障、布料折叠、绳索平滑）中，CORD-SLS 实现了毫秒级规划速度，在安全性、执行速度及任务成功率方面均优于现有基准方法。

Conclusion: CORD-SLS 通过结合可微仿真、鲁棒模型预测控制（MPC）和保形预测（Conformal Prediction），成功实现了在模型/感知不确定性下的高维、接触密集型柔性物体（绳索、布料）的实时安全操作。

Abstract: We present CORD-SLS, a real-time control method for safe deformable object manipulation, with a focus on ropes and cloth. At its core is a GPU-parallel differentiable simulator with contact smoothing which enables efficient gradient-based planning through intermittent contact. To robustly satisfy constraints under model and sensing uncertainty, we develop a real-time, GPU-parallel output-feedback robust model predictive control (MPC) algorithm that plans with this simulator. We further show that the simulator accelerates model-based RL for training neural manipulation policies. To improve real-world robustness, we use conformal prediction to calibrate visual-feedback and perception-error bounds for MPC, producing reachable tubes that enable high-probability safe control. We evaluate CORD-SLS on high-dimensional, contact-rich rope and cloth manipulation tasks in simulation and hardware, including obstacle avoidance, routing, folding, and smoothing. Across settings, CORD-SLS achieves millisecond-speed planning, exceeding baselines in safety, speed, and task success.

</details>


### [255] [Short-Horizon Position Accuracy of Single-Track Models: Implications for Motion Planning of Autonomous Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14216)
*Aron J. Aertssen,Lars A. T. H. van Alen,Igo J. M. Besselink,Rudolf G. M. Huisman,René M. J. G. van de Molengraft*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文通过实验对比了量产及实验环境中单轨车辆模型的预测精度，旨在为自动驾驶MPC系统选择合适的车辆模型提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶运动规划依赖于动力学模型，但缺乏关于单轨模型在不同工况下位置预测精度的系统性实验评估。

Method: 通过实机车载实验进行参数辨识，并在多种驾驶工况下，将三种不同复杂度的单轨车辆模型的预测位置与实测位置进行对比分析。

Result: 通过对比评估，量化了各模型在不同驾驶场景下的位置误差，明确了模型复杂性与精度之间的依存关系。

Conclusion: 本文通过实测数据评估了三种单轨车辆模型的短时位置预测精度，揭示了模型复杂性、参数化质量与预测精度之间的权衡关系，为模型预测控制（MPC）中的模型选择提供了指导。

Abstract: Accurate and computationally efficient vehicle models are essential for motion planning of autonomous vehicles, where positional accuracy directly affects trajectory feasibility and safety. However, the positional accuracy has not been systematically evaluated against real measurements. Therefore, this paper compares the short-horizon positional accuracy of three single-track vehicle models against vehicle measurements across various driving maneuvers. Model parameters are identified through dedicated experiments with the instrumented test vehicle. Rather than identifying a single best model, this work aims to provide insight into the trade-offs between model complexity, parameterization quality, and positional accuracy for informed model selection in Model Predictive Control applications.

</details>


### [256] [Universal Manipulation Exoskeleton: Learning Compliant Whole-body Policies with Real-time Torque Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14218)
*Litian Liang,Jingxi Xu,Xinda Qi,Yujun Cai,Houzhu Ding,Luqi Wang,Zhixin Sun,Jyh-Herng Chow,Ming Yang,Mark Cutkosky*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了UME上肢外骨骼，通过提供实时触觉力反馈及高精度动作捕捉，解决了机器人主动柔顺策略数据采集的痛点，实现了在复杂环境下高效的机器人远程操作与策略学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据采集方案缺乏力矩/力反馈获取能力，限制了机器人学习主动柔顺策略以应对家庭环境中复杂接触任务的需求。

Method: 开发了Universal Manipulation Exoskeleton (UME) 上肢外骨骼系统，集成力矩反馈与运动捕捉功能，并配合通用的重定向算法，能够实现对多种机器人（如Franka、OpenArm等）的远程操控。

Result: 利用UME，操作者可在视觉受限的情况下完成复杂的运动学约束任务；通过该系统采集的数据训练出的策略，在多项长程移动操作、力媒介操作及受限场景任务中展现了高成功率。

Conclusion: UME提供了一种低成本、轻量化且高效的远程操作方案，通过实时力反馈助力机器人学习复杂的主动柔顺策略，显著提升了机器人在受限空间及接触式任务中的操作能力。

Abstract: For robots to work safely in household environments, they need to be compliant and react to torque and force feedback during contact. However, the majority of existing data collection pipelines still lack the ability to capture force and torque data for learning active compliant policies. In this paper, we present Universal Manipulation Exoskeleton (UME), an upper-limb exoskeleton that provides real-time haptic torque feedback while recording whole-arm configurations and joint torque signals for teleoperation. With transparent torque feedback, human operators can even unsheathe kinematically constrained objects while blindfolded. UME is low-cost, lightweight, and portable.
