Equipped with an embedded IMU, it enables teleoperation for mobile manipulation. With our proposed universal retargeting algorithm, UME can teleoperate a range of robots, including the 7DoF OpenArm, 7DoF Franka, and 6DoF X-ARM. We demonstrate that this combination of capabilities enables learning bimanual, whole-body, and active compliant policies that operate effectively in highly constrained spaces. The learned robust autonomous policies achieve high success rates across a variety of tasks, including long-horizon mobile manipulation, force-mediated box flipping, visually occluded box pushing, and space-constrained tabletop manipulation. Videos, code, and additional information can be found at https://ume-exo.github.io.

</details>


### [257] [Selective Agentic Recovery for UAV Autonomy with a Persistent Mission Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14219)
*Taewoo Park,Kyeonghyun Yoo,Seunghyun Yoo,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 PMR 框架，通过引入“认知价值评估”（learned-CVI）门控，实现了对无人机远程智能体调用的按需优化，极大地提升了复杂场景下的任务成功率，同时降低了计算成本与资源开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在执行任务时常面临路径阻塞或状态停滞问题，单纯依赖本地控制算法存在局限性，而频繁调用远程 Agentic AI 又会引入高延迟、资源成本高和运行不确定性等问题。如何实现远程推理的高效且安全的选择性调用是本研究的动机。

Method: 提出了一种名为 Persistent Mission Runtime (PMR) 的无人机恢复框架。其核心机制 include: 1) 将安全关键任务保留在本地执行；2) 仅在必要时调用外部智能体作为补救模块；3) 引入 learned-CVI（认知价值评估）作为判别门控，评估调用智能体的预期收益与成本权衡。此外，所有远程决策需经过解析、验证和安全过滤后方可执行。

Result: 在 Gazebo/PX4 平台的 400 次基准测试中，PMR 的任务成功率从基准的 5.0% 提升至 95.0%；相较于其他基线方法，成功率分别提升了 20% 和 32.5%；同时，该方法比手动调节的规则方法减少了 16.7% 的调用频率和 29.2% 的 Token 消耗。

Conclusion: PMR 通过引入 learned-CVI 机制，成功实现了对远程智能体调用逻辑的精确控制，在保障飞行安全的同时显著提升了复杂环境下的任务成功率，并有效降低了资源开销。

Abstract: Agentic AI can support unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) autonomy by providing high-level recovery reasoning when local waypoint- or setpoint-based execution encounters blocked passages, repeated no-progress behavior, or mission-level ambiguity. On physical UAVs, however, remote reasoning is most useful when it is invoked selectively, since each call introduces latency, resource cost, backend uncertainty, and a need to validate the returned decision. This paper presents Persistent Mission Runtime (PMR), a UAV recovery framework that keeps the mission loop and safety-critical execution local while using an external agentic reasoner only as an on-demand recovery module. The reasoner selects from predefined recovery skills, and each returned decision is parsed, verified, safety-filtered, and mapped to local executor actions before it can affect flight. PMR introduces learned Cognitive Value of Invocation (learned-CVI), a compact admission gate that estimates when remote agentic reasoning is likely to improve near-term mission progress enough to justify its operational cost. Across a fixed 400-run Gazebo/PX4 benchmark with eight scenarios, learned-CVI raises hard/ambiguous-regime success from 5.0% under local-only autonomy to 95.0%, outperforms one-shot and periodic reasoning baselines by 20.0 and 32.5 percentage points, and reduces remote-agent calls by 16.7% and logged tokens by 29.2% relative to a manually tuned rule-based invocation baseline.

</details>


### [258] [BIM-Loc: BIM-Integrated Discrepancy-Aware LiDAR-based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14237)
*Yinqiang Zhang,Liang Lu,Yipeng Pan,Maolin Lei,Yuhan Xie,Zhanteng Xie,Xiaowei Luo,Jia Pan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BIM-Loc 是一种利用 BIM 模型作为先验的 LiDAR 定位方法，能够同时实现高精度定位与建筑环境下的在线差异检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统定位系统在特征稀疏的室内环境中鲁棒性差，且缺乏现成的高精度实时地图支持。利用已有的建筑信息模型（BIM）可以提高定位的鲁棒性，但需要解决现实环境与设计模型之间的差异问题。

Method: 1) 采用多重命中射线投射策略（multi-hit ray casting）进行 BIM 数据关联；2) 构建包含 BIM 集成因子的位姿图优化框架（pose graph optimization）；3) 引入层级贝叶斯推理模块，用于在线更新 2D 表面表示并进行差异检测。

Result: 在仿真与真实场景测试中，BIM-Loc 显著提升了定位精度和鲁棒性，有效实现了轨迹估计与差异识别的同步进行。

Conclusion: BIM-Loc 有效解决了室内特征稀疏环境下的定位难题，通过利用 BIM 模型实现高精度定位的同时，具备在线检测现实环境与设计模型之间差异的能力。实验证明其在精度和鲁棒性方面优于现有的地图匹配方法。

Abstract: Accurate and robust localization is a fundamental requirement for service and inspection robots, particularly in feature-sparse indoor environments where traditional systems struggle due to a lack of distinct landmarks. While prior maps can enhance robustness, precise and compact maps capturing real-world details are often unavailable for new or frequently changing environments. This paper presents BIM-Loc, a novel discrepancy-aware LiDAR-based localization method that directly integrates Building Information Models (BIM) from the design phase. BIM-Loc simultaneously estimates trajectories aligned with the BIM coordinate system and identifies discrepancies between real-world observations and the as-designed BIM in an online fashion. Our core contributions include: (1) a novel multi-hit ray casting strategy for efficient BIM-point data association and projection of 3D observations into 2D texture space; (2) a pose graph optimization framework with BIM-integrated factors that enforces consistency among odometry, sequential scans, and BIM structures; and (3) a hierarchical Bayesian inference module that incrementally updates a continuous 2D surface representation for discrepancy detection, propagating updates from the pixel to the structure level. Extensive evaluations in both simulation and real-world applications demonstrate that BIM-Loc significantly outperforms state-of-the-art map-based methods in localization accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [259] [When and How Severely: Scenario-Specific Safety Envelopes for Driving VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14238)
*Abhinaw Priyadershi,Jelena Frtunikj*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究发现VLA驾驶模型对噪声的容忍度并不能反映其失效后的严重程度，建议采用二维安全评价指标（容忍阈值+严重度分布）来构建自动驾驶的SOTIF规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ISO 21448 (SOTIF)框架在规范视觉-语言-动作(VLA)驾驶规划器的运行设计域(ODD)时，缺乏对其失效边界（何时失效）和失效后果（失效多严重）的定量关联评估。

Method: 在15,968个(片段, 攻击)数据对上评估Alpamayo R1（10B参数VLA模型）；通过误差预算(ADE)分析确定噪声容忍度；利用高斯混合模型(GMM)对失效严重程度进行分类（分为6档）；对比分析不同场景下的噪声阈值与高严重度故障(C4/C5)的相关性。

Result: 研究发现存在“保守-聚合偏差”，单一误差阈值无法反映局部场景的真实风险；严重性分析显示，即使平均误差相同，不同场景的严重程度分布差异巨大（例如：STOP_SIGNAL场景的C4/C5高危故障占比是LANE_KEEPING的4倍，尽管前者对噪声的容忍度更高）。

Conclusion: 针对自动驾驶VLA模型的SOTIF（道路车辆预期功能安全）ODD规范不能仅依赖单一的聚合阈值，必须使用涵盖“容忍度”与“严重程度”的二维安全包络。

Abstract: Safety certification of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) driving planners under ISO 21448 (SOTIF) rests on an Operational Design Domain (ODD) specification that answers two complementary questions: when does the planner start to fail, and how severely does it fail once it does? We evaluate Alpamayo R1, a 10B-parameter open-weight driving VLA, on 15,968 (clip, attack) pairs. We find a conservative-aggregate gap: an aggregate safe threshold of $σ\leq 50$ under a 15% average displacement error (ADE) budget masks well-sampled scenarios that tolerate the top of the tested grid ($σ= 70$). A Gaussian Mixture Model (GMM) on the changed-explanation subset identifies six discrete severity bands (BIC-optimal $k{=}6$), so two perturbation conditions with the same mean error can differ materially in their share of high-severity (C4/C5) failures. Joining the two analyses on the same corpus surfaces a finding neither yields in isolation: the scenarios with the loosest noise thresholds are not those with the lowest high-severity rate: STOP_SIGNAL concentrates roughly $4\times$ the C4/C5 share of LANE_KEEPING despite tolerating a larger $σ$. A deployable SOTIF ODD specification for driving VLAs therefore requires a two-dimensional safety envelope, not a single aggregate value per hazard.

</details>


### [260] [SyLink Hand: A Synergy-Inspired Linkage-Driven Anthropomorphic Hand for Human-Like Dexterity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14250)
*Hao Wu,Yanzhe Wang,Yu Feng,Yitong Li,Jingxiang Guo,Jian Liu,Jianshu Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了 SyLink Hand，一款利用协同原理和连杆驱动实现高拟人度、高性价比的灵巧机器人手，通过生物力学引导的简化自由度配置和新型球状连杆机构，实现了紧凑结构下的强大功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计一款既具备高拟人化灵巧度，又兼顾机械结构简单、紧凑且低成本的仿生机器人手。

Method: 基于运动捕捉手套的生物力学分析提取手指运动协同特征；利用优化连杆机构协调多关节运动以复现自然轨迹；创新设计球状四杆机构实现掌指关节的屈伸与外展/内收解耦。

Result: 成功研制出一款包含 19 个关节、仅使用 11 个驱动器的原型机；重量为 520g，制造成本约 400 美元；实验证明其具备高水平的运动学性能、负载能力以及多样化的抓取与操作能力。

Conclusion: 通过协同原理与连杆驱动机制的结合，SyLink Hand 有效平衡了拟人化外观、机械简化性与功能多样性，证明了该设计方案在成本效益和实际部署应用中的可行性。

Abstract: Designing anthropomorphic robotic hands that balance functional dexterity with mechanical simplicity remains a significant challenge. Inspired by human hand synergies, this paper presents the SyLink Hand, an anthropomorphic dexterous hand that integrates biomechanical synergy principles with linkage-driven transmission mechanisms to achieve a high degree of anthropomorphism in appearance, kinematics, and functionality within a compact and cost-effective architecture. Biomechanical analysis of natural hand motions using motion capture gloves reveals strong kinematic correlations among hand joints, providing the basis for a simplified yet functional degree-of-freedom (DOF) configuration. Guided by these synergistic characteristics, optimized linkage mechanisms are employed to coordinate multiple joint motions and reproduce natural finger trajectories. A novel spherical four-bar linkage is further proposed to achieve decoupled flexion/extension (Flex/Ext) and abduction/adduction (Abd/Add) at the metacarpophalangeal joint within a compact form factor. The resulting prototype integrates 19 joints driven by 11 actuators, with a total mass of 520g and a manufacturing cost of approximately USD 400. Experimental evaluations demonstrate its human-like kinematic performance, high load-bearing capability, and versatile grasping and manipulation skills. These results validate that the synergy-inspired, linkage-based design effectively balances anthropomorphism, mechanical simplicity, and functional versatility, highlighting its potential for practical deployment in dexterity-demanding robotic applications.

</details>


### [261] [Optimality-Preserving Decomposition for Scalable QAOA in Natural-Language-Guided Multi-Drone Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14252)
*Junyeop Bang,Byongho Lee,Dohyun An,Hwangnam Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种通过微调LLM将自然语言转化为QUBO约束，并结合创新的量子动态规划与CVaR-QAOA算法，实现了自然语言控制的大规模无人机任务分配，突破了经典计算与量子硬件规模的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多无人机编队规模的扩大，区域任务分配演变为NP难组合优化问题，经典穷举搜索难以处理；同时，将复杂的空间任务从自然语言意图映射到受限的量子硬件上存在巨大挑战。

Method: 提出了一个端到端的框架：1. 前端：利用经过SFT和DPO微调的LLM，将自然语言指令转化为QUBO约束；2. 后端：采用约束保持图分割与压缩分离器动态规划（DP）合并技术来应对量子硬件的量子比特数限制；3. 优化器：使用带有W态初始化和XY混合器的CVaR-QAOA算法进行量子组合优化。

Result: 该架构能够处理以往难以解决的规模，在理想预言机测试中达到100%的全局最优恢复率，在实测QAOA采样中达到96.3%的准确率，显著优于传统方法。

Conclusion: 该研究成功验证了通过结合自然语言处理和量子计算，能够解决复杂的多无人机区域分配问题。该方法绕过了经典计算的扩展瓶颈，在理想情况下实现了100%的全局最优解恢复，在实际QAOA采样中达到了96.3%的准确率，证明了自然语言引导大规模任务分配的可行性与高效性。

Abstract: As multi-drone fleets scale, zone assignment rapidly evolves into an intractable NP-hard combinatorial problem that overwhelms classical exhaustive search. While quantum optimization promises to shatter these classical bottlenecks, mapping complex spatial tasks from human intent to restricted quantum hardware remains a severe challenge. To bridge this gap, we present an end-to-end framework integrating a fine-tuned Large Language Model (LLM) front-end with a highly scalable, domain-specific quantum-classical backend. The front-end utilizes Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to translate free-form natural language instructions into structurally robust Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) constraints without false negatives. To overcome the strict qubit limits of near-term quantum devices, our framework features a novel constraint-preserving graph partitioner and a compressed separator-based dynamic programming (DP) merge. By structurally encoding constraints via W-state initialization and XY-mixers in Conditional Value-at-Risk Quantum Approximate Optimization (CVaR-QAOA), the pipeline stays highly compact. Empirical results demonstrate that this architecture circumvents classical scaling walls, recovering the global optimum on 100% of idealized oracle cases and 96.3% under real QAOA sampling, enabling natural-language-guided task allocation at previously intractable scales.

</details>


### [262] [ReactVLA: Fast and Lightweight Reactive Robot Manipulation via Improved Mean Flow Action Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14255)
*Yanzhao Guo,Wenkai Chen,Jianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReactVLA 是一个轻量级、低延迟的 VLA 框架，通过 iMF 生成器和 AttnRes 机制解决了扩散策略推理速度慢的问题，实现了高精度的实时机器人操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散模型的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略虽然表达能力强，但因其迭代采样过程导致推理延迟高，无法满足机器人实时闭环控制的需求。

Method: 1. 改进的平均流（iMF）动作生成器，通过简化扩散采样过程，将多步生成减少为单步或少步；2. 注意力残差（AttnRes）机制，通过动态深度特征路由，替代统一的残差累积，以更好保留任务相关多模态表示。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboIMI等大规模模拟基准及真实世界任务中，ReactVLA表现优于SmolVLA和π0；任务性能提升最高可达1.65倍，推理速度提升4倍以上，真实世界推理延迟控制在38.6ms以内。

Conclusion: ReactVLA 通过改进的动作生成机制和特征路由机制，在大幅提升推理速度（4倍以上）的同时，任务性能也优于同量级的基准模型，实现了实时闭环机器人控制。

Abstract: Diffusion-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies have demonstrated strong capability in modeling expressive and multimodal action distributions. However, their reliance on iterative sampling introduces substantial inference latency, which limits their applicability to reactive closed-loop robot manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose \texttt{ReactVLA}, a lightweight and low-latency VLA framework for real-time robotic manipulation. \texttt{ReactVLA} combines two complementary designs: (1) an improved Mean Flow (iMF) action generator that reduces expensive multi-step diffusion sampling to one-to-few-step action generation, and (2) Attention Residuals (AttnRes), a dynamic depth-wise feature routing mechanism that replaces uniform residual accumulation to better preserve task-relevant multimodal representations. We evaluate \texttt{ReactVLA} on large-scale simulation benchmarks, including LIBERO and RoboIMI, as well as real-world robotic manipulation tasks. Experimental results show that \texttt{ReactVLA} consistently outperforms similarly sized VLA baselines, including SmolVLA and $π_0$. On challenging precision manipulation tasks, \texttt{ReactVLA} achieves up to a 1.65$\times$ improvement in task performance while providing more than a 4$\times$ increase in inference speed compared with leading VLA models. Finally, it reduces real-world policy latency to below 38.6 ms, enabling fast reactive control on physical robot platforms. Please check out our project website at: https://game-loader.github.io/ReactVLA/.

</details>


### [263] [FloVerse: Floor Plan-Guided Multi-Modal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14267)
*Weiqi Huang,Shuangyi Dong,Jiaxin Li,Yifei Guo,Zan Wang,Wei Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了名为 FloVerse 的具身导航统一框架与大规模数据集 FloVerse-1.6K，并设计了名为 ThreeDiff 的两阶段导航策略，通过嵌入平面图先验大幅提升了跨模态导航的任务表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究多集中于单一 PointNav 任务且环境规模有限，缺乏一个能够统一集成多种导航任务（PointNav, ObjectNav, ImageNav）并利用平面图先验的综合性框架。

Method: 1. 提出 FloVerse 统一任务框架；2. 构建包含 1.6K 场景、24K 专家轨迹和 12M RGBD 帧的 FloVerse-1.6K 数据集；3. 提出 ThreeDiff 两阶段模仿学习策略：包含基于扩散模型的模态掩码目标推理规划器，以及基于深度的轨迹精炼器。

Result: 实验表明，引入平面图先验显著提升了所有导航模态的性能；ThreeDiff 模型成功实现了对平面图空间信息的隐式捕捉，在具身导航任务中表现优异。

Conclusion: 利用平面图先验信息能有效提升不同目标模态（PointNav, ObjectNav, ImageNav）下的导航性能，验证了 ThreeDiff 模型在处理具身导航任务中的有效性与空间信息捕捉能力。

Abstract: Floor plans encapsulate compact spatial priors, enabling agents to navigate unseen scenes more efficiently. While prior work has explored floor plan-guided navigation, it has focused mainly on PointNav and a limited set of environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce FloVerse, a new task for floor plan-guided embodied navigation that unifies PointNav, ObjectNav, and ImageNav. To support FloVerse, we assemble FloVerse-1.6K, a large-scale dataset of 1.6K scenes from HM3D and Gibson 4+, paired with corresponding floor plans, comprising 240K expert trajectories and 12M RGBD frames. We further propose ThreeDiff, a two-stage imitation learning policy comprising a planner, a diffusion-based multimodal goal-reasoning module trained via masked-modality modeling, and a refiner, a depth-based trajectory-refinement module for safe execution. Extensive experiments demonstrate that (1) floor-plan priors improve navigation performance across all goal modalities, and (2) ThreeDiff implicitly captures spatial information from floor plans. These results underscore the effectiveness of spatial priors and validate our proposed unified approach for floor plan-guided embodied navigation.

</details>


### [264] [Robust Fall Recovery for Armless Bipedal-Wheeled Robots Via Force-Guided Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14270)
*Haidong Hou,Zhangguo Yu,Tao Han,Hengbo Qi,Khaleel Ghazal,Yu Zhang,Yidong Du,Xuechao Chen,Fei Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对无臂双足轮式机器人的倒地恢复难题，本文提出了 FTSR 框架，通过引入可优化的外力约束和阶段性训练策略，引导机器人自主学会无需外力辅助的恢复性动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无臂双足轮式机器人缺乏辅助肢体（如手臂或多腿）来产生支撑力，导致在该类机器人上实现自主倒地恢复具有极高的技术挑战。

Method: 提出 FTSR 框架：1. 力引导法（Force-guided method）：在模拟中添加与高度相关、可优化的辅助力约束，通过约束强化学习引导策略逐渐脱离外部辅助；2. 阶段性奖励（Stage-wise rewards）：设计高度进度奖励以结构化恢复过程；3. 师生架构（Teacher-student architecture）：通过特权信息蒸馏实现动力学感知策略部署。

Result: 在物理机器人上实现了复杂工况下的鲁棒倒地恢复，并成功向高自由度人形机器人泛化，恢复后可保持稳定的运动能力。

Conclusion: 该框架通过力引导与阶段性奖励成功解决了无臂双足轮式机器人从任意姿态恢复站立并过渡到行走的问题，在物理实验中证明了其鲁棒性与泛化能力。

Abstract: Fall recovery is critical for autonomous legged locomotion. Existing methods have demonstrated that some legged robots, such as humanoids and quadrupeds, are capable of fall recovery from diverse postures by utilizing arms or coordinating multi-legs to generate support forces. Without arms or other legs to provide supportive assistance, a bipedal-wheeled robot must rely solely on the actuation of its legs, making recovery particularly difficult. To address this, we introduce FTSR (Force-guided Teacher-student framework with Stage-wise Rewards). The force-guided method constructs an external auxiliary force during simulation training that correlates directly with the robot's real-time height, explicitly formulating this force as an optimizable constraint. Through constrained reinforcement learning, the policy is guided toward reducing force dependency gradually and increasing the body height, developing internal recovery strategies despite having no arms for support. Height-progressive stage-Wise rewards progressively structure posture stabilization during recovery and transition to sustained locomotion, integrated with teacher-student architecture distilling privileged knowledge of force effects and recovery dynamics. After simulation training, the policy is deployed on a physical armless bipedal-wheeled robot and extensively evaluated. Experiments confirm robust and reliable fall recovery under diverse challenging conditions, demonstrating strong environmental adaptability and motion robustness, while maintaining full post-recovery motion capability. The framework also generalizes effectively to a high-DOF humanoid, confirming its practical generalizability. The project page is available at https://2350575870.github.io/force-guided.github.io/

</details>


### [265] [Elastic Queries Reinforcement Learning: Self-Aware Policy Execution for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14375)
*Ge Wang,Xinyu Tan,Xiang Li,Man Luo,Chengsi Yao,Shenhao Yan,Jiahao Yang,Fan Feng,Honghao Cai,Xiangyuan Wang,Zhixin Mai,Yiming Zhao,Yatong Han,Zhen Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EQRL 是一种新型 VLA 推理架构，通过学习动态调整推理预算和动作执行策略，使机器人能根据任务难度自适应地分配计算资源，从而在降低计算成本的同时保证了操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 VLA 模型通常采用固定的推理和重规划策略，难以应对机器人控制中因任务难度不均而产生的不同计算需求。即在处理接触密集或不确定性高的复杂状态时计算不足，而在简单状态下又存在计算浪费。

Method: 提出了 Elastic Queries Reinforcement Learning (EQRL) 框架。引入轻量级潜变量调度适配器，无需微调底层 VLA 模型，即可联合决策潜变量输入、去噪预算和动作块长度。利用评论家集成（Critic Ensemble）差异衡量状态难度，通过强化学习动态优化查询调度。

Result: 在仿真环境和真实机器人操作任务中，EQRL 在减少推理成本的同时，保持或提升了任务成功率，实现了计算预算的有效分配。

Conclusion: EQRL 框架通过引入轻量级自适应调度器，实现了 VLA 模型在推理预算与任务执行性能之间的动态平衡，为机器人控制提供了一种高效且具备计算感知能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are powerful action generators for robot manipulation, but they are typically executed with fixed inference and replanning schedules. This rigidity ignores the uneven difficulty of robot control: contact-rich or uncertain states may need more computation and fresher feedback, while easier states can often be handled with fewer inference steps and longer open-loop execution. We propose Elastic Queries Reinforcement Learning (EQRL), a framework that makes each VLA policy query elastic. A lightweight latent-schedule adaptor jointly selects the latent input, denoising budget, and action chunk length, without fine-tuning the underlying VLA model. To make scheduling difficulty-aware, EQRL trains a critic over the joint latent-schedule action and derives a state difficulty signal from critic ensemble disagreement. This signal guides compute toward difficult states, while a learned residual allows task-driven correction. We formulate variable chunk execution as query-level macro-action RL with chunk-dependent discounting and an amortized number-of-function-evaluations (NFE) budget. Across simulation and real-robot manipulation, EQRL reduces amortized inference cost while preserving or improving task success.

</details>


### [266] [Hy-Embodied-0.5-VLA: From Vision-Language-Action Models to a Real-World Robot Learning Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14409)
*He Zhang,Lingzhu Xiang,Haitao Lin,Zeyu Huang,Minghui Wang,Dingyan Zhong,Yubo Dong,Yihao Wu,Yongming Rao,Dongsheng Zhang,Wanjia He,Ling Chen,Kai Huang,Jiahao Chen,Sichang Su,Xumin Yu,Ziyi Wang,Chengwei Zhu,Xiao Teng,Yuchun Guo,Yufeng Zhang,Yuandong Liu,Rui Wang,Zisheng Lu,Han Hu,Zhengyou Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了 HyVLA-0.5，这是一个端到端的机器人学习系统，涵盖了从数据收集、模型训练（预训练/微调/RL后训练）到现实世界部署的完整技术全栈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建一个能够覆盖机器人学习完整技术栈的系统，以解决从模型开发到现实世界部署的一站式集成问题。

Method: 采用端到端（End-to-End）的学习架构，涵盖了数据收集、模型设计、持续预训练、监督微调（SFT）以及强化学习（RL）后训练等多个阶段。

Result: 成功构建并部署了 HyVLA-0.5 系统，验证了其在机器人任务执行中的实用价值，并提供了一套标准化的全栈开发流程。

Conclusion: HyVLA-0.5 是一套完整且高效的端到端机器人学习系统，证明了从数据收集到强化学习后训练的全栈流程在实际机器人部署中的有效性。

Abstract: In this report, we present Hy-Embodied-0.5-VLA, abbreviated as HyVLA-0.5, an end-to-end system that spans the full robot learning stack: data collection, model design, continued pre-training and supervised fine-tuning, RL post-training, and real-world deployment. Each component serves a distinct role in this stack.

</details>


### [267] [ForestBack: Breadcrumb-Based Pedestrian Dead Reckoning for Infrastructure-Free Return Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14421)
*Aueaphum Aueawatthanaphisut,Chanakan Chaipan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForestBack 是一款无需任何先验基础设施的行人返回导航系统，通过改进 PDR 算法和引入面包屑路径重构，有效提升了在 GPS 盲区的路径回溯精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在无法使用 GPS 和其他预装定位基础设施的环境（如森林等 GPS 盲区）下，行人返回导航困难的问题。

Method: 提出了一种基于面包屑（breadcrumb-based）的行人死区推算（PDR）框架，集成了基于加速度的步长检测、自适应步长估计、磁力计辅助航向估计、气压高度校正以及双向面包屑路径重构算法。

Result: 相比传统 PDR，ForestBack 的平均均方根误差（RMSE）从 1.129 米降低至 0.965 米（提升 15.76%），最终位置误差从 1.781 米降至 1.388 米，转弯事件检测一致性达到 99.90%。

Conclusion: ForestBack 是一种有效的无基础设施行人返回导航方案，通过改进传统的死区推算（PDR）算法，显著提升了轨迹重建精度和路径回溯导航的可靠性，特别是在存在障碍物的环境下具有显著优势。

Abstract: Reliable return navigation remains an important challenge in GPS-denied environments where external positioning infrastructure may be unavailable or unreliable. This paper presents ForestBack, an infrastructure-free pedestrian return navigation framework based on breadcrumb-based pedestrian dead reckoning (PDR). The system records a user's walking route as a sequence of reversible breadcrumb nodes and generates reverse-path guidance without requiring GPS, Wi-Fi, Bluetooth beacons, or pre-installed infrastructure. ForestBack integrates acceleration-based step detection, adaptive step-length estimation, magnetometer-assisted heading estimation, barometric-altitude correction, and bidirectional breadcrumb path reconstruction. The system was evaluated using an indoor obstacle-avoidance route with five checkpoints, where the user navigated around a central obstacle. A dataset of 36 walking trials and 42,474 time-series samples was used for evaluation, including IMU signals, magnetometer readings, barometric variables, turn-event labels, ground-truth trajectories, baseline PDR outputs, proposed ForestBack outputs, and power-related measurements. Experimental results show that ForestBack reduced the mean RMSE from 1.129 m to 0.965 m compared with traditional PDR, corresponding to a 15.76% improvement. The mean final-position error was reduced from 1.781 m to 1.388 m, while turn-event detection consistency reached approximately 99.90%. These results indicate that ForestBack improves trajectory reconstruction and route-preserving return guidance in obstacle-avoidance scenarios. The released dataset and analysis notebook support reproducibility and future benchmarking of infrastructure-free PDR-based return navigation systems.

</details>


### [268] [Kine2Go: Kinematic dataset for the Unitree Go2 robot with diverse gaits and motions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14433)
*Władysław Pałucki,Paweł Siwak,Krzysztof Ciebiera,Marek Cygan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对 Unitree Go2 四足机器人数据获取难的问题，本文提出了 Kine2Go 数据集，通过转换不同 morphologies 的运动数据并结合强化学习，生成了 800 条包含鲁棒运动轨迹与电机控制指令的数据，降低了机器人学习与控制研究的门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前基于 Unitree Go2 的机器人研究（如模仿学习、强化学习）严重依赖高质量的运动学与电机动作数据，但获取此类数据成本高昂、流程复杂且耗时长。

Method: 作者构建了一套数据处理流水线：首先将不同四足机器人的运动学数据转换为 Go2 兼容格式；其次利用强化学习算法训练专门执行轨迹的策略；最后从这些策略中采集包含运动学轨迹与电机级动作的鲁棒数据。

Result: 发布了 Kine2Go 数据集，包含由 40 种不同策略生成的 800 条多样化步态运动学轨迹，且每条轨迹都配有相应的电机级行动数据。

Conclusion: Kine2Go 为 Unitree Go2 四足机器人提供了一个高质量、多样化的运动轨迹数据集，简化了相关领域的开发难度，为模仿学习和强化学习研究提供了必要基准，促进了机器人控制技术的进步。

Abstract: The recent popularity of robotics, combined with the steadily decreasing cost of robotic hardware, has lowered the entry barrier to robotics research and enabled rapid advancements in the field. One of the primary examples is the Unitree Go2 quadruped robot, which is often used by researchers in the areas of locomotion, navigation, control, and others. Many researchers use the Go2 robot in combination with techniques like imitation learning, reinforcement learning, and behavioral cloning to allow machine learning systems to take full control of the robot. At the same time, many of those techniques require demonstration data consisting of the robot's kinematics information and actions applied to the motors. Obtaining such data is difficult, requires building complex pipelines, and can take significant time. To aid in those kinds of efforts, we present Kine2Go - a dataset with 800 diverse gait kinematics trajectory motion data for the Unitree Go2 robot, derived from 40 distinct policies. Our pipeline accepts data from various quadruped morphologies and translates them to a Go2-compatible format. Then we use Reinforcement Learning to train policies following a given motion, and finally we gather data from those policies, which grants robust, perturbed kinematic data with corresponding motor-level actions.

</details>


### [269] [CADET: Physics-Grounded Causal Auditing and Training-Free Deconfounding of End-to-End Driving Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14438)
*Zikun Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对端到端自动驾驶规划器学习虚假相关性（因果混淆）的问题，CADET 提出了一种无需模型重训练的“审计-修复”框架，提升了黑盒模型在复杂场景下的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模仿学习的端到端自动驾驶规划器容易产生“因果混淆”（如错误地将路边建筑物等虚假特征与驾驶决策挂钩），导致在长尾场景中可靠性下降；而现有的评估指标无法识别此类捷径，现有的修复方法又过于依赖模型重训练。

Method: CADET（Causal Auditing, DE-biasing, and Testing）：一个无需权重更新的框架。利用因果干预技术审计预训练模型对场景元素的依赖性，并设计了相应的修复机制，无需对大模型进行昂贵的重新训练。

Result: CADET 能够在不修改模型参数的前提下，准确识别并消除规划器对虚假相关性的依赖，提升模型在复杂交通场景中的决策质量。

Conclusion: CADET 提供了一种无需重训练的高效方法，通过审计和修复预训练自动驾驶模型的因果依赖问题，显著提升了模型在长尾场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: End-to-end (E2E) autonomous-driving planners trained by imitation are prone to statistical shortcuts: they associate scene elements that merely co-occur with expert actions (a roadside object, a building facade) with driving decisions, rather than the variables that causally determine them. Such causal confusion silently compromises reliability in long-tail scenarios, and it is difficult to detect, because prevailing open-loop metrics (L2 displacement and collision rate) are dominated by ego status and do not indicate whether a planner depends on spurious cues. Existing remedies based on causal-intervention training require retraining large models and cannot audit a planner that is already deployed. We present CADET, a training-free framework that audits, benchmarks, and repairs spurious reliance in pretrained E2E planners without any parameter update.

</details>


### [270] [AERMANI-PLACE: Language Guided Object Placement with Aerial Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14531)
*Sarthak Mishra,Ritama Sanyal,Rishabh Dev Yadav,Wei Pan,Spandan Roy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AERMANI-PLACE 框架实现了利用自然语言指令指挥无人机进行物体放置，通过视觉辅助锚点定位与深度感知，省去了繁琐的坐标设置，有效提升了人机协作效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机操作需精确的度量坐标输入，对用户不友好且操作复杂。为了实现更直观的人机交互，作者希望利用人类常用的“语言+指引”方式来实现自动化目标放置。

Method: 提出了一种两阶段方法：1. 利用图像编辑模型处理场景图片与文字指令，生成视觉标记（Marker）以指示放置点；2. 结合深度信息进行空间基准定位（Grounding），随后生成并执行轨迹规划。

Result: 在测试集中，该方法的指令解析与定位成功率为 87%，在现实世界的无人机平台上实现了 72% 的任务执行成功率。

Conclusion: AERMANI-PLACE 成功实现了通过自然语言指令引导无人机进行物体放置，证明了将多模态语义理解与物理操作相结合的有效性，为提升无人机与人类操作的交互友好性提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Object placement is a fundamental component of aerial manipulation tasks, yet existing systems typically require the desired placement position to be specified explicitly in metric coordinates. Such interfaces are not intuitive and require users to reason about coordinate frames and scene geometry, making them difficult to use in practical deployments. In contrast, humans often communicate spatial goals through a combination of language and pointing gestures. Inspired by this observation, we present AERMANI-PLACE, a framework for language-guided object placement with aerial manipulators. Given a scene image and a natural language instruction, an image editing model generates a modified version of the scene containing a visual marker that indicates where the object should be placed. This marker is then grounded into the physical environment using depth observations to recover a metric place point, after which a placement trajectory is generated and executed by the aerial manipulator. We evaluate the proposed approach on a test set of 100 language-guided placement tasks and demonstrate successful execution on a real aerial manipulation platform. Experimental results show that the proposed method reliably infers placement locations from language instructions with an average success rate of 87\% on the test-set and transfers effectively to real-world aerial manipulation with an average success rate of 72\%.
  Video: https://youtu.be/SgwwgLBsv0g

</details>


### [271] [Spatially Conditioned Diffusion Policy: Learning Precise and Robust Manipulation with a Single RGB Camera](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14535)
*Seoyoon Kim,Kanghyun Kim,Dongwoo Ko,Yeong Jin Heo,Min Jun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对单视角视觉模仿学习中细节捕捉困难的问题，本文提出了SCDP，通过利用端点轨迹作为注意力锚点引导扩散策略，在减少硬件配置前提下实现了高性能的机器人操纵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉模仿学习依赖多视角（含手腕相机），但在单全局视角下，模型难以捕捉精细交互细节及识别任务相关区域，亟需一种能在单视角下实现鲁棒精细操作的方法。

Method: 提出SCDP（空间条件扩散策略），包含两部分：(1) 多尺度视觉编码器，用于同时捕捉全局上下文与精细视觉特征；(2) 空间条件模块，在扩散循环中沿着中间端点轨迹采样点级特征以引导策略学习。

Result: 在仿真实验中，SCDP显著优于现有的强单视角基准，且性能可与多视角系统媲美；在真实世界机器人任务中展现出高精度操作能力及对视觉干扰的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SCDP通过将末端执行器轨迹作为视觉注意力锚点，成功实现了在单视角摄像头配置下，达到媲美多视角系统的机器人精细操作能力，证明了单视角模仿学习的高效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Recent visual imitation learning systems have widely adopted multi-camera setups with wrist-mounted cameras as the de facto standard. However, manipulation from a single global view remains challenging, as the policy should capture fine-grained interaction details and identify task-relevant regions without local wrist views. To address this challenge, we present Spatially Conditioned Diffusion Policy (SCDP), a diffusion-based visuomotor policy that achieves precise and robust manipulation in a single-camera setting. Our key idea is that end-effector trajectories can serve as visual attention anchors that reflect task-relevant regions. Building on this idea, SCDP consists of two key components: (i) a visual encoder that produces multi-scale feature maps to capture both broader context and fine-grained visual features, and (ii) a spatial conditioning module that samples point-wise features along intermediate end-effector trajectories in the diffusion loop. Extensive simulation experiments show that SCDP consistently outperforms strong single-view baselines and achieves performance comparable to multi-camera baselines. Real-world experiments further demonstrate precise manipulation and robustness to visual distractors, highlighting the potential of single-camera imitation learning.

</details>


### [272] [TRACE: Trajectory-Routed Causal Memory for Delayed-Evidence Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14551)
*Zihao Li,Ranpeng Qiu,Yincong Chen,Guoqiang Ren,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRACE 是一种新型机器人视觉模仿学习记忆框架，利用路径签名将历史关键证据（如被隐藏的目标信息）编码存取，从而解决了长时序任务中因关键视觉线索消失导致的决策歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自主操作中常面临“延迟证据”任务（delayed-evidence tasks），即决策所需的关键视觉线索在决策点前已消失，导致当前显式视觉观测不足以支撑正确决策，需要具备长时序记忆以保持上下文一致性。

Method: 提出了 TRACE（TRAjectory-routed Causal Evidence）框架：1. 使用路径签名（path signatures）作为关键索引，而非时间或标签；2. 采用固定大小的潜空间记忆模块存储与任务相关的视觉和本体感知证据；3. 利用轻量级适配器（adapters）集成到现有策略中，无需修改主干网络。

Result: 在长时序、存在视觉歧义点的真实世界操作任务中，TRACE 在分支选择准确率和任务成功率方面均显著超过了对比基准（包括基于短时历史和循环神经网络的记忆方法）。

Conclusion: TRACE 是一种高效的视觉运动模仿策略记忆框架，通过路径签名（path signatures）实现对延迟证据的有效存取，在解决视觉歧义的长时序任务中表现优于现有的短时和循环记忆基准方法，且具备良好的模型兼容性。

Abstract: Robots under autonomous operation may require decisions based on evidence that is no longer visible. We study \emph{delayed-evidence} tasks, where an early cue disappears before a later decision point, so visually similar observations can require different actions. In these settings, the current observation is not a sufficient state for control. We introduce TRAjectory-routed Causal Evidence (TRACE), a memory framework for visuomotor imitation policies. TRACE stores task-relevant visual and robot-state evidence, such as object identity, target choice, or route-dependent state, in a fixed-size latent memory that remains bounded over long episodes. Instead of indexing memory by raw time or manually provided task labels, TRACE uses \emph{path signatures}: compact, order-sensitive features of the executed robot-state trajectory. These signatures do not store the visual cue itself; rather, they provide trajectory-conditioned keys for writing and retrieving the evidence stored when the cue was visible. When the robot later reaches an ambiguous observation, the policy conditions on TRACE memory to recover the missing context and choose the correct branch. TRACE attaches through lightweight adapters to policies, without changing the policy backbone, action head, or imitation objective. Across real-world long-horizon manipulation tasks with visually ambiguous branch points, TRACE improves branch selection and task success over alternative baselines, including short-history and recurrent memory. Project page: https://jeong-zju.github.io/trace

</details>


### [273] [ORCA: A Platform for Open-Source Dexterity Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14561)
*Francesco Capuano,Maximilian Eberlein,Fabrice Bourquin,Clemens Claudio Christoph*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Orca是一个开源灵巧操作学习栈，通过统一软硬件接口并集成主流学习框架，解决了拟人灵巧手研究中开发环境割裂的问题，推动了灵巧操控的研究与应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双指夹具在灵巧任务中具有局限性，而拟人手虽然具有优势，但现有的软硬件生态零散、缺乏统一标准，导致其在机器人学习研究中的应用难度较高。

Method: 提出了Orca学习架构，集成了低级控制、仿真、消费级设备遥操作及动作重定向，并与主流机器人学习框架（如LeRobot）深度兼容。通过VR头显进行专家演示收集，并利用LeRobot端到端训练自主策略。

Result: 验证了Orca在手内重定向任务中的完整工作流，实现了从遥操作演示收集到自主策略训练及评估的闭环，且具备极高的可重复性和可观测性。

Conclusion: Orca作为一个开源的Dexterous操作研究栈，通过统一的控制、仿真、遥操作和重定向接口，降低了灵巧手研究的门槛，有效促进了灵巧操控在机器人学习生态系统中的集成应用。

Abstract: Robotics manipulation research increasingly focuses on two-finger parallel grippers for their effectiveness, affordability, and ease of teleoperation. Grippers are nonetheless limited by their form factor, often requiring bimanual setups even for simple reorientation tasks. Anthropomorphic hands are a more natural platform for dexterous robot learning -- closer to the human hand, and capable of learning from human video -- yet they remain hard to use in learning research: even where open and accessible hand hardware exists, the software for control, simulation, teleoperation, and retargeting is scattered in one-off code bases, and largely disconnected from the robot-learning ecosystem. In this work, we introduce the \orca~learning stack, an open-source research stack for dexterity as a first-class robot learning domain. Our \orca~stack unifies low-level control, simulation, teleoperation from a range of consumer platforms, and hand retargeting, behind a single interface, and integrates natively with popular robot-learning frameworks such as \lerobot, so dexterous hand researchers can leverage the same data, training, and evaluation pipelines used for non-dexterous robot learning. We demonstrate a complete end-to-end workflow, collecting expert demonstrations of an in-hand reorientation task by teleoperation with a consumer-grade VR headset, training an autonomous policy with \lerobot, and evaluating the learned policy in a fully reproducible and observable setup. We open-source the entire stack as a shared, reproducible foundation for dexterous-manipulation research.

</details>


### [274] [Sensitivity Shaping for Latent Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14585)
*Hongzhan Yu,Chenghao Li,Ruipeng Zhang,Henrik Christensen,Sicun Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种正则化方法，通过增强学习动态模型的控制敏感度，解决了模型对外推过程缺乏敏感性导致的OOD检测失效问题，提升了机器人的执行安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模型（model-based）的规划方法在检测策略诱导的分布外（OOD）状态时存在缺陷：当模型对特定控制行为不敏感时，即使是不受支持的控制动作也可能产生看似正常的预测，导致OOD检测失效。

Method: 引入支持条件控制敏感度正则化（support-conditioned control-sensitivity regularization），在训练过程中强制要求动态模型在高支持度区域对控制输入的变化表现出敏感的响应，从而区分真实的控制影响与模型的外推偏离。

Result: 在视觉避障、机械臂操作和真实机器人导航实验中，该方法表现出更优的OOD检测性能，显著增强了闭环规划的安全性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的支持条件控制敏感度正则化方法（support-conditioned control-sensitivity regularization）能有效提升动态模型对OOD行为的检测能力，从而实现更安全的机器人闭环规划。

Abstract: Generative dynamics models enable planning in challenging robotic systems, but safe deployment requires reliably detecting policy-induced out-of-distribution (OOD) transitions. Existing methods typically treat the learned dynamics as fixed and attach post hoc support surrogates. We show that these surrogates can fail when the dynamics are locally insensitive to critical action choices: unsupported control actions may produce latent predictions that resemble demonstrated transitions, suppressing OOD signals despite large true predictive errors. To address this, we introduce support-conditioned control-sensitivity regularization, which promotes sensitive local response to control input changes in learned dynamics in high-support training regions. This preserves control-induced variation while limiting unstable extrapolation due to weak empirical support. Experiments in vision-based obstacle avoidance, manipulation, and real-robot navigation show improved OOD detection and safer closed-loop planning.

</details>


### [275] [What Robots Do Matters More Than What They Look Like: Task Context Shapes Trust in Educational HRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14602)
*Anna-Maria Velentza,Konstantina Nikou,Anne-Gwenn Bosser,Nikolaos Fachantidis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究发现，在教育场景中，人们对机器人的信任主要取决于任务属性而非外观，强调了“任务感知”部署在机器人设计中的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型推动社交辅助机器人（SAR）的普及，目前尚不清楚机器人的外观对不同交互任务是否同样适用，以及信任度是更多取决于机器人的外观还是具体的应用语境。

Method: 采用被试内视频实验法（N=81），参与者评估三种不同外观的机器人执行三种教育相关任务（教授知识、程序指导、个人信息讨论）的表现，并通过重复测量方差分析进行数据处理。

Result: 任务类型对信任度有显著的主效应（指导性任务信任度最高，个人信息讨论最低）；机器人的外观对信任度没有显著影响；外观与任务类型的交互作用不显著。

Conclusion: 在人机交互（HRI）中，用户对机器人的信任度主要受任务语境的影响，而非机器人的外观形态。未来的机器人部署应优先考虑任务目标、角色定位及交互行为的契合度，而非仅依赖拟人化设计。

Abstract: Socially assistive robots (SARs) are increasingly deployed in educational and information-sharing contexts, supported by advances in large language models that enable fluent real-time interaction. Despite the growing diversity of robot embodiments, it remains unclear whether a single robot appearance is appropriate across different interaction tasks or whether trust depends primarily on contextual factors. In this study, we examine how robot appearance and task type jointly influence trust in robots. Using a within-subjects video-based experiment (N = 81), participants evaluated three robots with distinct appearances while performing three educationally relevant tasks: teaching, procedural instruction, and personal-information discussion. Results from repeated-measures analyses show a strong main effect of task on trust, with participants reporting the highest trust during instructional guidance, moderate trust during teaching activities, and significantly lower trust when robots requested personal information. In contrast, robot appearance showed no significant main effect, and the interaction between appearance and task was marginal. These findings suggest that trust in human-robot interaction is shaped more strongly by task context than by physical embodiment alone. By focusing on future educators as end users, this work contributes empirical evidence toward task-aware robot deployment in educational environments and highlights the importance of aligning robot roles and behaviors with interaction goals rather than relying solely on anthropomorphic design.

</details>


### [276] [Impedance MPC with Disturbance Estimation for Dexterous Hand Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14606)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的阻抗MPC框架，通过将复杂执行器动力学简化并实现离线预计算，在保持高实时性（500Hz）的同时，显著提升了灵巧手在复杂接触任务中的精度与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧手在执行任务时，精确轨迹跟踪与安全柔顺接触往往存在冲突。传统的固定增益控制器难以同时满足这两者的需求，需要一种新的控制框架来兼顾性能与安全性。

Method: 开发了一种与执行器无关的阻抗MPC框架，利用代数前馈将不同类型的执行器简化为常系数双积分器，允许离线预计算QP逆矩阵，实现了500Hz的零偏移MPC控制，并结合增强卡尔曼滤波器消除稳态误差。

Result: 在液压手指实验中，相较于传统阻抗控制，该方法在RMS误差、稳态误差和峰值偏转方面分别提升了183倍、1500倍和23倍。在高维度（16-DOF）仿真中，表现出优异的鲁棒性和抗干扰能力。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用的阻抗模型预测控制（Impedance MPC）框架，能够在各种执行器类型下实现高精度的轨迹跟踪与接触力安全控制，且具有极高的实时性与稳定性。

Abstract: Dexterous hands must simultaneously track precise finger trajectories and maintain safe, compliant contact -- objectives in tension for any fixed-gain controller. We present an actuator-agnostic Impedance Model Predictive Control (Impedance MPC) framework for dexterous fingers, instantiating the constant-$A_d$ offset-free architecture established for physical human-robot interaction (pHRI); its stability, recursive-feasibility, and input-to-state-stability guarantees are inherited by preserving the architectural assumptions. An algebraic feedforward reduces the tendon transmission -- hydraulic, cable, pneumatic, twisted-string, or series-elastic -- to a constant-coefficient double integrator, so the QP cost inverse is precomputed offline and a 10-step receding-horizon quadratic program runs at 500\,Hz while enforcing hard constraints on contact force (ISO/TS 15066), actuation limits, and jerk. An encoder-only augmented-Kalman disturbance state drives steady-state error to zero under any constant contact load. On a hydraulically actuated finger -- the worked example platform, adding pressure and cavitation constraints -- the 500\,Hz Kalman MPC attains 0.5\,mrad RMS, 0.1\,mrad steady-state, and 6.6\,mrad peak deflection under 1.5\,Nm contact: 183$\times$, 1500$\times$, and 23$\times$ better than classical impedance. The realized first-move stiffness (18$\to$323\,Nm/rad with update rate) is independently verified. The architecture scales to a 16-DOF LEAP Hand MuJoCo simulation, recovering from 2.5\,N grasp-load disturbances within 0.7\,s.

</details>


### [277] [Safe Reinforcement Learning of Autonomous Highway Driving: A Unified Framework for Safety and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14609)
*Chufei Yan,Zhihao Cui,Yiyan Lv,Taojie Chen,Ning Bian,Yulei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MoE-RM-SRL的安全强化学习框架，通过融合安全规则与混合专家模型，解决了自动驾驶训练与部署过程中的安全与效率矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习（DRL）在自动驾驶中通过试错进行学习，难以在训练中保证安全性，且难以在部署时同时兼顾安全与效率。

Method: 提出了一种统一的安全强化学习（SRL）框架：1. 部署阶段：利用SD和RM构建规则感知奖励机制以确保安全；2. 训练阶段：引入包含11个DQN的稀疏门控MoE层，并配合SD驱动的门控规则，以减少控制器切换时的不稳定性；3. 系统集成：在CARLA仿真器中实现并连接至6-DoF虚拟现实测试平台。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在随机双车道交通场景下显著提升了安全性与效率，并具备泛化至多车道行驶、匝道汇入及驶出场景的能力。

Conclusion: MoE-RM-SRL框架通过结合安全距离(SD)、奖励机(RM)和专家混合(MoE)模型，有效解决了自动驾驶在训练过程中的安全性与部署过程中的效率平衡问题，在复杂交通场景下表现优于现有基准。

Abstract: Deep reinforcement learning (DRL) offers a compelling route to decision-making for advanced autonomous vehicles (AVs), yet its trial-and-error nature makes it difficult to guarantee safety during training and to achieve both safety and efficiency at deployment. We propose a unified safe reinforcement learning (SRL) framework that integrates safe distance (SD), reward machines (RM), and mixture-of-experts (MoE), termed MoE-RM-SRL. For deployment, SD and RM jointly shape a rule-aware reward that encodes highway traffic regulations and stage-wise objectives, enabling safe and reliable behavior without sacrificing efficiency. For training, we introduce a sparsely gated MoE layer comprising up to 11 deep Q-networks (DQNs); an SD-based gating rule activates a minimal set of experts for lane-keeping and lane-changing, mitigating the instability, discontinuities, and impulsive transients commonly induced by switching between heterogeneous controllers (e.g., MPC/rule-based modules and learned policies). We implement the proposed architecture in CARLA and integrate it with a 6-DoF driver-in-the-loop virtual-reality (DiL-VR) platform. Experiments in stochastic two-lane traffic show that MoE-RM-SRL substantially improves safety and efficiency over state-of-the-art baselines, and the framework naturally extends to multi-lane driving as well as on-ramp merging and exiting scenarios.

</details>


### [278] [Whole-Body Impedance Model Predictive Control for Safe Physical Human--Robot Interaction on Floating-Base Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14617)
*Yongyan Cao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种浮动基座机器人全身控制架构，通过MPC、WBC及基于卡尔曼滤波的残差QP干扰抑制，实现了人机交互中的稳态误差消除及高性能阻抗控制，特别适用于类人机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浮动基座机器人的全身控制（WBC）存在两个主要缺陷：一是过度依赖固定增益阻抗反馈，在持续人机交互（pHRI）力作用下会累积稳态误差；二是无法有效平衡整体运动与交互安全。

Method: 提出了一种三层控制架构：1) 质心MPC进行接触力规划；2) 优先级WBC层通过零空间投影处理平衡任务；3) 辅助QP残差层利用卡尔曼滤波预测并抑制人机交互干扰。此外，采用接触一致反馈线性化将末端执行器简化为双积分器，实现离线参数预计算以支持kHz级控制，并引入协方差膨胀协议处理接触模式切换。

Result: 在17自由度双足机器人和Unitree G1人形机器人仿真中验证了该方法。系统实现了对恒定pHRI负载的零稳态误差跟踪，并在kHz采样频率下保证了高稳定性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功将阻抗MPC扩展至浮动基座机器人，通过三层架构实现了人体物理交互（pHRI）下的精确力控。通过阻抗等效定理证明了系统在无限时域内可恢复为经典的阻抗控制律，且系统参数能自适应姿态和接触配置，解决了长期存在的稳态误差问题。

Abstract: Floating-base robots must balance under rigid contact constraints while interacting safely with humans. Existing whole-body control~(WBC) frameworks allocate the full joint space to locomotion or rely on fixed-gain impedance feedback that accumulates steady-state error under sustained physical human--robot interaction~(pHRI) forces. This paper extends the authors' fixed-base two-layer Impedance MPC to floating-base platforms through a three-level architecture: a centroidal MPC plans contact forces over a 500\,ms horizon; a priority-driven WBC layer resolves balance into joint torques through contact-consistent null-space projection; and the residual null space is governed by a receding-horizon quadratic program~(QP) that predicts and rejects pHRI disturbances using a Kalman-augmented state. A contact-consistent feedback linearization reduces the arm end-effector plant to a double integrator with a \emph{constant} state matrix within each contact mode, enabling offline precomputation of the QP cost and ${\geq}1$\,kHz operation. A covariance-inflation protocol preserves the disturbance estimate across contact-mode switches, guaranteeing zero steady-state error under bounded constant pHRI loads, and an Impedance Equivalence Theorem shows the infinite-horizon limit recovers a classical task-space impedance law whose effective mass, damping, and stiffness adapt to posture and contact configuration. Simulations on a 17-DOF biped and the Unitree G1 humanoid validate the design.

</details>


### [279] [EgoGuide: Egocentric Guidance for Efficient Robot-Free Demonstration Collection and Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14665)
*Yue Xu,Mingtao Nie,Tianle Li,Hong Li,Yibo Luo,Siyuan Huang,Yong-Lu Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为解决机器人操作学习中数据效率低和场景上下文缺失的问题，EgoGuide通过引入多视角同步采集、实时质量引导及门控自中心残差策略，显著提升了数据采集效率与模型的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作学习受限于数据规模。传统的通用操作接口（UMI）存在数据冗余高、缺乏全局场景上下文的问题，导致采集效率低下。

Method: 1. 硬件接口层面：设计了结合腕部（wrist）和头戴式（egocentric）相机的同步采集系统，并加入在线视觉-几何数据质量引导；2. 策略架构层面：提出了“门控自中心残差策略”（Gated Egocentric Residual Policy），利用头戴式视角纠正腕部视角下的模糊性，同时维持腕部的稳定控制。

Result: 真实世界实验验证了EgoGuide能够有效减少完成任务所需的演示次数，提升数据采集效率，且在视觉遮挡（visual occlusion）情况下表现出更强的稳健性。

Conclusion: EgoGuide通过引入多视角同步采集和在线质量引导，成功提升了机器人操作学习的数据效率，且残差策略显著增强了模型在遮挡场景下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Robot learning from real-world demonstrations is currently constrained by data scaling. Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) provides an efficient robot-free data collection interface, yet current UMI-style pipelines often collect redundant demonstrations and lack global scene context. To improve data efficiency, we present EgoGuide, a collection interface that records synchronized wrist and head/egocentric observations and couples them with online visual-geometric data quality guidance. We also introduce a Gated Egocentric Residual Policy for robust learning from a viewpoint-varying egocentric camera, allowing head/egocentric context to correct ambiguous local observations while preserving stable wrist-view control. Real-world experiments show that EgoGuide reduces the required number of data episodes and improves data efficiency. The residual policy further improves robustness under visual occlusion. Project Page: https://silicx.github.io/EgoGuide

</details>
